# Plan 3: Spends Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Logging money: a server transactions API (idempotent create, list, edit, delete, unlink, assign extra income) with refunds, spread-over-cycle spends and extra income feeding the budget, an XP ledger that pays +5 for a logged spend (10 a day), and on the phone a two-tap quick-add with a number pad that works offline, a Today list on Home, and a spend detail screen to edit or delete.

**Architecture:** Server: a new `ingestion` package owns the one transaction pipeline (spec 2.3: source → normalise → categorise → match → save); this plan builds the manual source and the save step, and Plan 4 plugs statements into the same `TransactionService.save`. A new `gamification` package owns the XP event ledger (`XpService.award/reverse`), which Plan 6 extends with levels, streaks and badges. The budget engine keeps reading the `transactions` table through its own aggregate query, now with refunds, spread spends and the pool share of extra income. App: a new `features/spends` folder (API, categories, number-pad reducer, hooks, quick-add and spend-detail screens), an offline outbox in `lib/queue.ts` stored in `expo-sqlite` that writes first and syncs with backoff, and Home's Today section listing the day's spends with queued and failed ones merged in. Optimistic updates recompute Home's figures with the client mirror of the mood rules and roll back with a message when the server says no.

**Tech Stack:** Server: Spring Boot 4.1.1, Java 21, Spring Data JPA, `JdbcClient` for aggregates and concurrency-sensitive writes, Flyway (V006, V007), PostgreSQL 17, Testcontainers, ArchUnit. App: Expo SDK (Plan 2's version), Expo Router (modal route), TypeScript strict, TanStack Query v5, `expo-sqlite` (new), `expo-haptics`, `expo-notifications`, `expo-crypto`, Reanimated (the Gullak's coin), Jest + React Native Testing Library, Maestro for E2E-02, 03 and 08.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-23-gullak-v1-design.md` (sections 2.1, 2.3, 2.4, 2.6 XP, 2.7 the 80% message, 4.4, 4.5, 4.6 Spends, 4.8), with test cases in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-23-gullak-v1-test-cases.md`, visual rules in `.claude/design.md`, prototype findings in `docs/ux-test-findings.md` and rules in `docs/engineering-standards.md`. Read all of them before starting any task. Plans 1 and 2 are the contract this plan builds on: Plan 1 is implemented (`server/` is authoritative), Plan 2's code blocks define the app (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-plan-02-app-foundation.md`). Mockup copy quoted here comes from the design canvas (`QuickAdd.dc.html`, `SpendDetail.dc.html`, `Main.dc.html`, `TabBar.dc.html`) and is verbatim unless a decision below says otherwise.

## Global Constraints

- Migrations for this plan use V006–V009; if an earlier plan was executed with more migrations than planned, renumber to the next free number in this block. This plan uses V006 and V007.
- Money is `long` paise on the server and whole-number paise (`number`) in the app. No floating point near money. The largest single amount is `Money.MAX_AMOUNT_PAISE` = 999,999,900 paise (₹99,99,999, 7 digits); the app's `MAX_AMOUNT_PAISE` is the same number.
- Day boundaries are Asia/Kolkata (`Ist` on the server, `todayIst`/`istDateOf` in the app). Instants travel as ISO strings in UTC.
- Every query on user data filters by the user ID from the JWT subject; another user's transaction is 404, never 403. No endpoint accepts a user ID from the client.
- Error bodies are `{code, message}` (plus `fields`, `errorId`). `ErrorCode` is append-only; new codes go before `INTERNAL`. After adding codes, run `cd app && npm run gen:errors` and add each code's message to `app/src/lib/errors.ts` in the same task.
- Never log amounts, merchant text, notes, emails, UPI handles or tokens, on the server or in the app. Business events use `Events` constants with ids and counts only.
- Server: constructor injection, records at the service boundary, package-private `*Entity` and `*Repository` per feature, controllers never touch repositories, entities or `JdbcClient`; `JdbcClient` and native SQL only for aggregates and concurrency-sensitive writes; `ddl-auto: validate`; Flyway continues at `V006`.
- JSON enums are the Java constant names (`SPEND`, `UPI`, `NEED`, `PENDING`). Category codes are lowercase snake_case strings (`eating_out`) because they are reference data, not an enum.
- App: routes only under `app/app/`; tests never live there. Features never call `fetch` (only `api` from `@/lib/client`); money only through `lib/money.ts`; dates and times only through `lib/dates.ts`; `console` only in `lib/log.ts`; no `any`; tokens only in `lib/storage.ts`; design tokens only in `src/theme/`.
- Every screen renders loading, empty and error states. Every control is at least 44×44 and has an accessible name. Selected chips and radios show a 2px ink border and expose `accessibilityState` (`selected` or `checked`).
- Copy follows `.claude/design.md`: sentence case; a single sentence has no full stop; two or more sentences take a full stop after each; ₹ with Indian grouping; dates as "Fri, 23 Oct"; times as "9:12 am".
- Animations run on the UI thread and respect Reduce Motion (the Gullak component already does). Haptics are never the only feedback.
- Each task's owner is named; after the owner hands back, `qa-engineer` runs, then `red-team` where the task says so, then `manager` reviews (`docs/team.md`). The server suite (`cd server && ./gradlew test`) and the app suite (`cd app && npm test && npm run lint && npm run typecheck`) are green before every commit.
- Commit messages end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

## Review Focus

- A double-tapped Add, or an offline retry that overlaps the first request, sends the same `clientId` twice at the same moment: exactly one row and one +5 XP exist, and both requests answer with the same id. Pinned in Task 4 (`parallelPostsWithOneClientIdMakeOneRowAndOneXp`).
- Eleven spends saved at the same instant (a sync flushing a backlog) still earn exactly ten XP events. Pinned in Task 3 (`parallelAwardsNeverPassTheDailyCap`).
- Someone signs out with spends still waiting to send and a different person signs in on the same phone: the first person's spends are never sent under the second account. Pinned in Task 8 (`signOutClearsTheQueue`).
- A spend typed offline at 23:59 IST and sent after midnight counts on the day it was typed, not the day it reached the server. Pinned in Task 10 (`queuedSpendKeepsTheTimeItWasTyped`).
- A refund larger than everything spent today does not make today's spent figure negative; the extra goes back into the pool. Pinned in Task 7 (`refundBiggerThanTodaysSpendsGoesBackToThePool`).

## Decisions made in this plan

Where the spec is silent, these were decided here. Each says what it costs if it is wrong.

- **Home's Today rows come from `GET /transactions?from=<today>&to=<today>`**, not embedded in `GET /budget/today`. The list endpoint is needed anyway, the `budget` package stays free of `ingestion`, and optimistic updates touch two small caches instead of one big one. Cost: Home makes two requests, and for a moment after a sync the figures and the list can come from different snapshots.
- **Extra income reaches the budget through `transactions.assigned_pool_paise`**, summed by `SpendTotalsRepository` for income credits in the cycle up to today, and added to spendable (or to the first cycle's `pool_override_paise`). No new `cycles` column. Cost: cycle close (Plan 7) must read the same sum, which it gets by asking `BudgetService`.
- **Kinds of transaction:** the API has `kind` = `SPEND` (debit), `REFUND` (a credit that lowers spent) or `INCOME` (a credit that does nothing until assigned). The table keeps `direction` and adds `credit_kind` (`refund` or `income`). Cost: none; Plan 4 sets `kind` from the statement line.
- **Refunds:** a refund dated today lowers today's spent figure, never below zero, and any extra goes back into the pool; a refund dated earlier in the cycle lowers the pool's spent figure (BUD-12). Cost: a refund bigger than the day's spends shows as a slightly higher budget tomorrow rather than a negative spent today.
- **Spread over the cycle:** a spread spend comes out of the pool from the day it happens (including today) and never counts in today's spent figure (BUD-11). Only spends can be spread. In the app the toggle is offered on quick-add when the amount triggers "Sure?" and on spend detail. Cost: a spread spend lowers today's budget at once, which is the honest reading of "reduces the pool".
- **Extra income split** is sent as whole percentages (`savingsPercent`, `poolPercent`, adding to 100). Savings get `amount × savingsPercent ÷ 100` rounded down; the pool gets the rest. An income credit can be reassigned. Unassigned income counts nowhere. The app screen for assigning income ships with Plan 4, where credits first appear (statements); this plan builds and tests the endpoint. Cost: until Plan 4 a user cannot assign income from the app.
- **Categories:** a `categories` table keyed by `code` (reference data, so the code is the primary key instead of a UUID), seeded with the twelve spec categories. Codes, names, order and the discretionary flag (Plan 6's weekly challenge uses it):

  | code | name | sort | discretionary |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | `eating_out` | Eating out | 1 | yes |
  | `food_delivery` | Food delivery | 2 | yes |
  | `quick_commerce` | Quick commerce | 3 | yes |
  | `shopping` | Shopping | 4 | yes |
  | `travel` | Travel | 5 | yes |
  | `upi_to_people` | UPI to people | 6 | no |
  | `groceries` | Groceries | 7 | no |
  | `subscriptions` | Subscriptions | 8 | no |
  | `bills_recharges` | Bills and recharges | 9 | no |
  | `entertainment` | Entertainment | 10 | yes |
  | `health` | Health | 11 | no |
  | `other` | Other | 12 | no |

  The order puts the six mockup chips first. Cost: renaming a code later needs a data migration in every table that stores it.
- **Category chip labels** use the spec names everywhere ("Food delivery", "UPI to people", as `SpendDetail.dc.html` does), not the shorter "Delivery" and "To a person" of `QuickAdd.dc.html`, and quick-add shows all twelve in one scrolling row. Quick-add starts on Eating out and UPI, as the mockup does. Cost: the first quick-add chip row is wider than drawn.
- **A note field** (`transactions.note`, 140 characters) is added: spec 2.3 lists "optional merchant and note" but the data model has no column. Quick-add shows it behind an "Add a note" button so the drawn layout stays as it is. Cost: one column.
- **Manual dates:** a manual spend may be at most 24 hours in the future (a phone clock a little ahead) and at most 90 days old (API-SP-07, 08, 15, 16). Statement lines are not bound by the 90 days. Cost: a spend from four months ago must come from a statement.
- **Idempotent create:** the same `clientId` with the same body answers 200 with the stored spend and no second XP; with a different body, or after the spend was deleted, it answers 409 `CLIENT_ID_CONFLICT`. The app cannot replay a deleted spend: a spend is deleted only after the queue received its 2xx. Cost: none.
- **XP cap:** "10 a day" counts the user's un-reversed `SPEND_LOGGED` events for the IST day the spend was logged (the server's clock), under a per-user advisory lock. A deleted spend frees its slot, which earns nothing because the delete took the 5 back. Cost: none.
- **Create rate limit:** 200 creates per user per hour (`RATE_LIMITED`). The offline queue treats 429 as "try again later", never as a failure. Cost: someone logging more than 200 spends in an hour waits.
- **Links (for Plan 4):** a link is symmetric: the manual spend and the statement line point at each other through `linked_txn_id`, both are `confirmed`, and the statement half has `counts_in_budget = false` while linked, so the money is counted once. Linked statement halves never appear in lists. Unlinking or deleting one half returns the manual half to `pending` and the statement half to `statement_only` with `counts_in_budget = true`. This plan implements unlink and delete for that model; Plan 4 creates links. Cost: Plan 4 must create links in exactly this shape.
- **Category corrections (API-SP-27):** changing the category of a statement-sourced transaction publishes a `CategoryCorrected` Spring event; Plan 4 adds the listener that writes the user merchant rule. This plan tests that the event is published. Cost: none.
- **Offline outbox:** `lib/queue.ts` is a small generic queue (store, sender, backoff) with an `expo-sqlite` store on phones and an in-memory store on web (quick-add is not on web, spec 1.1). Items send one at a time, oldest first; a network error, a 5xx, 408 or 429 retries after 2 s, doubling to at most 5 minutes; any other 4xx marks the item failed with a reason (ERR-17). The `clientId` is the item's primary key, so a restart resends the same id and the server dedupes (APP-10). Sign-out clears the queue. Cost: a failed spend stays on Home until the user removes it.
- **Optimistic Home:** a quick-add updates Home's cached figures at once (spent, left, over, mood via `moodFor`), and the Gullak's line becomes a plain "₹263 left for today" until the server's line arrives with the next fetch. Spread spends are not applied optimistically (the pool maths needs the server). A failed delete or edit puts the row and figures back and shows "Couldn't delete Third Wave Coffee. It's back on your list." Cost: for a second or two the line is not in the chosen personality.
- **First-spend flag:** `kv.set("first-spend-logged", "1")` is written when the spend is saved on the phone (queued), not when the server acknowledges it, because that is when it appears on Home. Cost: none.
- **"Sure?" (APP-05):** asked once when the amount is above ₹10,000, or above 5 × today's budget when today's budget is above ₹0 (with a ₹0 budget every amount would ask). The Add button reads "Sure? Add ₹12,000" and a line says "That's a big one. Tap again to add it." Changing the amount asks again. Cost: none.
- **Chip state (APP-08):** React Native has no `aria-pressed`; chips expose `accessibilityState.selected` (rendered as `aria-selected` on web), as Plan 2's `Chip` already does. Task 13 changes the APP-08 wording. Cost: none.
- **The 80% message (spec 2.7):** when a quick-add takes today's spent from under 80% of the budget to 80% or more, the phone shows a local message at once, at most once a day, never between 23:00 and 07:00, and only if notifications were already allowed. It carries no amount. Messages also show while the app is open. Cost: the "4 a day" cap is Plan 8's; until then this message is not counted against it. From Plan 8 on, the server reserves one of the 4 daily slots for it whenever the `nearLimit` switch is on.
- **Gullak coin:** a tiny event store (`features/gullak/events.ts`) is bumped on every quick-add; Home's hero passes it to `<Gullak event>` so the coin drops when the modal closes (APP-14, trigger half). Haptics: one success tap when the spend is saved.
- **Task order:** the first app task (Task 8) is the data layer and outbox; the quick-add task (Task 9) is the one that enables the tab bar's + button and Home's "Add first spend", because only then is there a screen to open. Spend detail (Task 11) comes before Home's list (Task 12) so the route exists when Home links to it. The tab bar's + keeps Plan 2's label "Add a spend" (the mockup says "Add expense"); the quick-add title is the mockup's "New expense". Cost: none.
- **Rows:** saved rows open spend detail; rows still waiting to send are not tappable ("Waiting to send"); failed rows show the reason and a Remove button. Cost: a waiting spend cannot be edited until it has been sent.
- **ArchUnit:** the `commonDoesNotDependOnFeatures` deny-list is replaced by an allow-list of which `in.gullak` packages each feature may use, and a new top-level package fails the build until it is added on purpose. Cost: one line per new feature.
- **`XpService.reverse` takes the user id** (`reverse(userId, refType, refId)`) so the reversal is scoped like every other query. Cost: none.

## Server

### Task 1: Package allow-list and the deferred cycle cases

**Owner:** backend-engineer. **Test IDs:** BUD-03 (leap February), BUD-04 and BUD-05 (last working day across the year end), ERR-05/06 neighbours (the architecture rules keep biting). **Red team:** no.

Plan 1 deferred two items here: an allow-list in place of the `commonDoesNotDependOnFeatures` deny-list (it must exist before `ingestion` and `gamification` appear, or they would slip past it), and extra `CycleCalculatorTest` cases.

**Files:**
- Modify: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/rules/ArchitectureTest.java` (replace `commonDoesNotDependOnFeatures`)
- Modify: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/rules/ArchitectureRulesBiteTest.java`
- Create: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/common/archfixture/LeakyCommon.java`, `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/unlisted/Stray.java`
- Modify: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/budget/CycleCalculatorTest.java`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `CycleCalculator.containing(LocalDate, PaydayRule, Integer)`, `CycleCalculator.payday(YearMonth, PaydayRule, Integer)`, `Cycle` (Plan 1).
- Produces: `ArchitectureTest.ALLOWED_FEATURE_DEPENDENCIES` (a `Map<String, Set<String>>`) and the rule `featuresDependOnlyOnAllowedFeatures`. Every later plan that adds a top-level package or a cross-feature dependency adds one entry here. After this task: `common → {}`, `auth → {common}`, `profile → {common}`, `content → {common, profile}`, `budget → {common, profile, content}`, `gamification → {common}`, `ingestion → {common, gamification}`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the bite fixtures and the failing bite tests**

`server/src/test/java/in/gullak/common/archfixture/LeakyCommon.java` (a `common` class that reaches into a feature; test sources only, so the real rule never sees it):

```java
package in.gullak.common.archfixture;

import in.gullak.budget.Cycle;
import java.time.LocalDate;

/** Fixture for ArchitectureRulesBiteTest: common must never depend on a feature. */
public class LeakyCommon {
    public Cycle leak() {
        return new Cycle(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 1), LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 31));
    }
}
```

`server/src/test/java/in/gullak/unlisted/Stray.java` (a top-level package nobody added to the allow-list):

```java
package in.gullak.unlisted;

import in.gullak.common.money.Money;

/** Fixture for ArchitectureRulesBiteTest: a package missing from the allow-list fails. */
public class Stray {
    public long max() {
        return Money.MAX_AMOUNT_PAISE;
    }
}
```

Replace `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/rules/ArchitectureRulesBiteTest.java` with:

```java
package in.gullak.rules;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import com.tngtech.archunit.core.domain.JavaClasses;
import com.tngtech.archunit.core.importer.ClassFileImporter;
import com.tngtech.archunit.lang.ArchRule;
import com.tngtech.archunit.lang.EvaluationResult;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

/** Proves the rules that allow an empty selection still fail on real violations. */
class ArchitectureRulesBiteTest {
    static final JavaClasses FIXTURES = new ClassFileImporter().importPackages("archfixture");
    static final JavaClasses LEAKY_COMMON = new ClassFileImporter().importPackages("in.gullak.common.archfixture");
    static final JavaClasses STRAY = new ClassFileImporter().importPackages("in.gullak.unlisted");

    @Test
    void repositoryUsedFromAnotherFeatureViolates() {
        assertBites(ArchitectureTest.repositoriesStayInTheirFeature, FIXTURES);
    }

    @Test
    void controllerDependingOnRepositoryViolates() {
        assertBites(ArchitectureTest.controllersDoNotTouchRepositoriesOrEntities, FIXTURES);
    }

    @Test
    void publicEntityViolates() {
        assertBites(ArchitectureTest.entitiesStayInsideTheirFeature, FIXTURES);
    }

    @Test
    void commonReachingIntoAFeatureViolates() {
        EvaluationResult result = ArchitectureTest.featuresDependOnlyOnAllowedFeatures.evaluate(LEAKY_COMMON);
        assertThat(result.hasViolation()).isTrue();
        assertThat(result.getFailureReport().getDetails()).anyMatch(d -> d.contains("in.gullak.budget.Cycle"));
    }

    @Test
    void aPackageMissingFromTheAllowListViolates() {
        EvaluationResult result = ArchitectureTest.featuresDependOnlyOnAllowedFeatures.evaluate(STRAY);
        assertThat(result.hasViolation()).isTrue();
        assertThat(result.getFailureReport().getDetails()).anyMatch(d -> d.contains("no allow-list entry"));
    }

    private static void assertBites(ArchRule rule, JavaClasses classes) {
        assertThat(rule.evaluate(classes).hasViolation()).isTrue();
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the extra cycle cases (they should pass at once: they pin behaviour the next plans rely on)**

Add to `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/budget/CycleCalculatorTest.java`, inside the class:

```java
    @Test
    void leapFebruaryHas29Days() { // BUD-03, leap year
        Cycle c = CycleCalculator.containing(LocalDate.of(2028, 2, 10), PaydayRule.FIXED_DAY, 1);
        assertThat(c).isEqualTo(new Cycle(LocalDate.of(2028, 2, 1), LocalDate.of(2028, 2, 29)));
        assertThat(c.days()).isEqualTo(29);
    }

    @Test
    void paydayOnThe28thAcrossLeapFebruary() {
        Cycle c = CycleCalculator.containing(LocalDate.of(2028, 2, 28), PaydayRule.FIXED_DAY, 28);
        assertThat(c).isEqualTo(new Cycle(LocalDate.of(2028, 2, 28), LocalDate.of(2028, 3, 27)));
        assertThat(c.days()).isEqualTo(29);
    }

    @Test
    void lastWorkingDayInLeapFebruaryIsThe29th() { // 29 Feb 2028 is a Tuesday
        assertThat(CycleCalculator.payday(YearMonth.of(2028, 2), PaydayRule.LAST_WORKING_DAY, null))
            .isEqualTo(LocalDate.of(2028, 2, 29));
        Cycle c = CycleCalculator.containing(LocalDate.of(2028, 3, 10), PaydayRule.LAST_WORKING_DAY, null);
        assertThat(c).isEqualTo(new Cycle(LocalDate.of(2028, 2, 29), LocalDate.of(2028, 3, 30)));
        assertThat(c.days()).isEqualTo(31);
    }

    @Test
    void lastWorkingDayAcrossTheYearEnd() { // BUD-05 then January: 31 Jan 2027 is a Sunday, so payday is Fri 29 Jan
        assertThat(CycleCalculator.payday(YearMonth.of(2027, 1), PaydayRule.LAST_WORKING_DAY, null))
            .isEqualTo(LocalDate.of(2027, 1, 29));
        assertThat(CycleCalculator.containing(LocalDate.of(2026, 12, 30), PaydayRule.LAST_WORKING_DAY, null))
            .isEqualTo(new Cycle(LocalDate.of(2026, 11, 30), LocalDate.of(2026, 12, 30)));
        Cycle yearEnd = CycleCalculator.containing(LocalDate.of(2026, 12, 31), PaydayRule.LAST_WORKING_DAY, null);
        assertThat(yearEnd).isEqualTo(new Cycle(LocalDate.of(2026, 12, 31), LocalDate.of(2027, 1, 28)));
        assertThat(yearEnd.days()).isEqualTo(29);
        assertThat(CycleCalculator.containing(LocalDate.of(2027, 1, 10), PaydayRule.LAST_WORKING_DAY, null))
            .isEqualTo(yearEnd);
    }

    @Test
    void fixedPaydayAcrossTheYearEnd() {
        assertThat(CycleCalculator.containing(LocalDate.of(2027, 1, 5), PaydayRule.FIXED_DAY, 28))
            .isEqualTo(new Cycle(LocalDate.of(2026, 12, 28), LocalDate.of(2027, 1, 27)));
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Replace the deny-list with the allow-list**

In `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/rules/ArchitectureTest.java`, add the imports `java.util.Map` and `java.util.Set`, delete the `commonDoesNotDependOnFeatures` rule, and add in its place:

```java
    /**
     * The in.gullak packages each top-level feature may depend on, besides itself. An allow-list, so a new
     * package (or a new arrow between features) fails the build until it is added here on purpose.
     */
    static final Map<String, Set<String>> ALLOWED_FEATURE_DEPENDENCIES = Map.of(
        "common", Set.of(),
        "auth", Set.of("common"),
        "profile", Set.of("common"),
        "content", Set.of("common", "profile"),
        "budget", Set.of("common", "profile", "content"),
        "gamification", Set.of("common"),
        "ingestion", Set.of("common", "gamification"));

    /** "in.gullak.budget.x" → "budget"; the root package and anything outside in.gullak → null. */
    static String featureOf(JavaClass c) {
        String pkg = c.getPackageName();
        if (!pkg.startsWith("in.gullak.")) {
            return null;
        }
        String rest = pkg.substring("in.gullak.".length());
        int dot = rest.indexOf('.');
        return dot < 0 ? rest : rest.substring(0, dot);
    }

    @ArchTest
    static final ArchRule featuresDependOnlyOnAllowedFeatures = classes()
        .that().resideInAPackage("in.gullak..")
        .should(new ArchCondition<JavaClass>("depend only on the in.gullak packages their feature allows") {
            @Override
            public void check(JavaClass c, ConditionEvents events) {
                String from = featureOf(c);
                if (from == null) {
                    return;
                }
                Set<String> allowed = ALLOWED_FEATURE_DEPENDENCIES.get(from);
                if (allowed == null) {
                    events.add(SimpleConditionEvent.violated(c,
                        c.getName() + " is in package '" + from + "', which has no allow-list entry"));
                    return;
                }
                for (Dependency d : c.getDirectDependenciesFromSelf()) {
                    String to = featureOf(d.getTargetClass());
                    if (to != null && !to.equals(from) && !allowed.contains(to)) {
                        events.add(SimpleConditionEvent.violated(d, d.getDescription()));
                    }
                }
            }
        });
```

### Task 2: Categories and the transaction rules in the schema

**Owner:** backend-engineer. **Test IDs:** API-SP-09 (database half: an unknown category cannot be stored), API-SP-10 and API-SP-29 (database half), BUD-30 (still no daily-budget column). **Red team:** no.

Plan 1 deferred the allowed values for tag, source, status and payment method. This task adds them as CHECK constraints, adds the `categories` table with the twelve codes, and adds the columns refunds, extra income and notes need. Nothing relies on these constraints for a user-facing error (the API validates first); they keep bad data out if a bug or a later plan slips.

**Files:**
- Create: `server/src/main/resources/db/migration/V006__categories_and_transaction_rules.sql`
- Create: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/Category.java`, `CategoryEntity.java`, `CategoryRepository.java`, `CategoryService.java`
- Test: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/ingestion/TransactionSchemaApiTest.java`, `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/ingestion/CategoryServiceApiTest.java`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the `transactions` table (Plan 1, V003), `NotFoundException`.
- Produces: table `categories (code, name, sort_order, discretionary)`; columns `transactions.credit_kind`, `assigned_savings_paise`, `assigned_pool_paise`, `note`; `Category(String code, String name, int sortOrder, boolean discretionary)`; `CategoryService.all() : List<Category>` (sorted), `CategoryService.exists(String code) : boolean`, `CategoryService.get(String code) : Category` (404 when unknown). Plans 4 and 7 use these codes and this service.

- [ ] **Step 3: The migration**

`server/src/main/resources/db/migration/V006__categories_and_transaction_rules.sql`:

```sql
-- Spec 2.4: twelve fixed categories. Reference data keyed by its code; Plans 4 and 7 use the same codes.
create table categories (
    code varchar(30) primary key,
    name varchar(40) not null,
    sort_order int not null unique,
    discretionary boolean not null,
    created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

insert into categories (code, name, sort_order, discretionary) values
    ('eating_out', 'Eating out', 1, true),
    ('food_delivery', 'Food delivery', 2, true),
    ('quick_commerce', 'Quick commerce', 3, true),
    ('shopping', 'Shopping', 4, true),
    ('travel', 'Travel', 5, true),
    ('upi_to_people', 'UPI to people', 6, false),
    ('groceries', 'Groceries', 7, false),
    ('subscriptions', 'Subscriptions', 8, false),
    ('bills_recharges', 'Bills and recharges', 9, false),
    ('entertainment', 'Entertainment', 10, true),
    ('health', 'Health', 11, false),
    ('other', 'Other', 12, false);

-- credit_kind: a credit is either a refund (lowers spent) or income (counts only once assigned, spec 2.1).
-- assigned_*: how an income credit was split between savings and the pool.
-- note: spec 2.3 lists an optional note on quick-add.
alter table transactions
    add column credit_kind varchar(8),
    add column assigned_savings_paise bigint,
    add column assigned_pool_paise bigint,
    add column note varchar(140);

alter table transactions
    add constraint transactions_category_fk foreign key (category_code) references categories (code),
    add constraint transactions_tag_ck check (tag is null or tag in ('need', 'want', 'regret')),
    add constraint transactions_payment_method_ck check (payment_method in ('upi', 'card', 'cash', 'unknown')),
    add constraint transactions_source_ck check (source in ('manual', 'statement', 'aa')),
    add constraint transactions_status_ck check (status in ('pending', 'confirmed', 'statement_only')),
    add constraint transactions_credit_kind_ck check (
        (direction = 'debit' and credit_kind is null)
        or (direction = 'credit' and credit_kind in ('refund', 'income'))),
    add constraint transactions_spread_ck check (not spread_over_cycle or direction = 'debit'),
    add constraint transactions_assigned_ck check (
        (assigned_savings_paise is null and assigned_pool_paise is null)
        or (credit_kind = 'income' and assigned_savings_paise >= 0 and assigned_pool_paise >= 0
            and assigned_savings_paise + assigned_pool_paise = amount_paise));
```

```java
public record Category(String code, String name, int sortOrder, boolean discretionary) {}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/CategoryEntity.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.Id;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;

/** Read-only reference data seeded by V006. */
@Entity
@Table(name = "categories")
class CategoryEntity {
    @Id
    private String code;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private String name;

    @Column(name = "sort_order", nullable = false)
    private int sortOrder;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private boolean discretionary;

    protected CategoryEntity() {}

    Category toRecord() {
        return new Category(code, name, sortOrder, discretionary);
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/CategoryRepository.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import java.util.List;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;

interface CategoryRepository extends JpaRepository<CategoryEntity, String> {
    List<CategoryEntity> findAllByOrderBySortOrderAsc();
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/CategoryService.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import in.gullak.common.error.NotFoundException;
import java.util.List;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

/** The twelve fixed categories (spec 2.4). They never change at runtime, so the list is read once. */
@Service
public class CategoryService {
    private final CategoryRepository repo;
    private volatile List<Category> cache;

    CategoryService(CategoryRepository repo) {
        this.repo = repo;
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<Category> all() {
        List<Category> c = cache;
        if (c == null) {
            c = repo.findAllByOrderBySortOrderAsc().stream().map(CategoryEntity::toRecord).toList();
            cache = c;
        }
        return c;
    }

    public boolean exists(String code) {
        return code != null && all().stream().anyMatch(c -> c.code().equals(code));
    }

    public Category get(String code) {
        return all().stream().filter(c -> c.code().equals(code)).findFirst()
            .orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Category"));
    }
}
```

### Task 3: The XP ledger

**Owner:** backend-engineer. **Test IDs:** XP-01, XP-02, XP-03, XP-04 (service half; Task 4 and Task 6 prove them through the API). **Red team:** yes (XP farming: parallel awards, repeat awards for one spend, reversing another user's events).

XP is a ledger of events, never a counter (spec 2.6, 4.1). Each event names what earned it (`ref_type`, `ref_id`), so the same spend can never earn twice and its delete can take exactly its own XP back.

**Files:**
- Create: `server/src/main/resources/db/migration/V007__xp_events.sql`
- Create: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/gamification/XpKind.java`, `XpEventEntity.java`, `XpEventRepository.java`, `XpLedgerRepository.java`, `XpService.java`
- Test: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/gamification/XpServiceApiTest.java`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Ist.today(Clock)`, `Events.XP_AWARDED`, `Events.XP_REVERSED`.
- Produces: `XpKind` (`SPEND_LOGGED(5 XP, cap 10 a day)`; Plan 6 appends kinds), `XpService.REF_TRANSACTION = "transaction"`, `XpService.award(UUID userId, XpKind kind, String refType, UUID refId) : int` (XP earned, 0 when capped or already awarded; joins the caller's transaction), `XpService.reverse(UUID userId, String refType, UUID refId) : int` (XP taken back), `XpService.total(UUID userId) : long`, `XpService.activeAwards(UUID userId, String refType, Collection<UUID> refIds) : Map<UUID, Integer>`. Table `xp_events (id, user_id, kind, xp, ref_type, ref_id, day, reversed_at, created_at, updated_at)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`server/src/test/java/in/gullak/gamification/XpServiceApiTest.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.gamification;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.Callable;
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.Future;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;

class XpServiceApiTest extends ApiTest {
    @Autowired XpService xp;

    private long events(TestUser u) {
        return jdbc.sql("select count(*) from xp_events where user_id = ? and reversed_at is null")
            .param(u.id()).query(Long.class).single();
    }

    @Test
    void aLoggedSpendEarnsFive() throws Exception { // XP-01
        TestUser u = signIn();
        assertThat(xp.award(u.id(), XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, UUID.randomUUID())).isEqualTo(5);
        assertThat(xp.total(u.id())).isEqualTo(5L);
    }

    @Test
    void theEleventhSpendOfTheDayEarnsNothing() throws Exception { // XP-02
        TestUser u = signIn();
        List<Integer> earned = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < 11; i++) {
            earned.add(xp.award(u.id(), XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, UUID.randomUUID()));
        }
        assertThat(earned.subList(0, 10)).containsOnly(5);
        assertThat(earned.get(10)).isZero();
        assertThat(events(u)).isEqualTo(10L);
        assertThat(xp.total(u.id())).isEqualTo(50L);
    }

    @Test
    void theCapResetsAtMidnightIst() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = signIn();
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2026-10-23T18:29:00Z")); // 23:59 IST
        for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            xp.award(u.id(), XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, UUID.randomUUID());
        }
        assertThat(xp.award(u.id(), XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, UUID.randomUUID())).isZero();
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2026-10-23T18:31:00Z")); // 00:01 IST the next day
        assertThat(xp.award(u.id(), XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, UUID.randomUUID())).isEqualTo(5);
    }

    @Test
    void oneSpendNeverEarnsTwice() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = signIn();
        UUID spend = UUID.randomUUID();
        assertThat(xp.award(u.id(), XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, spend)).isEqualTo(5);
        assertThat(xp.award(u.id(), XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, spend)).isZero();
        assertThat(events(u)).isEqualTo(1L);
    }

    @Test
    void reverseTakesTheEventBackAndTheTotalExcludesIt() throws Exception { // XP-03
        TestUser u = signIn();
        UUID spend = UUID.randomUUID();
        xp.award(u.id(), XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, spend);
        assertThat(xp.reverse(u.id(), XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, spend)).isEqualTo(5);
        assertThat(xp.total(u.id())).isZero();
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select reversed_at is not null from xp_events where ref_id = ?").param(spend)
            .query(Boolean.class).single()).isTrue();
        assertThat(xp.reverse(u.id(), XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, spend)).isZero(); // twice: nothing more
    }

    @Test
    void reversingASpendThatEarnedNothingChangesNothing() throws Exception { // XP-04
        TestUser u = signIn();
        UUID eleventh = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < 11; i++) {
            eleventh = UUID.randomUUID();
            xp.award(u.id(), XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, eleventh);
        }
        assertThat(xp.reverse(u.id(), XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, eleventh)).isZero();
        assertThat(xp.total(u.id())).isEqualTo(50L);
    }

    @Test
    void anotherUsersEventsAreUntouchable() throws Exception {
        TestUser a = signIn();
        TestUser b = signIn();
        UUID spend = UUID.randomUUID();
        xp.award(a.id(), XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, spend);
        assertThat(xp.reverse(b.id(), XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, spend)).isZero();
        assertThat(xp.total(a.id())).isEqualTo(5L);
        assertThat(xp.activeAwards(b.id(), XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, List.of(spend))).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void activeAwardsByReference() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = signIn();
        UUID kept = UUID.randomUUID();
        UUID reversed = UUID.randomUUID();
        UUID never = UUID.randomUUID();
        xp.award(u.id(), XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, kept);
        xp.award(u.id(), XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, reversed);
        xp.reverse(u.id(), XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, reversed);
        assertThat(xp.activeAwards(u.id(), XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, List.of(kept, reversed, never)))
            .isEqualTo(Map.of(kept, 5));
        assertThat(xp.activeAwards(u.id(), XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, List.of())).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void parallelAwardsNeverPassTheDailyCap() throws Exception { // Review Focus
        TestUser u = signIn();
        ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(11);
        try {
            CountDownLatch start = new CountDownLatch(1);
            List<Future<Integer>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 11; i++) {
                Callable<Integer> call = () -> {
                    start.await();
                    return xp.award(u.id(), XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, UUID.randomUUID());
                };
                futures.add(pool.submit(call));
            }
            start.countDown();
            int sum = 0;
            for (Future<Integer> f : futures) {
                sum += f.get();
            }
            assertThat(sum).isEqualTo(50);
            assertThat(events(u)).isEqualTo(10L);
        } finally {
            pool.shutdownNow();
        }
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/resources/db/migration/V007__xp_events.sql`:

```sql
-- Spec 2.6 and 4.1: XP is a ledger of events, never a counter. A reversed event keeps its row.
create table xp_events (
    id uuid primary key,
    user_id uuid not null references users (id) on delete cascade,
    kind varchar(30) not null,
    xp int not null check (xp > 0),
    ref_type varchar(30) not null,
    ref_id uuid not null,
    -- The IST day the event was earned; daily caps count on it.
    day date not null,
    reversed_at timestamptz,
    created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    unique (user_id, kind, ref_type, ref_id)
);
create index xp_events_user_kind_day_idx on xp_events (user_id, kind, day) where reversed_at is null;
create index xp_events_user_ref_idx on xp_events (user_id, ref_type, ref_id);
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/gamification/XpKind.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.gamification;

/** What earns XP (spec 2.6). Plan 6 appends the other rows of the XP table. */
public enum XpKind {
    SPEND_LOGGED(5, 10);

    private final int xp;
    private final Integer dailyCap;

    XpKind(int xp, Integer dailyCap) {
        this.xp = xp;
        this.dailyCap = dailyCap;
    }

    public int xp() { return xp; }

    /** At most this many events of the kind per IST day, or null for no daily cap. */
    public Integer dailyCap() { return dailyCap; }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/gamification/XpEventEntity.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.gamification;

import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.Id;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.hibernate.annotations.UpdateTimestamp;

/** Rows are created by {@link XpLedgerRepository#insertIfAbsent}; JPA only reads them and marks reversals. */
@Entity
@Table(name = "xp_events")
class XpEventEntity {
    @Id
    private UUID id;

    @Column(name = "user_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID userId;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private String kind;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private int xp;

    @Column(name = "ref_type", nullable = false)
    private String refType;

    @Column(name = "ref_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID refId;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private LocalDate day;

    @Column(name = "reversed_at")
    private Instant reversedAt;

    @UpdateTimestamp
    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private Instant updatedAt;

    protected XpEventEntity() {}

    int getXp() { return xp; }
    UUID getRefId() { return refId; }

    void reverse(Instant at) {
        this.reversedAt = at;
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/gamification/XpEventRepository.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.gamification;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;

interface XpEventRepository extends JpaRepository<XpEventEntity, UUID> {

    @Query("""
            select e from XpEventEntity e
            where e.userId = :userId and e.refType = :refType and e.refId = :refId and e.reversedAt is null
            """)
    List<XpEventEntity> findActive(@Param("userId") UUID userId, @Param("refType") String refType, @Param("refId") UUID refId);

    @Query("""
            select e from XpEventEntity e
            where e.userId = :userId and e.refType = :refType and e.refId in :refIds and e.reversedAt is null
            """)
    List<XpEventEntity> findActiveIn(@Param("userId") UUID userId, @Param("refType") String refType,
                                     @Param("refIds") Collection<UUID> refIds);

    @Query("select coalesce(sum(e.xp), 0) from XpEventEntity e where e.userId = :userId and e.reversedAt is null")
    long total(@Param("userId") UUID userId);
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/gamification/XpLedgerRepository.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.gamification;

import in.gullak.common.db.Db;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

/** The concurrency-sensitive half of the ledger: the per-user lock, the cap count and the idempotent insert. */
@Repository
class XpLedgerRepository {
    private final JdbcClient jdbc;

    XpLedgerRepository(JdbcClient jdbc) {
        this.jdbc = jdbc;
    }

    /** Serialises awards of one kind for one user until the caller's transaction ends, so a cap cannot be raced. */
    void lock(UUID userId, XpKind kind) {
        jdbc.sql("select pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(?, 0))")
            .param("xp:" + userId + ":" + kind.name())
            .query((rs, n) -> Boolean.TRUE)
            .list();
    }

    int countActive(UUID userId, XpKind kind, LocalDate day) {
        return jdbc.sql("""
                select count(*) from xp_events
                where user_id = ? and kind = ? and day = ? and reversed_at is null
                """)
            .params(userId, kind.name(), day)
            .query(Integer.class)
            .single();
    }

    /** False when this reference already earned this kind (a replay, or a race the lock already settled). */
    boolean insertIfAbsent(UUID id, UUID userId, XpKind kind, String refType, UUID refId, LocalDate day, Instant now) {
        return jdbc.sql("""
                insert into xp_events (id, user_id, kind, xp, ref_type, ref_id, day, created_at, updated_at)
                values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                on conflict (user_id, kind, ref_type, ref_id) do nothing
                """)
            .params(id, userId, kind.name(), kind.xp(), refType, refId, day, Db.ts(now), Db.ts(now))
            .update() == 1;
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/gamification/XpService.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.gamification;

import in.gullak.common.log.Events;
import in.gullak.common.time.Ist;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

/** The XP ledger (spec 2.6). Plan 6 adds levels, streaks and badges on top of these events. */
@Service
public class XpService {
    public static final String REF_TRANSACTION = "transaction";

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(XpService.class);

    private final XpLedgerRepository ledger;
    private final XpEventRepository events;
    private final Clock clock;

    XpService(XpLedgerRepository ledger, XpEventRepository events, Clock clock) {
        this.ledger = ledger;
        this.events = events;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    /** Awards {@code kind} for one reference. Returns the XP earned: 0 when the daily cap is reached or it was already awarded. */
    @Transactional
    public int award(UUID userId, XpKind kind, String refType, UUID refId) {
        LocalDate day = Ist.today(clock);
        ledger.lock(userId, kind);
        if (kind.dailyCap() != null && ledger.countActive(userId, kind, day) >= kind.dailyCap()) {
            return 0;
        }
        if (!ledger.insertIfAbsent(UUID.randomUUID(), userId, kind, refType, refId, day, clock.instant())) {
            return 0;
        }
        log.info("{} user={} kind={} xp={} ref={}", Events.XP_AWARDED, userId, kind, kind.xp(), refId);
        return kind.xp();
    }

    /** Takes back every live event of the caller's that this reference earned. Returns the XP taken back. */
    @Transactional
    public int reverse(UUID userId, String refType, UUID refId) {
        List<XpEventEntity> live = events.findActive(userId, refType, refId);
        int taken = 0;
        for (XpEventEntity e : live) {
            e.reverse(clock.instant());
            taken += e.getXp();
        }
        if (taken > 0) {
            log.info("{} user={} xp={} ref={}", Events.XP_REVERSED, userId, taken, refId);
        }
        return taken;
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public long total(UUID userId) {
        return events.total(userId);
    }

    /** XP still held by each reference; references that earned nothing are absent. */
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Map<UUID, Integer> activeAwards(UUID userId, String refType, Collection<UUID> refIds) {
        if (refIds.isEmpty()) {
            return Map.of();
        }
        Map<UUID, Integer> out = new HashMap<>();
        for (XpEventEntity e : events.findActiveIn(userId, refType, refIds)) {
            out.merge(e.getRefId(), e.getXp(), Integer::sum);
        }
        return out;
    }
}
```

```java
    /** 09:12 IST on the example day, Fri 23 Oct 2026. */
    protected static final Instant COFFEE_TIME = Instant.parse("2026-10-23T03:42:00Z");

    /** A quick-add body. {@code extra} is spliced in before the closing brace, e.g. ",\"spreadOverCycle\":true". */
    protected static String spendJson(UUID clientId, long paise, Instant at, String category, String payment, String extra) {
        return "{\"clientId\":\"" + clientId + "\",\"amountPaise\":" + paise + ",\"occurredAt\":\"" + at
            + "\",\"category\":\"" + category + "\",\"paymentMethod\":\"" + payment + "\"" + extra + "}";
    }

    protected ResultActions postSpend(TestUser user, String json) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/transactions").with(auth(user))
            .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content(json));
    }

    /** Logs a UPI eating-out spend through the API and returns its id. */
    protected String logSpend(TestUser user, long paise, Instant at) throws Exception {
        return logSpend(user, paise, at, "");
    }

    protected String logSpend(TestUser user, long paise, Instant at, String extra) throws Exception {
        String body = postSpend(user, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), paise, at, "eating_out", "UPI", extra))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated())
            .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        return JsonPath.read(body, "$.id");
    }
```

`server/src/test/java/in/gullak/ingestion/CreateSpendApiTest.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.Callable;
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.Future;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.MvcResult;

class CreateSpendApiTest extends ApiTest {

    private long rows(TestUser u) {
        return jdbc.sql("select count(*) from transactions where user_id = ?").param(u.id()).query(Long.class).single();
    }

    private long xpEvents(TestUser u) {
        return jdbc.sql("select count(*) from xp_events where user_id = ? and reversed_at is null")
            .param(u.id()).query(Long.class).single();
    }

    @Test
    void createsAPendingSpendWorthFiveXp() throws Exception { // API-SP-01, XP-01
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID clientId = UUID.randomUUID();
        postSpend(u, spendJson(clientId, 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI",
                ",\"merchant\":\"  Third   Wave Coffee \",\"note\":\"with Riya\",\"tag\":\"WANT\""))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.id").isString())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.clientId").value(clientId.toString()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.kind").value("SPEND"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.amountPaise").value(25_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.occurredAt").value("2026-10-23T03:42:00Z"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.merchant").value("Third Wave Coffee"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.note").value("with Riya"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.category").value("eating_out"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.tag").value("WANT"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.paymentMethod").value("UPI"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.source").value("MANUAL"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value("PENDING"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.spreadOverCycle").value(false))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.linked").value(false))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.xpAwarded").value(5));
        assertThat(xpEvents(u)).isEqualTo(1L);
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select direction || '/' || payment_method || '/' || source || '/' || status from transactions where client_id = ?")
            .param(clientId).query(String.class).single()).isEqualTo("debit/upi/manual/pending");
    }

    @Test
    void sameClientIdAndBodyIsAReplay() throws Exception { // API-SP-02
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String body = spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", ",\"merchant\":\"Third Wave\"");
        String first = postSpend(u, body).andExpect(status().isCreated()).andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        postSpend(u, body)
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.id").value((String) JsonPath.read(first, "$.id")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.xpAwarded").value(5));
        assertThat(rows(u)).isEqualTo(1L);
        assertThat(xpEvents(u)).isEqualTo(1L);
    }

    @Test
    void sameClientIdWithADifferentBodyConflicts() throws Exception { // API-SP-03
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID clientId = UUID.randomUUID();
        postSpend(u, spendJson(clientId, 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", "")).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(clientId, 26_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isConflict())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("CLIENT_ID_CONFLICT"));
        postSpend(u, spendJson(clientId, 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "shopping", "UPI", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isConflict());
        assertThat(rows(u)).isEqualTo(1L);
    }

    @Test
    void replayingADeletedSpendConflicts() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID clientId = UUID.randomUUID();
        String body = spendJson(clientId, 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", "");
        postSpend(u, body).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        jdbc.sql("update transactions set deleted_at = now() where client_id = ?").param(clientId).update();
        postSpend(u, body).andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("CLIENT_ID_CONFLICT"));
    }

    @Test
    void theSameClientIdBelongsToEachUserSeparately() throws Exception {
        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();
        UUID clientId = UUID.randomUUID();
        postSpend(a, spendJson(clientId, 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", "")).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        postSpend(b, spendJson(clientId, 99_000, COFFEE_TIME, "shopping", "CARD", "")).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        assertThat(rows(a)).isEqualTo(1L);
        assertThat(rows(b)).isEqualTo(1L);
    }

    @Test
    void parallelPostsWithOneClientIdMakeOneRowAndOneXp() throws Exception { // Review Focus
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String body = spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", "");
        ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(4);
        try {
            CountDownLatch start = new CountDownLatch(1);
            List<Future<MvcResult>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
                Callable<MvcResult> call = () -> {
                    start.await();
                    return postSpend(u, body).andReturn();
                };
                futures.add(pool.submit(call));
            }
            start.countDown();
            List<Integer> statuses = new ArrayList<>();
            List<String> ids = new ArrayList<>();
            for (Future<MvcResult> f : futures) {
                MvcResult r = f.get();
                statuses.add(r.getResponse().getStatus());
                ids.add(JsonPath.read(r.getResponse().getContentAsString(), "$.id"));
            }
            assertThat(statuses).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(201, 200, 200, 200);
            assertThat(ids).containsOnly(ids.getFirst());
            assertThat(rows(u)).isEqualTo(1L);
            assertThat(xpEvents(u)).isEqualTo(1L);
        } finally {
            pool.shutdownNow();
        }
    }

    @Test
    void amountsAreValidated() throws Exception { // API-SP-04, 05, 06, API-X-09
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        for (String amount : List.of("0", "-100", "1000000000", "250.5", "\"25000\"")) {
            String body = "{\"clientId\":\"" + UUID.randomUUID() + "\",\"amountPaise\":" + amount
                + ",\"occurredAt\":\"" + COFFEE_TIME + "\",\"category\":\"eating_out\",\"paymentMethod\":\"UPI\"}";
            postSpend(u, body).andExpect(status().isBadRequest()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("VALIDATION"));
        }
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 999_999_900, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated()); // ₹99,99,999 is the cap itself
    }

    @Test
    void datesAreValidated() throws Exception { // API-SP-07, 08, 15, 16
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        Instant now = clock.instant();
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, now.plus(Duration.ofDays(2)), "eating_out", "UPI", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields[0].field").value("occurredAt"));
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, now.plus(Duration.ofDays(1)), "eating_out", "UPI", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, now.minus(Duration.ofDays(60)), "eating_out", "UPI", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, now.minus(Duration.ofDays(91)), "eating_out", "UPI", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields[0].field").value("occurredAt"));
    }

    @Test
    void categoryPaymentAndTextAreValidated() throws Exception { // API-SP-09, 10, 11
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "crypto", "UPI", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields[0].field").value("category"));
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "Eating Out", "UPI", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "CHEQUE", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UNKNOWN", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields[0].field").value("paymentMethod"));
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", ",\"merchant\":\"" + "m".repeat(81) + "\""))
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", ",\"merchant\":\"" + "m".repeat(80) + "\""))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", ",\"note\":\"" + "n".repeat(141) + "\""))
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", ",\"merchant\":\"line\\nbreak\""))
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", ",\"tag\":\"MEH\""))
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        String noClientId = "{\"amountPaise\":25000,\"occurredAt\":\"" + COFFEE_TIME + "\",\"category\":\"eating_out\",\"paymentMethod\":\"UPI\"}";
        postSpend(u, noClientId).andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
    }

    @Test
    void cashIsAccepted() throws Exception { // API-SP-12
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 6_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "CASH", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.paymentMethod").value("CASH"));
    }

    @Test
    void spreadOverCycleIsStored() throws Exception { // API-SP-13 (the budget half is Task 7)
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 1_500_000, COFFEE_TIME, "travel", "CARD", ",\"spreadOverCycle\":true"))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.spreadOverCycle").value(true));
    }

    @Test
    void theEleventhSpendOfTheDayEarnsNoXp() throws Exception { // API-SP-14, XP-02
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            logSpend(u, 2_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        }
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 2_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.xpAwarded").value(0));
        assertThat(xpEvents(u)).isEqualTo(10L);
    }

    @Test
    void refundsAndIncomeAreCreditsWithoutXp() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 30_000, COFFEE_TIME, "shopping", "CARD", ",\"kind\":\"REFUND\""))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.kind").value("REFUND"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.xpAwarded").value(0));
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 500_000, COFFEE_TIME, "other", "UPI", ",\"kind\":\"INCOME\""))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.kind").value("INCOME"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.assignedPoolPaise").doesNotExist());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 30_000, COFFEE_TIME, "shopping", "CARD", ",\"kind\":\"REFUND\",\"tag\":\"NEED\""))
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields[0].field").value("tag"));
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 30_000, COFFEE_TIME, "shopping", "CARD", ",\"kind\":\"REFUND\",\"spreadOverCycle\":true"))
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields[0].field").value("spreadOverCycle"));
        assertThat(xpEvents(u)).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void getReturnsOwnSpendsOnly() throws Exception { // API-SP-22, 23, 24
        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();
        String id = logSpend(a, 25_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/transactions/" + id).with(auth(a)))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.amountPaise").value(25_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.xpAwarded").value(5));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/transactions/" + id).with(auth(b)))
            .andExpect(status().isNotFound()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_FOUND"));
        jdbc.sql("update transactions set deleted_at = now() where id = ?::uuid").param(id).update();
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/transactions/" + id).with(auth(a))).andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/transactions/not-a-uuid").with(auth(a))).andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/transactions/" + id)).andExpect(status().isUnauthorized());
    }

    @Test
    void hostileTextIsStoredAsText() throws Exception { // API-X-07
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String body = postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI",
                ",\"merchant\":\"' OR 1=1 --\",\"note\":\"<script>alert(1)</script>\""))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated()).andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        assertThat((String) JsonPath.read(body, "$.merchant")).isEqualTo("' OR 1=1 --");
        assertThat((String) JsonPath.read(body, "$.note")).isEqualTo("<script>alert(1)</script>");
        assertThat(rows(u)).isEqualTo(1L);
    }

    @Test
    void unknownFieldsAreIgnoredAndCannotPickTheUser() throws Exception { // API-X-06, mass assignment
        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();
        postSpend(a, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 25_000, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI",
                ",\"userId\":\"" + b.id() + "\",\"status\":\"CONFIRMED\",\"source\":\"STATEMENT\",\"xpAwarded\":500"))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value("PENDING"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.source").value("MANUAL"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.xpAwarded").value(5));
        assertThat(rows(b)).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void createsAreRateLimitedPerUser() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        for (int i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
            postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 100, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", "")).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        }
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 100, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", ""))
            .andExpect(status().isTooManyRequests())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("RATE_LIMITED"));
        TestUser other = exampleUser();
        postSpend(other, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 100, COFFEE_TIME, "eating_out", "UPI", "")).andExpect(status().isCreated());
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.springframework.boot.test.system.CapturedOutput;
import org.springframework.boot.test.system.OutputCaptureExtension;

@ExtendWith(OutputCaptureExtension.class)
class SpendLoggingApiTest extends ApiTest {
    private static final long AMOUNT = 4_321_09;
    private static final String MERCHANT = "Zebra Momo Point";
    private static final String NOTE = "Quokka birthday";

    @Test
    void creatingASpendLogsOneEventWithIdsOnly(CapturedOutput out) throws Exception { // LOG-03, LOG-05, SEC-01
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = logSpend(u, AMOUNT, COFFEE_TIME, ",\"merchant\":\"" + MERCHANT + "\",\"note\":\"" + NOTE + "\"");
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/transactions/" + id).with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isOk());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), AMOUNT, COFFEE_TIME, "crypto", "UPI", ",\"merchant\":\"" + MERCHANT + "\""))
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest());

        String logs = out.getOut();
        assertThat(logs).contains("spend.created user=" + u.id() + " id=" + id);
        assertThat(logs).contains("xp.awarded user=" + u.id());
        assertThat(logs.lines().filter(l -> l.contains("spend.created")).count()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(logs).doesNotContain(String.valueOf(AMOUNT)).doesNotContain(MERCHANT).doesNotContain(NOTE)
            .doesNotContain("Zebra").doesNotContain("Quokka");
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/TransactionKind.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

/** SPEND is a debit; REFUND and INCOME are credits (spec 2.1). Stored as direction + credit_kind. */
public enum TransactionKind {
    SPEND("debit", null),
    REFUND("credit", "refund"),
    INCOME("credit", "income");

    private final String direction;
    private final String creditKind;

    TransactionKind(String direction, String creditKind) {
        this.direction = direction;
        this.creditKind = creditKind;
    }

    public String direction() { return direction; }

    public String creditKind() { return creditKind; }

    public static TransactionKind fromDb(String direction, String creditKind) {
        if ("debit".equals(direction)) {
            return SPEND;
        }
        return "income".equals(creditKind) ? INCOME : REFUND;
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * The pipeline's input (spec 2.3: source → normalise → categorise → match → save). The manual source builds it
 * from a quick-add; Plan 4 builds it from statement lines (source STATEMENT, fingerprint, importId, clientId null).
 */
public record NewTransaction(
    UUID clientId,
    TransactionKind kind,
    long amountPaise,
    Instant occurredAt,
    String merchant,
    String note,
    String categoryCode,
    SpendTag tag,
    PaymentMethod paymentMethod,
    TransactionSource source,
    TransactionStatus status,
    boolean spreadOverCycle,
    boolean countsInBudget,
    String fingerprint,
    UUID importId) {

    NewTransaction withText(String cleanMerchant, String cleanNote) {
        return new NewTransaction(clientId, kind, amountPaise, occurredAt, cleanMerchant, cleanNote, categoryCode, tag,
            paymentMethod, source, status, spreadOverCycle, countsInBudget, fingerprint, importId);
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import in.gullak.common.money.Money;
import in.gullak.common.web.SingleLineText;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Max;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Pattern;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Positive;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

/** POST /transactions. {@code kind} defaults to SPEND and {@code spreadOverCycle} to false. */
public record NewSpendRequest(
    @NotNull UUID clientId,
    TransactionKind kind,
    @NotNull @Positive @Max(Money.MAX_AMOUNT_PAISE) Long amountPaise,
    @NotNull Instant occurredAt,
    @NotBlank @Size(max = 30) @Pattern(regexp = "[a-z_]+", message = "Pick a category") String category,
    @NotNull PaymentMethod paymentMethod,
    @Size(max = 80) @SingleLineText String merchant,
    @Size(max = 140) @SingleLineText String note,
    SpendTag tag,
    Boolean spreadOverCycle) {

    NewTransaction toNewTransaction() {
        return new NewTransaction(clientId, kind == null ? TransactionKind.SPEND : kind, amountPaise, occurredAt,
            merchant, note, category, tag, paymentMethod, TransactionSource.MANUAL, TransactionStatus.PENDING,
            Boolean.TRUE.equals(spreadOverCycle), true, null, null);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Entity and repositories**

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/TransactionEntity.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.Id;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.hibernate.annotations.UpdateTimestamp;

/**
 * Rows are inserted by {@link TransactionInsertRepository} (idempotent on client_id); JPA reads and updates them.
 * merchant_raw_enc is left unmapped until Plan 4 encrypts statement text into it.
 */
@Entity
@Table(name = "transactions")
class TransactionEntity {
    @Id
    private UUID id;

    @Column(name = "user_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID userId;

    @Column(name = "client_id")
    private UUID clientId;

    @Column(name = "amount_paise", nullable = false)
    private long amountPaise;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private String direction;

    @Column(name = "credit_kind")
    private String creditKind;

    @Column(name = "occurred_at", nullable = false)
    private Instant occurredAt;

    @Column(name = "merchant_name")
    private String merchantName;

    @Column
    private String note;

    @Column(name = "category_code", nullable = false)
    private String categoryCode;

    @Column
    private String tag;

    @Column(name = "payment_method", nullable = false)
    private String paymentMethod;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private String source;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private String status;

    @Column(name = "counts_in_budget", nullable = false)
    private boolean countsInBudget;

    @Column(name = "spread_over_cycle", nullable = false)
    private boolean spreadOverCycle;

    @Column(name = "fixed_cost_id")
    private UUID fixedCostId;

    @Column(name = "goal_id")
    private UUID goalId;

    @Column(name = "linked_txn_id")
    private UUID linkedTxnId;

    @Column(name = "import_id")
    private UUID importId;

    @Column
    private String fingerprint;

    @Column(name = "assigned_savings_paise")
    private Long assignedSavingsPaise;

    @Column(name = "assigned_pool_paise")
    private Long assignedPoolPaise;

    @Column(name = "deleted_at")
    private Instant deletedAt;

    @UpdateTimestamp
    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private Instant updatedAt;

    protected TransactionEntity() {}

    UUID getId() { return id; }
    long getAmountPaise() { return amountPaise; }
    Instant getOccurredAt() { return occurredAt; }
    String getCategoryCode() { return categoryCode; }
    UUID getLinkedTxnId() { return linkedTxnId; }

    TransactionKind kind() { return TransactionKind.fromDb(direction, creditKind); }
    TransactionSource source() { return TransactionSource.fromDb(source); }
    TransactionStatus status() { return TransactionStatus.fromDb(status); }
    boolean isDeleted() { return deletedAt != null; }

    /** True when {@code t} (already normalised) describes this row: the test for an idempotent replay. */
    boolean matches(NewTransaction t) {
        return kind() == t.kind()
            && amountPaise == t.amountPaise()
            && occurredAt.truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.MILLIS).equals(t.occurredAt().truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.MILLIS))
            && categoryCode.equals(t.categoryCode())
            && paymentMethod.equals(t.paymentMethod().db())
            && Objects.equals(merchantName, t.merchant())
            && Objects.equals(note, t.note())
            && Objects.equals(tag, t.tag() == null ? null : t.tag().db())
            && spreadOverCycle == t.spreadOverCycle();
    }

    Transaction toRecord(int xpAwarded) {
        return new Transaction(id, clientId, kind(), amountPaise, occurredAt, merchantName, note, categoryCode,
            SpendTag.fromDb(tag), PaymentMethod.fromDb(paymentMethod), source(), status(), spreadOverCycle,
            linkedTxnId != null, assignedSavingsPaise, assignedPoolPaise, xpAwarded);
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;

interface TransactionRepository extends JpaRepository<TransactionEntity, UUID> {

    Optional<TransactionEntity> findByUserIdAndClientId(UUID userId, UUID clientId);

    Optional<TransactionEntity> findByIdAndUserIdAndDeletedAtIsNull(UUID id, UUID userId);
}
```
