`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/TransactionInsertRepository.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import in.gullak.common.db.Db;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

/** The one insert into transactions. ON CONFLICT on (user_id, client_id) makes a retried quick-add a no-op. */
@Repository
class TransactionInsertRepository {
    private final JdbcClient jdbc;

    TransactionInsertRepository(JdbcClient jdbc) {
        this.jdbc = jdbc;
    }

    /** False when this user already has a row with the same clientId (a replay or a racing duplicate). */
    boolean insertIfAbsent(UUID id, UUID userId, NewTransaction t) {
        return jdbc.sql("""
                insert into transactions (id, user_id, client_id, amount_paise, direction, credit_kind, occurred_at,
                    merchant_name, note, category_code, tag, payment_method, source, status, counts_in_budget,
                    spread_over_cycle, fingerprint, import_id)
                values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                on conflict (user_id, client_id) do nothing
                """)
            .params(id, userId, t.clientId(), t.amountPaise(), t.kind().direction(), t.kind().creditKind(),
                Db.ts(t.occurredAt()), t.merchant(), t.note(), t.categoryCode(), t.tag() == null ? null : t.tag().db(),
                t.paymentMethod().db(), t.source().db(), t.status().db(), t.countsInBudget(), t.spreadOverCycle(),
                t.fingerprint(), t.importId())
            .update() == 1;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: The service and controller**

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/TransactionService.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import in.gullak.common.error.ConflictException;
import in.gullak.common.error.ErrorCode;
import in.gullak.common.error.NotFoundException;
import in.gullak.common.error.ValidationException;
import in.gullak.common.log.Events;
import in.gullak.gamification.XpKind;
import in.gullak.gamification.XpService;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

/**
 * The one transaction pipeline (spec 2.3): source → normalise → categorise → match → save. This plan builds the
 * manual source and save; Plan 4 adds statement categorising and matching in front of the same {@link #save}.
 */
@Service
public class TransactionService {
    static final Duration MAX_FUTURE = Duration.ofDays(1);
    static final Duration MAX_AGE = Duration.ofDays(90);

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(TransactionService.class);

    private final TransactionRepository repo;
    private final TransactionInsertRepository inserts;
    private final CategoryService categories;
    private final XpService xp;
    private final Clock clock;

    TransactionService(TransactionRepository repo, TransactionInsertRepository inserts, CategoryService categories,
                       XpService xp, Clock clock) {
        this.repo = repo;
        this.inserts = inserts;
        this.categories = categories;
        this.xp = xp;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Transactional
    public SaveResult save(UUID userId, NewTransaction raw) {
        NewTransaction t = raw.withText(clean(raw.merchant()), clean(raw.note()));
        check(t);
        UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();
        if (!inserts.insertIfAbsent(id, userId, t)) {
            TransactionEntity existing = repo.findByUserIdAndClientId(userId, t.clientId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("insert skipped but no row holds the client id"));
            if (existing.isDeleted() || !existing.matches(t)) {
                throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.CLIENT_ID_CONFLICT, "This spend was already saved with different details");
            }
            return new SaveResult(withXp(userId, existing), false);
        }
        int earned = t.source() == TransactionSource.MANUAL && t.kind() == TransactionKind.SPEND
            ? xp.award(userId, XpKind.SPEND_LOGGED, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, id)
            : 0;
        TransactionEntity created = repo.findById(id)
            .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("inserted transaction not found"));
        log.info("{} user={} id={} source={} kind={} xp={}", Events.SPEND_CREATED, userId, id, t.source().db(), t.kind(), earned);
        return new SaveResult(created.toRecord(earned), true);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Transaction get(UUID userId, UUID id) {
        return withXp(userId, live(userId, id));
    }

    TransactionEntity live(UUID userId, UUID id) {
        return repo.findByIdAndUserIdAndDeletedAtIsNull(id, userId).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Spend"));
    }

    Transaction withXp(UUID userId, TransactionEntity e) {
        return e.toRecord(xp.activeAwards(userId, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, List.of(e.getId())).getOrDefault(e.getId(), 0));
    }

    /** Trims, collapses inner whitespace, and turns blank text into null. */
    static String clean(String text) {
        if (text == null) {
            return null;
        }
        String c = text.strip().replaceAll("\\s+", " ");
        return c.isEmpty() ? null : c;
    }

    private void check(NewTransaction t) {
        if (!categories.exists(t.categoryCode())) {
            throw new ValidationException("category", "Pick a category");
        }
        if (t.kind() != TransactionKind.SPEND && t.tag() != null) {
            throw new ValidationException("tag", "Only a spend can be tagged");
        }
        if (t.kind() != TransactionKind.SPEND && t.spreadOverCycle()) {
            throw new ValidationException("spreadOverCycle", "Only a spend can be spread over the cycle");
        }
        if (t.source() == TransactionSource.MANUAL) {
            Instant now = clock.instant();
            if (t.occurredAt().isAfter(now.plus(MAX_FUTURE))) {
                throw new ValidationException("occurredAt", "That time is in the future");
            }
            if (t.occurredAt().isBefore(now.minus(MAX_AGE))) {
                throw new ValidationException("occurredAt", "Spends older than 90 days come in with a statement");
            }
            if (t.paymentMethod() == PaymentMethod.UNKNOWN) {
                throw new ValidationException("paymentMethod", "Pick UPI, card or cash");
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/TransactionController.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import in.gullak.common.web.CurrentUser;
import in.gullak.common.web.RateLimiter;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.core.annotation.AuthenticationPrincipal;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.Jwt;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/transactions")
class TransactionController {
    /** Creates per user per hour. A day of offline spends is far below it; a script is not. */
    static final int CREATE_LIMIT = 200;

    private final TransactionService transactions;
    private final RateLimiter limiter;

    TransactionController(TransactionService transactions, RateLimiter limiter) {
        this.transactions = transactions;
        this.limiter = limiter;
    }

    @PostMapping
    ResponseEntity<Transaction> create(@AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt, @Valid @RequestBody NewSpendRequest body) {
        UUID user = CurrentUser.id(jwt);
        limiter.check("transactions.create:" + user, CREATE_LIMIT, Duration.ofHours(1));
        SaveResult r = transactions.save(user, body.toNewTransaction());
        return ResponseEntity.status(r.created() ? HttpStatus.CREATED : HttpStatus.OK).body(r.transaction());
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    Transaction get(@AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt, @PathVariable UUID id) {
        return transactions.get(CurrentUser.id(jwt), id);
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.OffsetDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.ResultActions;

class ListSpendsApiTest extends ApiTest {
    static final Instant NINE_TWELVE = Instant.parse("2026-10-23T03:42:00Z");
    static final Instant ONE_FORTY = Instant.parse("2026-10-23T08:10:00Z");
    static final Instant SIX_PM = Instant.parse("2026-10-23T12:30:00Z");
    static final Instant YESTERDAY = Instant.parse("2026-10-22T10:00:00Z");

    private ResultActions list(TestUser u, String query) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/transactions?" + query).with(auth(u)));
    }

    @Test
    void ownSpendsNewestFirstWithACursor() throws Exception { // API-SP-17
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String a = logSpend(u, 15_000, NINE_TWELVE);
        String b = logSpend(u, 3_000, ONE_FORTY);
        String c = logSpend(u, 9_000, SIX_PM);
        logSpend(u, 7_000, YESTERDAY);
        String page1 = list(u, "from=2026-10-23&to=2026-10-23&limit=2")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items.length()").value(2))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].id").value(c))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[1].id").value(b))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].xpAwarded").value(5))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.nextCursor").isString())
            .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        String cursor = JsonPath.read(page1, "$.nextCursor");
        list(u, "from=2026-10-23&to=2026-10-23&limit=2&cursor=" + cursor)
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items.length()").value(1))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].id").value(a))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.nextCursor").doesNotExist());
        list(u, "from=2026-10-22&to=2026-10-23").andExpect(jsonPath("$.items.length()").value(4));
    }

    @Test
    void daysAreIstDays() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String justAfterMidnight = logSpend(u, 1_000, Instant.parse("2026-10-22T18:31:00Z")); // 00:01 IST, 23 Oct
        logSpend(u, 1_000, Instant.parse("2026-10-22T18:29:00Z")); // 23:59 IST, 22 Oct
        list(u, "from=2026-10-23&to=2026-10-23")
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items.length()").value(1))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].id").value(justAfterMidnight));
    }

    @Test
    void filterByCategory() throws Exception { // API-SP-18
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 40_000, NINE_TWELVE, "food_delivery", "UPI", "")).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        logSpend(u, 15_000, ONE_FORTY);
        list(u, "from=2026-10-23&to=2026-10-23&category=food_delivery")
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items.length()").value(1))

    @Test
    void badRangesAreRejected() throws Exception { // API-SP-19
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        list(u, "from=2026-10-24&to=2026-10-23").andExpect(status().isBadRequest()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("VALIDATION"));

        TestUser u = exampleUser();

        jdbc.sql("update transactions set deleted_at = now() where id = ?::uuid").param(gone).update();
        jdbc.sql("""
                insert into transactions (id, user_id, amount_paise, direction, occurred_at, category_code,
                    payment_method, source, status, counts_in_budget, linked_txn_id)
                values (?, ?, 15000, 'debit', ?, 'eating_out', 'unknown', 'statement', 'confirmed', false, ?::uuid)
                """)
            .params(UUID.randomUUID(), u.id(), OffsetDateTime.parse("2026-10-23T04:00:00Z"), kept).update();
        list(u, "from=2026-10-23&to=2026-10-23")

    @Test
    void anotherUsersSpendsNeverAppear() throws Exception { // API-SP-21, API-X-01
        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();
        logSpend(b, 99_000, NINE_TWELVE);
        list(a, "from=2026-10-01&to=2026-10-31").andExpect(jsonPath("$.items.length()").value(0));
    }

    @Test
    void aCursorIsUselessToAnotherUser() throws Exception { // API-X-10
        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();
        logSpend(a, 1_000, NINE_TWELVE);
        logSpend(a, 1_000, ONE_FORTY);
        logSpend(b, 1_000, Instant.parse("2026-10-23T02:00:00Z"));
        String page = list(a, "from=2026-10-23&to=2026-10-23&limit=1").andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        String cursor = JsonPath.read(page, "$.nextCursor");
        list(b, "from=2026-10-23&to=2026-10-23&cursor=" + cursor)
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items.length()").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.nextCursor").doesNotExist());
    }

    @Test
    void walkingAllPagesSeesEveryRowOnce() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
            logSpend(u, 1_000 + i, NINE_TWELVE); // same instant: the id breaks the tie
        }
        String cursor = null;
        int seen = 0;
        for (int pageNo = 0; pageNo < 10; pageNo++) {
            String q = "from=2026-10-23&to=2026-10-23&limit=3" + (cursor == null ? "" : "&cursor=" + cursor);
            String body = list(u, q).andExpect(status().isOk()).andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
            List<String> ids = JsonPath.read(body, "$.items[*].id");
            seen += ids.size();
            cursor = JsonPath.read(body, "$.nextCursor");
            if (cursor == null) {
                break;
            }
        }
        assertThat(seen).isEqualTo(7);
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;

class CategoryTotalsApiTest extends ApiTest {
    @Autowired TransactionService transactions;

    @Test
    void debitsMinusRefundsPerCategoryLargestFirst() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        Instant at = Instant.parse("2026-10-20T06:00:00Z");
        logSpend(u, 15_000, at);
        logSpend(u, 3_000, at);
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 30_000, at, "quick_commerce", "UPI", "")).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 50_000, at, "shopping", "CARD", "")).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 10_000, at, "shopping", "CARD", ",\"kind\":\"REFUND\"")).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 500_000, at, "other", "UPI", ",\"kind\":\"INCOME\"")).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        String gone = logSpend(u, 99_000, at);
        jdbc.sql("update transactions set deleted_at = now() where id = ?::uuid").param(gone).update();
        logSpend(u, 77_000, Instant.parse("2026-09-20T06:00:00Z")); // outside the range

        assertThat(transactions.categoryTotals(u.id(), LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 1), LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 31)))
            .containsExactly(
                new CategoryTotal("shopping", 40_000, 1),
                new CategoryTotal("quick_commerce", 30_000, 1),
                new CategoryTotal("eating_out", 18_000, 2));
    }

    @Test
    void anotherUsersSpendsAreNotCounted() throws Exception {
        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();
        logSpend(b, 15_000, Instant.parse("2026-10-20T06:00:00Z"));
        assertThat(transactions.categoryTotals(a.id(), LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 1), LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 31))).isEmpty();
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/TransactionCursor.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import static java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8;

import in.gullak.common.error.ValidationException;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Keyset cursor: the last row's (occurred_at, id) and the user it was issued to. A cursor presented by another
 * user reads as "no more rows" (API-X-10); a malformed one is a 400.
 */
final class TransactionCursor {
    record Position(Instant at, UUID id) {}

    /** Sorts after every real row: the first page starts here. */
    static final Position START = new Position(Instant.parse("9999-12-31T00:00:00Z"), new UUID(-1L, -1L));

    private TransactionCursor() {}

    static String encode(UUID userId, Instant at, UUID id) {
        long micros = ChronoUnit.MICROS.between(Instant.EPOCH, at);
        String raw = "v1|" + userId + "|" + micros + "|" + id;
        return Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString(raw.getBytes(UTF_8));
    }

    static Optional<Position> decode(UUID userId, String cursor) {
        String[] parts;
        try {
            parts = new String(Base64.getUrlDecoder().decode(cursor), UTF_8).split("\\|");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new ValidationException("cursor", "That page link is not valid");
        }
        if (parts.length != 4 || !"v1".equals(parts[0])) {
            throw new ValidationException("cursor", "That page link is not valid");
        }
        try {
            UUID owner = UUID.fromString(parts[1]);
            Instant at = Instant.EPOCH.plus(Long.parseLong(parts[2]), ChronoUnit.MICROS);
            UUID id = UUID.fromString(parts[3]);
            return owner.equals(userId) ? Optional.of(new Position(at, id)) : Optional.empty();
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new ValidationException("cursor", "That page link is not valid");
        }
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/TransactionRepository.java` (whole file):

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;

interface TransactionRepository extends JpaRepository<TransactionEntity, UUID> {

    Optional<TransactionEntity> findByUserIdAndClientId(UUID userId, UUID clientId);

    Optional<TransactionEntity> findByIdAndUserIdAndDeletedAtIsNull(UUID id, UUID userId);

    /**
     * One page, newest first, strictly after the cursor position. category '' means every category. Linked
     * statement halves are hidden: the manual half stands for the pair.
     */
    @Query(value = """
            select * from transactions t
            where t.user_id = :userId and t.deleted_at is null
              and not (t.source = 'statement' and t.linked_txn_id is not null)
              and t.occurred_at >= :from and t.occurred_at < :to
              and (:category = '' or t.category_code = :category)
              and (t.occurred_at < :beforeAt or (t.occurred_at = :beforeAt and t.id < :beforeId))
            order by t.occurred_at desc, t.id desc
            limit :limit
            """, nativeQuery = true)
    List<TransactionEntity> page(@Param("userId") UUID userId, @Param("from") Instant from, @Param("to") Instant to,
                                 @Param("category") String category, @Param("beforeAt") Instant beforeAt,
                                 @Param("beforeId") UUID beforeId, @Param("limit") int limit);
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/CategoryTotalsRepository.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import in.gullak.common.db.Db;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

/** Aggregate for Insights (Plan 7): spent per category over a range. */
@Repository
class CategoryTotalsRepository {
    private final JdbcClient jdbc;

    CategoryTotalsRepository(JdbcClient jdbc) {
        this.jdbc = jdbc;
    }

    List<CategoryTotal> totals(UUID userId, Instant fromInclusive, Instant toExclusive) {
        return jdbc.sql("""
                select category_code,
                       sum(case when direction = 'debit' then amount_paise else -amount_paise end) as spent,
                       count(*) filter (where direction = 'debit') as debits
                from transactions
                where user_id = ? and deleted_at is null and counts_in_budget
                  and (direction = 'debit' or credit_kind = 'refund')
                  and occurred_at >= ? and occurred_at < ?
                group by category_code
                order by spent desc, category_code
                """)
            .params(userId, Db.ts(fromInclusive), Db.ts(toExclusive))
            .query((rs, n) -> new CategoryTotal(rs.getString(1), rs.getLong(2), rs.getInt(3)))
            .list();
    }
}
```

```java
    static final int MAX_RANGE_DAYS = 400;

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public TransactionPage list(UUID userId, TransactionQuery q) {
        if (q.from().isAfter(q.to())) {
            throw new ValidationException("from", "The start date is after the end date");
        }
        if (ChronoUnit.DAYS.between(q.from(), q.to()) >= MAX_RANGE_DAYS) {
            throw new ValidationException("to", "Ask for 400 days or fewer at a time");
        }
        if (q.category() != null && !categories.exists(q.category())) {
            throw new ValidationException("category", "Pick a category");
        }
        TransactionCursor.Position from = TransactionCursor.START;
        if (q.cursor() != null) {
            Optional<TransactionCursor.Position> decoded = TransactionCursor.decode(userId, q.cursor());
            if (decoded.isEmpty()) {
                return new TransactionPage(List.of(), null);
            }
            from = decoded.get();
        }
        List<TransactionEntity> rows = repo.page(userId, Ist.startOf(q.from()), Ist.startOf(q.to().plusDays(1)),
            q.category() == null ? "" : q.category(), from.at(), from.id(), q.limit() + 1);
        boolean more = rows.size() > q.limit();
        List<TransactionEntity> page = more ? rows.subList(0, q.limit()) : rows;
        Map<UUID, Integer> held = xp.activeAwards(userId, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION,
            page.stream().map(TransactionEntity::getId).toList());
        String next = more ? TransactionCursor.encode(userId, page.getLast().getOccurredAt(), page.getLast().getId()) : null;
        return new TransactionPage(page.stream().map(e -> e.toRecord(held.getOrDefault(e.getId(), 0))).toList(), next);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<CategoryTotal> categoryTotals(UUID userId, LocalDate from, LocalDate to) {
        return totals.totals(userId, Ist.startOf(from), Ist.startOf(to.plusDays(1)));
    }
```

In `TransactionController`, add the imports `jakarta.validation.constraints.Max`, `jakarta.validation.constraints.Min`, `jakarta.validation.constraints.Size`, `java.time.LocalDate`, `org.springframework.format.annotation.DateTimeFormat`, `org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam`, and the method:

```java
    TransactionPage list(@AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt,
                         @RequestParam @DateTimeFormat(iso = DateTimeFormat.ISO.DATE) LocalDate from,
                         @RequestParam @DateTimeFormat(iso = DateTimeFormat.ISO.DATE) LocalDate to,
                         @RequestParam(required = false) @Size(max = 30) String category,
                         @RequestParam(required = false) @Size(max = 200) String cursor,
                         @RequestParam(defaultValue = "50") @Min(1) @Max(100) int limit) {
        return transactions.list(CurrentUser.id(jwt), new TransactionQuery(from, to, category, cursor, limit));
    }
```

### Task 6: Edit, delete, unlink and assign extra income

**Owner:** backend-engineer. **Test IDs:** API-SP-25 to 38, XP-03, XP-04 (through the API), API-X-01 (edit, delete, unlink, assign). **Red team:** yes (another user's ids on every verb, double deletes in parallel, XP left behind, linked pairs left half-updated, assignment rounding).

**Files:**
- Create: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/TransactionPatch.java`, `AssignIncomeRequest.java`, `CategoryCorrected.java`
- Modify: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/ingestion/TransactionEntity.java` (getters and mutators), `TransactionRepository.java` (`findLiveForUpdate`), `TransactionService.java` (`update`, `delete`, `unlink`, `assignIncome`), `TransactionController.java` (four endpoints)
- Modify: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/common/error/ErrorCode.java` (append three codes), `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/common/log/Events.java` (append `INCOME_ASSIGNED`)
- Modify: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/support/ApiTest.java` (`seedLinkedPair`, `seedStatementSpend`, `patchSpend`, `deleteSpend`)
- Modify: `app/src/lib/error-codes.ts` (regenerated), `app/src/lib/errors.ts`
- Test: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/ingestion/EditSpendApiTest.java`, `DeleteSpendApiTest.java`, `LinkApiTest.java`, `AssignIncomeApiTest.java`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TransactionService.live/withXp/clean` (Task 4), `XpService.reverse` (Task 3), `ApplicationEventPublisher`.
- Produces:
  - `PATCH /api/v1/transactions/{id}` body `{amountPaise?, category?, merchant?, note?, paymentMethod?, tag?, spreadOverCycle?}` (a missing field keeps its value; `""` clears merchant or note) → 200 `Transaction`; 409 `CONFIRMED_AMOUNT_LOCKED` when the amount of a confirmed row changes.
  - `DELETE /api/v1/transactions/{id}` → 204 (soft delete, XP reversed, a linked partner restored).
  - `POST /api/v1/transactions/{id}/unlink` → 200 `Transaction`; 409 `NOT_LINKED`.
  - `POST /api/v1/transactions/{id}/assign-income` body `{savingsPercent, poolPercent}` (whole numbers adding to 100) → 200 `Transaction`; 409 `NOT_INCOME`.
  - Java: `TransactionService.update(UUID, UUID, TransactionPatch) : Transaction`, `delete(UUID, UUID)`, `unlink(UUID, UUID) : Transaction`, `assignIncome(UUID, UUID, int savingsPercent, int poolPercent) : Transaction`; `CategoryCorrected(UUID userId, UUID transactionId, String fromCategory, String toCategory)` published when a statement-sourced row changes category (Plan 4 listens and writes the user merchant rule); `ErrorCode.CONFIRMED_AMOUNT_LOCKED`, `NOT_LINKED`, `NOT_INCOME`; `Events.INCOME_ASSIGNED = "spend.income_assigned"`.
  - Test helpers: `seedLinkedPair(user, paise, at) : String[] {manualId, statementId}`, `seedStatementSpend(user, paise, at, category) : String`, `patchSpend(user, id, json)`, `deleteSpend(user, id)`.

Add to `ApiTest` (new imports: `static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.delete`, `static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.patch`):

```java
    protected ResultActions patchSpend(TestUser user, String id, String json) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(patch("/api/v1/transactions/" + id).with(auth(user))
            .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content(json));
    }

    protected ResultActions deleteSpend(TestUser user, String id) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/transactions/" + id).with(auth(user)));
    }

    /** A statement line with no quick-add behind it (Plan 4 creates these on import). */
    protected String seedStatementSpend(TestUser user, long paise, Instant at, String category) {
        UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();
        jdbc.sql("""
                insert into transactions (id, user_id, amount_paise, direction, occurred_at, category_code,
                    payment_method, source, status)
                values (?, ?, ?, 'debit', ?, ?, 'unknown', 'statement', 'statement_only')
                """)
            .params(id, user.id(), paise, OffsetDateTime.ofInstant(at, ZoneOffset.UTC), category)
            .update();
        return id.toString();
    }

    /**
     * A quick-add confirmed by a statement line, in the link shape Plan 4 must produce: both rows point at each
     * other, both are confirmed, and the statement half does not count in the budget. Returns {manualId, statementId}.
     */
    protected String[] seedLinkedPair(TestUser user, long paise, Instant at) throws Exception {
        String manual = logSpend(user, paise, at);
        UUID statement = UUID.randomUUID();
        jdbc.sql("""
                insert into transactions (id, user_id, amount_paise, direction, occurred_at, category_code,
                    payment_method, source, status, counts_in_budget, linked_txn_id)
                values (?, ?, ?, 'debit', ?, 'eating_out', 'unknown', 'statement', 'confirmed', false, ?::uuid)
                """)
            .params(statement, user.id(), paise, OffsetDateTime.ofInstant(at, ZoneOffset.UTC), manual)
            .update();
        jdbc.sql("update transactions set status = 'confirmed', linked_txn_id = ? where id = ?::uuid")
            .params(statement, manual)
            .update();
        return new String[] {manual, statement.toString()};
    }
```

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

@RecordApplicationEvents
class EditSpendApiTest extends ApiTest {

    @Test

        patchSpend(u, id, "{\"amountPaise\":18000}")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.amountPaise").value(18_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.xpAwarded").value(5));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/budget/today").with(auth(u))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.spentPaise").value(18_000));
    }

    @Test
    void aConfirmedAmountIsLocked() throws Exception { // API-SP-26
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String manual = seedLinkedPair(u, 25_000, COFFEE_TIME)[0];
        patchSpend(u, manual, "{\"amountPaise\":24900}")
            .andExpect(status().isConflict())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("CONFIRMED_AMOUNT_LOCKED"));
        patchSpend(u, manual, "{\"amountPaise\":25000,\"category\":\"food_delivery\"}")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.category").value("food_delivery"));
    }

    @Test
    void aCategoryFixOnAStatementSpendIsAnnounced() throws Exception { // API-SP-27 (Plan 4 turns it into a rule)
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = seedStatementSpend(u, 40_000, COFFEE_TIME, "other");
        patchSpend(u, id, "{\"category\":\"eating_out\"}").andExpect(status().isOk());
        assertThat(events.stream(CategoryCorrected.class))
            .containsExactly(new CategoryCorrected(u.id(), UUID.fromString(id), "other", "eating_out"));
    }

    @Test
    void aCategoryChangeOnAQuickAddIsNotACorrection() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = logSpend(u, 15_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        patchSpend(u, id, "{\"category\":\"shopping\"}").andExpect(status().isOk());
        assertThat(events.stream(CategoryCorrected.class)).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void tagsAndTheRestOfTheFields() throws Exception { // API-SP-28, 29
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = logSpend(u, 15_000, COFFEE_TIME, ",\"merchant\":\"Third Wave\",\"note\":\"team\"");
        patchSpend(u, id, "{\"tag\":\"REGRET\",\"paymentMethod\":\"CARD\",\"merchant\":\"  Blue   Tokai \",\"spreadOverCycle\":true}")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.tag").value("REGRET"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.paymentMethod").value("CARD"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.merchant").value("Blue Tokai"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.note").value("team"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.spreadOverCycle").value(true));
        patchSpend(u, id, "{\"merchant\":\"\",\"note\":\"\"}")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.merchant").doesNotExist())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.note").doesNotExist());
        patchSpend(u, id, "{\"tag\":\"MEH\"}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("VALIDATION"));
        patchSpend(u, id, "{\"paymentMethod\":\"UNKNOWN\"}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        patchSpend(u, id, "{\"category\":\"crypto\"}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        patchSpend(u, id, "{\"amountPaise\":0}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        patchSpend(u, id, "{\"amountPaise\":1000000000}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());

    @Test
    void creditsTakeNoTagAndNoSpread() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String body = postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 30_000, COFFEE_TIME, "shopping", "CARD", ",\"kind\":\"REFUND\""))
            .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        String id = JsonPath.read(body, "$.id");
        patchSpend(u, id, "{\"tag\":\"NEED\"}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        patchSpend(u, id, "{\"spreadOverCycle\":true}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
    }

    @Test
    void changingAnAssignedIncomeAmountClearsTheSplit() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String body = postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 500_000, COFFEE_TIME, "other", "UPI", ",\"kind\":\"INCOME\""))
            .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        String id = JsonPath.read(body, "$.id");
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/transactions/" + id + "/assign-income").with(auth(u))
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"savingsPercent\":60,\"poolPercent\":40}"))
            .andExpect(status().isOk());
        patchSpend(u, id, "{\"amountPaise\":600000}")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.assignedPoolPaise").doesNotExist())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.assignedSavingsPaise").doesNotExist());
    }

    @Test
    void anotherUsersOrADeletedSpendIsNotFound() throws Exception { // API-SP-30
        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();
        String id = logSpend(a, 15_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        patchSpend(b, id, "{\"amountPaise\":1}").andExpect(status().isNotFound()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_FOUND"));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/transactions/" + id).with(auth(a))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.amountPaise").value(15_000));
        deleteSpend(a, id).andExpect(status().isNoContent());
        patchSpend(a, id, "{\"amountPaise\":1}").andExpect(status().isNotFound());
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import in.gullak.gamification.XpService;
import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.Callable;
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.Future;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;

class DeleteSpendApiTest extends ApiTest {
    @Autowired XpService xp;

    private String row(String id, String column) {
        return jdbc.sql("select " + column + "::text from transactions where id = ?::uuid").param(id).query(String.class).optional().orElse(null);
    }

    @Test
    void deleteIsSoftAndTakesTheXpBack() throws Exception { // API-SP-31, XP-03
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = logSpend(u, 15_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        assertThat(xp.total(u.id())).isEqualTo(5L);
        deleteSpend(u, id).andExpect(status().isNoContent());
        assertThat(row(id, "deleted_at")).isNotNull();
        assertThat(xp.total(u.id())).isZero();
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/budget/today").with(auth(u))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.spentPaise").value(0));
    }

    @Test
    void deletingTwiceIsNotFound() throws Exception { // API-SP-32
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = logSpend(u, 15_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        deleteSpend(u, id).andExpect(status().isNoContent());
        deleteSpend(u, id).andExpect(status().isNotFound()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_FOUND"));
    }

    @Test
    void deletingTheEleventhSpendTakesNothingBack() throws Exception { // XP-04
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String eleventh = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < 11; i++) {
            eleventh = logSpend(u, 1_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        }
        deleteSpend(u, eleventh).andExpect(status().isNoContent());
        assertThat(xp.total(u.id())).isEqualTo(50L);
    }

    @Test
    void anotherUserCannotDelete() throws Exception { // API-X-01
        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();
        String id = logSpend(a, 15_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        deleteSpend(b, id).andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        assertThat(row(id, "deleted_at")).isNull();
        assertThat(xp.total(a.id())).isEqualTo(5L);
    }

    @Test
    void deletingTheManualHalfOfALinkKeepsTheBankLine() throws Exception { // API-SP-33
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String[] pair = seedLinkedPair(u, 25_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/budget/today").with(auth(u))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.spentPaise").value(25_000)); // counted once
        deleteSpend(u, pair[0]).andExpect(status().isNoContent());
        assertThat(row(pair[1], "status")).isEqualTo("statement_only");
        assertThat(row(pair[1], "counts_in_budget")).isEqualTo("true");
        assertThat(row(pair[1], "linked_txn_id")).isNull();
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/budget/today").with(auth(u))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.spentPaise").value(25_000)); // now from the line
    }

    @Test
    void deletingTheBankHalfReturnsTheQuickAddToPending() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String[] pair = seedLinkedPair(u, 25_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        deleteSpend(u, pair[1]).andExpect(status().isNoContent());
        assertThat(row(pair[0], "status")).isEqualTo("pending");
        assertThat(row(pair[0], "linked_txn_id")).isNull();
    }

    @Test
    void parallelDeletesSucceedOnceAndReverseOnce() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = logSpend(u, 15_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(3);
        try {
            CountDownLatch start = new CountDownLatch(1);
            List<Future<Integer>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                Callable<Integer> call = () -> {
                    start.await();
                    return deleteSpend(u, id).andReturn().getResponse().getStatus();
                };
                futures.add(pool.submit(call));
            }
            start.countDown();
            List<Integer> statuses = new ArrayList<>();
            for (Future<Integer> f : futures) {
                statuses.add(f.get());
            }
            assertThat(statuses).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(204, 404, 404);
            assertThat(jdbc.sql("select count(*) from xp_events where ref_id = ? and reversed_at is not null")
                .param(UUID.fromString(id)).query(Long.class).single()).isEqualTo(1L);
        } finally {
            pool.shutdownNow();
        }
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.ResultActions;

class LinkApiTest extends ApiTest {

    private ResultActions unlink(TestUser u, String id) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/transactions/" + id + "/unlink").with(auth(u)));
    }

    private String row(String id, String column) {
        return jdbc.sql("select " + column + "::text from transactions where id = ?::uuid").param(id).query(String.class).optional().orElse(null);
    }

    @Test
    void unlinkingSplitsThePairBack() throws Exception { // API-SP-34, MAT-12 shape
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String[] pair = seedLinkedPair(u, 25_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        unlink(u, pair[0])
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value("PENDING"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.linked").value(false));
        assertThat(row(pair[1], "status")).isEqualTo("statement_only");
        assertThat(row(pair[1], "counts_in_budget")).isEqualTo("true");
        assertThat(row(pair[1], "linked_txn_id")).isNull();
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/budget/today").with(auth(u))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.spentPaise").value(50_000)); // two records now
    }

    @Test
    void unlinkWorksFromTheBankHalfToo() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String[] pair = seedLinkedPair(u, 25_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        unlink(u, pair[1]).andExpect(status().isOk()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value("STATEMENT_ONLY"));
        assertThat(row(pair[0], "status")).isEqualTo("pending");
    }

    @Test
    void unlinkingSomethingUnlinkedConflicts() throws Exception { // API-SP-35
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = logSpend(u, 15_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        unlink(u, id).andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_LINKED"));
    }

    @Test
    void anotherUserCannotUnlink() throws Exception {
        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();
        String[] pair = seedLinkedPair(a, 25_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        unlink(b, pair[0]).andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        assertThat(row(pair[0], "status")).isEqualTo("confirmed");
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.ResultActions;

class AssignIncomeApiTest extends ApiTest {

    private String income(TestUser u, long paise) throws Exception {
        String body = postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), paise, COFFEE_TIME, "other", "UPI", ",\"kind\":\"INCOME\""))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated()).andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        return JsonPath.read(body, "$.id");
    }

    private ResultActions assign(TestUser u, String id, String json) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/transactions/" + id + "/assign-income").with(auth(u))
            .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content(json));
    }

    @Test
    void splitsByPercent() throws Exception { // API-SP-36, BUD-14 amounts
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = income(u, 500_000);
        assign(u, id, "{\"savingsPercent\":60,\"poolPercent\":40}")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.assignedSavingsPaise").value(300_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.assignedPoolPaise").value(200_000));
        assign(u, id, "{\"savingsPercent\":0,\"poolPercent\":100}")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.assignedSavingsPaise").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.assignedPoolPaise").value(500_000));
    }

    @Test
    void savingsRoundDownAndThePoolGetsTheRest() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = income(u, 333);
        assign(u, id, "{\"savingsPercent\":50,\"poolPercent\":50}")
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.assignedSavingsPaise").value(166))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.assignedPoolPaise").value(167));
    }

    @Test
    void onlyIncomeCanBeAssigned() throws Exception { // API-SP-37
        TestUser u = exampleUser();

            .andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_INCOME"));
        String refundBody = postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), 30_000, COFFEE_TIME, "shopping", "CARD", ",\"kind\":\"REFUND\""))
            .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();

            .andExpect(status().isConflict());

    void theSplitMustAddUpTo100() throws Exception { // API-SP-38
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = income(u, 500_000);
        assign(u, id, "{\"savingsPercent\":60,\"poolPercent\":50}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("VALIDATION"));
        assign(u, id, "{\"savingsPercent\":-1,\"poolPercent\":101}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        assign(u, id, "{\"savingsPercent\":60}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        assign(u, id, "{\"savingsPercent\":59.5,\"poolPercent\":40.5}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());

        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();

        assign(b, id, "{\"savingsPercent\":0,\"poolPercent\":100}").andExpect(status().isNotFound());
```

```java
package in.gullak.ingestion;

import in.gullak.common.money.Money;
import in.gullak.common.web.SingleLineText;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Max;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Pattern;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Positive;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;

/** Every field optional: null keeps the current value; "" clears merchant or note. */
public record TransactionPatch(
    @Positive @Max(Money.MAX_AMOUNT_PAISE) Long amountPaise,
    @Size(max = 30) @Pattern(regexp = "[a-z_]+", message = "Pick a category") String category,
    @Size(max = 80) @SingleLineText String merchant,
    @Size(max = 140) @SingleLineText String note,
    PaymentMethod paymentMethod,
    SpendTag tag,
    Boolean spreadOverCycle) {}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Entity mutators and the locking read**

In `TransactionEntity`, add these getters and mutators:

```java
    String getMerchantName() { return merchantName; }
    String getNote() { return note; }
    String getPaymentMethodDb() { return paymentMethod; }
    String getTagDb() { return tag; }
    boolean isSpreadOverCycle() { return spreadOverCycle; }

    /** Applies an edit. An income credit whose amount changes loses its split: the user assigns it again. */
    void edit(long newAmount, String newCategory, String newMerchant, String newNote, String newPaymentMethod,
              String newTag, boolean newSpread) {
        if (newAmount != amountPaise && "income".equals(creditKind)) {
            assignedSavingsPaise = null;
            assignedPoolPaise = null;
        }
        amountPaise = newAmount;
        categoryCode = newCategory;
        merchantName = newMerchant;
        note = newNote;
        paymentMethod = newPaymentMethod;
        tag = newTag;
        spreadOverCycle = newSpread;
    }

    void softDelete(Instant at) {
        deletedAt = at;
        linkedTxnId = null;
    }

    /** Spec 2.5 / MAT-12: the manual half goes back to pending; the statement half stands alone and counts again. */
    void restoreAfterUnlink() {
        linkedTxnId = null;
        if (source() == TransactionSource.STATEMENT) {
            status = TransactionStatus.STATEMENT_ONLY.db();
            countsInBudget = true;
        } else {
            status = TransactionStatus.PENDING.db();
        }
    }

    void assign(long savingsPaise, long poolPaise) {
        assignedSavingsPaise = savingsPaise;
        assignedPoolPaise = poolPaise;
    }
```

Add to `TransactionRepository` (imports `jakarta.persistence.LockModeType`, `org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Lock`):

```java
    /** Row lock for every write: two deletes, or a delete racing an edit, queue here instead of both succeeding. */
    @Lock(LockModeType.PESSIMISTIC_WRITE)
    @Query("select t from TransactionEntity t where t.id = :id and t.userId = :userId and t.deletedAt is null")
    Optional<TransactionEntity> findLiveForUpdate(@Param("id") UUID id, @Param("userId") UUID userId);
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Service methods**

In `TransactionService`, add the constructor parameter `ApplicationEventPublisher events` (import `org.springframework.context.ApplicationEventPublisher`; the constructor becomes `TransactionService(TransactionRepository repo, TransactionInsertRepository inserts, CategoryTotalsRepository totals, CategoryService categories, XpService xp, ApplicationEventPublisher events, Clock clock)`), and add:

```java
    @Transactional
    public Transaction update(UUID userId, UUID id, TransactionPatch p) {
        TransactionEntity t = repo.findLiveForUpdate(id, userId).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Spend"));
        TransactionKind kind = t.kind();
        if (p.amountPaise() != null && p.amountPaise() != t.getAmountPaise() && t.status() == TransactionStatus.CONFIRMED) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.CONFIRMED_AMOUNT_LOCKED, "Your bank confirmed this amount, so it can't be changed");
        }
        if (p.category() != null && !categories.exists(p.category())) {
            throw new ValidationException("category", "Pick a category");
        }
        if (p.paymentMethod() == PaymentMethod.UNKNOWN) {
            throw new ValidationException("paymentMethod", "Pick UPI, card or cash");
        }
        if (p.tag() != null && kind != TransactionKind.SPEND) {
            throw new ValidationException("tag", "Only a spend can be tagged");
        }
        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(p.spreadOverCycle()) && kind != TransactionKind.SPEND) {
            throw new ValidationException("spreadOverCycle", "Only a spend can be spread over the cycle");
        }
        String before = t.getCategoryCode();
        t.edit(
            p.amountPaise() != null ? p.amountPaise() : t.getAmountPaise(),
            p.category() != null ? p.category() : before,
            p.merchant() != null ? clean(p.merchant()) : t.getMerchantName(),
            p.note() != null ? clean(p.note()) : t.getNote(),
            p.paymentMethod() != null ? p.paymentMethod().db() : t.getPaymentMethodDb(),
            p.tag() != null ? p.tag().db() : t.getTagDb(),
            p.spreadOverCycle() != null ? p.spreadOverCycle() : t.isSpreadOverCycle());
        if (!before.equals(t.getCategoryCode()) && t.source() == TransactionSource.STATEMENT) {
            events.publishEvent(new CategoryCorrected(userId, id, before, t.getCategoryCode()));
        }
        log.info("{} user={} id={}", Events.SPEND_UPDATED, userId, id);
        return withXp(userId, t);
    }

    @Transactional
    public void delete(UUID userId, UUID id) {
        TransactionEntity t = repo.findLiveForUpdate(id, userId).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Spend"));
        UUID partner = t.getLinkedTxnId();
        t.softDelete(clock.instant());
        if (partner != null) {
            repo.findLiveForUpdate(partner, userId).ifPresent(TransactionEntity::restoreAfterUnlink);
        }
        int back = xp.reverse(userId, XpService.REF_TRANSACTION, id);
        log.info("{} user={} id={} xpReversed={}", Events.SPEND_DELETED, userId, id, back);
    }

    @Transactional
    public Transaction unlink(UUID userId, UUID id) {
        TransactionEntity t = repo.findLiveForUpdate(id, userId).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Spend"));
        UUID partner = t.getLinkedTxnId();
        if (partner == null) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.NOT_LINKED, "This spend isn't linked to a bank line");
        }
        t.restoreAfterUnlink();
        repo.findLiveForUpdate(partner, userId).ifPresent(TransactionEntity::restoreAfterUnlink);
        log.info("{} user={} id={} other={}", Events.SPEND_UNLINKED, userId, id, partner);
        return withXp(userId, t);
    }

    @Transactional
    public Transaction assignIncome(UUID userId, UUID id, int savingsPercent, int poolPercent) {
        if (savingsPercent + poolPercent != 100) {
            throw new ValidationException("poolPercent", "The two parts must add up to 100%");
        }
        TransactionEntity t = repo.findLiveForUpdate(id, userId).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Spend"));
        if (t.kind() != TransactionKind.INCOME) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.NOT_INCOME, "Only money coming in can be assigned");
        }
        long savings = t.getAmountPaise() * savingsPercent / 100;
        t.assign(savings, t.getAmountPaise() - savings);
        log.info("{} user={} id={} savingsPercent={}", Events.INCOME_ASSIGNED, userId, id, savingsPercent);
        return withXp(userId, t);
    }
```

Locking order: a write locks the row it was asked about, then its partner. Two requests on the two halves of one pair at the same instant can deadlock; Postgres aborts one, which answers 500 and is safe to retry. That needs one person editing both halves of a pair in the same millisecond, so it is accepted here and noted for Plan 4's review.

### Task 7: Spread spends, refunds and extra income in the budget

**Owner:** backend-engineer. **Test IDs:** BUD-11, BUD-12, BUD-13, BUD-14, BUD-19, BUD-29, API-SP-13 (budget half), API-SP-15 (budget half). **Red team:** yes (money: a refund or income that inflates the budget, another user's credits, spread on credits).

`SpendTotalsRepository.spent(...)` left `spread_over_cycle` rows out everywhere, so a spread flight never reduced the pool. BUD-11 needs it in the pool (from the day it happens) but out of today's spent figure. This task replaces the two `spent` calls with one aggregate that returns every figure the budget needs from one snapshot.

**Files:**
- Create: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/budget/SpendTotals.java`
- Modify: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/budget/SpendTotalsRepository.java` (whole file), `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/budget/BudgetService.java` (`compute`)
- Test: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/budget/SpendTotalsTest.java`, `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/budget/BudgetSpendsApiTest.java`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `BudgetService.compute` and `CurrentCycle` as Plan 2 Task 5 left them; `BudgetInput`, `BudgetInput.ofSpendable`, `BudgetCalculator.compute`; the `transactions` columns from Task 2.
- Produces: `SpendTotals(long debitsBefore, long refundsBefore, long spreadThroughToday, long debitsToday, long refundsToday, long extraPoolPaise)` with `spentToday()` and `spentBeforeToday()`; `SpendTotalsRepository.totals(UUID userId, Instant cycleStart, Instant todayStart, Instant tomorrowStart) : SpendTotals`. `GET /budget/today` keeps its shape. Rules, for Plan 6's daily close and Plan 7's cycle close (they must ask `BudgetService` rather than re-derive them):
  - today's spent = today's non-spread debits − today's refunds, never below 0;
  - spent before today = earlier non-spread debits − earlier refunds + every spread debit up to the end of today − the part of today's refunds that today's spends could not absorb;
  - spendable = income + assigned pool share of income credits in the cycle up to today − fixed costs − savings (or the first cycle's override + that share).

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class SpendTotalsTest {

    @Test
    void plainSpends() {
        SpendTotals t = new SpendTotals(966_000, 0, 0, 18_000, 0, 0);
        assertThat(t.spentBeforeToday()).isEqualTo(966_000);
        assertThat(t.spentToday()).isEqualTo(18_000);

        assertThat(t.spentToday()).isEqualTo(8_000);
        assertThat(t.spentBeforeToday()).isEqualTo(966_000);
    }

    @Test
    void aRefundBiggerThanTodayGoesBackToThePool() {
        SpendTotals t = new SpendTotals(966_000, 0, 0, 18_000, 30_000, 0);
        assertThat(t.spentToday()).isZero();
        assertThat(t.spentBeforeToday()).isEqualTo(954_000);

}
```

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.ResultActions;

class BudgetSpendsApiTest extends ApiTest {
    static final Instant EARLIER_THIS_CYCLE = Instant.parse("2026-10-10T06:00:00Z");
    static final Instant YESTERDAY = Instant.parse("2026-10-22T10:00:00Z");
    static final Instant TOMORROW = Instant.parse("2026-10-24T03:00:00Z");

    private ResultActions today(TestUser u) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/budget/today").with(auth(u)));
    }

    private String credit(TestUser u, String kind, long paise, Instant at) throws Exception {
        String body = postSpend(u, spendJson(UUID.randomUUID(), paise, at, "other", "UPI", ",\"kind\":\"" + kind + "\""))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated()).andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        return JsonPath.read(body, "$.id");
    }

    private void assign(TestUser u, String id, int savings, int pool) throws Exception {
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/transactions/" + id + "/assign-income").with(auth(u)).contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("{\"savingsPercent\":" + savings + ",\"poolPercent\":" + pool + "}"))
            .andExpect(status().isOk());
    }

    @Test
    void aSpreadSpendShrinksThePoolButNotToday() throws Exception { // BUD-11, API-SP-13
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 966_000, EARLIER_THIS_CYCLE);
        logSpend(u, 300_000, COFFEE_TIME, ",\"spreadOverCycle\":true");
        today(u)
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(26_000)) // (15,000 − 9,660 − 3,000) ÷ 9
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.spentPaise").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.leftPaise").value(26_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.mood").value("HAPPY"));
    }

    @Test
    void theSameSpendNotSpreadFaintsTheGullak() throws Exception { // BUD-11 contrast
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 966_000, EARLIER_THIS_CYCLE);
        logSpend(u, 300_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        today(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(59_300)).andExpect(jsonPath("$.mood").value("FAINTED"));
    }

    @Test
    void aFlightTheSizeOfTheWholeCycleLeavesNothingButDoesNotFaint() throws Exception { // BUD-11, the spec's ₹15,000
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        logSpend(u, 1_500_000, COFFEE_TIME, ",\"spreadOverCycle\":true");
        today(u)
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.spentPaise").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.overPaise").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.poolOverPaise").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.mood").value("NERVOUS"));
    }

    @Test
    void spreadSpendsEarlierCountLikeAnyEarlierSpendAndTomorrowsWait() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        logSpend(u, 300_000, EARLIER_THIS_CYCLE, ",\"spreadOverCycle\":true");
        today(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(133_300)); // (15,000 − 3,000) ÷ 9
        TestUser v = exampleUser();
        logSpend(v, 300_000, TOMORROW, ",\"spreadOverCycle\":true");
        today(v).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(166_600));
    }

    @Test
    void anEarlierRefundRaisesTodaysBudget() throws Exception { // BUD-12
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 966_000, EARLIER_THIS_CYCLE);
        credit(u, "REFUND", 30_000, YESTERDAY);
        today(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(62_600)); // (15,000 − 9,660 + 300) ÷ 9
    }

    @Test
    void aRefundTodayLowersTodaysSpent() throws Exception { // BUD-12
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 966_000, EARLIER_THIS_CYCLE);
        logSpend(u, 18_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        credit(u, "REFUND", 10_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        today(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(59_300)).andExpect(jsonPath("$.spentPaise").value(8_000));
    }

    @Test
    void refundBiggerThanTodaysSpendsGoesBackToThePool() throws Exception { // Review Focus
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 966_000, EARLIER_THIS_CYCLE);
        logSpend(u, 18_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        credit(u, "REFUND", 30_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        today(u)
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.spentPaise").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(60_600)); // (15,000 − 9,660 + 120) ÷ 9

    @Test

        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String bonus = credit(u, "INCOME", 500_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        today(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(166_600)); // unassigned: no change

    }

    void aDeletedSpendLeavesTheBudgetAtOnce() throws Exception { // BUD-19
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = logSpend(u, 15_000, COFFEE_TIME);
        today(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.spentPaise").value(15_000));
        deleteSpend(u, id).andExpect(status().isNoContent());

    @Test
    void aSpendInAnEarlierCycleChangesThatHistoryNotToday() throws Exception { // BUD-29, API-SP-15
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = logSpend(u, 120_000, Instant.parse("2026-09-20T05:00:00Z"));
        today(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(166_600)).andExpect(jsonPath("$.spentPaise").value(0));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/transactions?from=2026-09-20&to=2026-09-20").with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].id").value(id));

    @Test

        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();
        assign(b, credit(b, "INCOME", 500_000, COFFEE_TIME), 0, 100);
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The totals**

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/budget/SpendTotals.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

/**
 * The cycle's money so far, from one query (spec 2.1). Debits counted here count in the budget; spread debits
 * leave today's figure and join the pool from the day they happen; refunds today lower today's spent, and what
 * today cannot absorb goes back to the pool; {@code extraPoolPaise} is the pool share of assigned income.
 */
record SpendTotals(
    long debitsBefore,
    long refundsBefore,
    long spreadThroughToday,
    long debitsToday,
    long refundsToday,
    long extraPoolPaise) {

    long spentToday() {
        return Math.max(0, debitsToday - refundsToday);
    }

    long spentBeforeToday() {
        long unabsorbedRefund = Math.max(0, refundsToday - debitsToday);
        return debitsBefore - refundsBefore + spreadThroughToday - unabsorbedRefund;
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/budget/SpendTotalsRepository.java` (whole file):

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

import in.gullak.common.db.Db;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.OffsetDateTime;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

/** Read-only totals over the transactions table for the budget, all from one snapshot. */
@Repository
class SpendTotalsRepository {
    private final JdbcClient jdbc;

    SpendTotalsRepository(JdbcClient jdbc) {
        this.jdbc = jdbc;
    }

    SpendTotals totals(UUID userId, Instant cycleStart, Instant todayStart, Instant tomorrowStart) {
        OffsetDateTime today = Db.ts(todayStart);
        return jdbc.sql("""
                select
                  coalesce(sum(amount_paise) filter (where direction = 'debit' and counts_in_budget
                      and not spread_over_cycle and occurred_at < ?), 0),
                  coalesce(sum(amount_paise) filter (where credit_kind = 'refund' and counts_in_budget
                      and occurred_at < ?), 0),
                  coalesce(sum(amount_paise) filter (where direction = 'debit' and counts_in_budget
                      and spread_over_cycle), 0),
                  coalesce(sum(amount_paise) filter (where direction = 'debit' and counts_in_budget
                      and not spread_over_cycle and occurred_at >= ?), 0),
                  coalesce(sum(amount_paise) filter (where credit_kind = 'refund' and counts_in_budget
                      and occurred_at >= ?), 0),
                  coalesce(sum(assigned_pool_paise) filter (where credit_kind = 'income'), 0)
                from transactions
                where user_id = ? and deleted_at is null and occurred_at >= ? and occurred_at < ?
                """)
            .params(today, today, today, today, userId, Db.ts(cycleStart), Db.ts(tomorrowStart))
            .query((rs, n) -> new SpendTotals(rs.getLong(1), rs.getLong(2), rs.getLong(3), rs.getLong(4), rs.getLong(5), rs.getLong(6)))
            .single();
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Use them in the budget**

In `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/budget/BudgetService.java`, replace `compute` with:

```java
    private TodayResponse compute(UUID userId, Profile profile, LocalDate today, long fixed, CurrentCycle current) {
        Cycle cycle = current.cycle();
        SpendTotals t = totals.totals(userId, Ist.startOf(cycle.start()), Ist.startOf(today), Ist.startOf(today.plusDays(1)));
        int daysLeft = (int) ChronoUnit.DAYS.between(today, cycle.end()) + 1;
        BudgetInput input = current.poolOverridePaise() != null
            ? BudgetInput.ofSpendable(current.poolOverridePaise() + t.extraPoolPaise(), t.spentBeforeToday(), t.spentToday(), daysLeft)
            : new BudgetInput(profile.incomePaise() + t.extraPoolPaise(), fixed, profile.savingsPaise(),
                t.spentBeforeToday(), t.spentToday(), daysLeft);
        DailyBudget b = BudgetCalculator.compute(input);
        return new TodayResponse(today, b.budgetPaise(), b.spentTodayPaise(), b.leftPaise(), b.overPaise(),
            b.poolOverPaise(), b.mood(), GullakLines.line(profile.personality(), b.mood(), b.leftPaise(), b.totalOverPaise()), daysLeft,
            cycle.start(), cycle.end());
    }
```

Adding the extra pool share to income is the spec's "spendable = income − fixed − savings" with the bonus counted as income; `BudgetCalculator` stays unchanged.

`app/src/lib/queue.test.ts`:

```ts
import { ApiError, NetworkError } from "./api-errors";
import { backoffMs, createQueue, failureMessage, memoryStore, type QueueStore } from "./queue";

type Spend = { clientId: string; amountPaise: number };

function harness(send: (p: Spend) => Promise<unknown>, store: QueueStore<Spend> = memoryStore<Spend>()) {
  let now = 1_000_000;
  const timers: { fn: () => void; ms: number; live: boolean }[] = [];
  const sent: Spend[] = [];
  const onSent = jest.fn();
  const onFailed = jest.fn();
  const queue = createQueue<Spend, unknown>({
    store,
    send: (p) => {
      sent.push(p);
      return send(p);
    },
    onSent,
    onFailed,
    now: () => now,
    setTimer: (fn, ms) => {
      const t = { fn, ms, live: true };
      timers.push(t);
      return () => void (t.live = false);
    },
  });
  return {
    queue,
    store,
    sent,
    onSent,
    onFailed,
    timers,
    advance: (ms: number) => void (now += ms),
    lastTimer: () => timers.filter((t) => t.live).at(-1),
  };
}

const never = () => new Promise<never>(() => undefined);

describe("offline queue (APP-09)", () => {
  it("writes the spend to the store before add resolves, even when the send never answers", async () => {
    const h = harness(never);
    await h.queue.add("c1", { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 25000 });
    expect((await h.store.all()).map((i) => i.id)).toEqual(["c1"]);
    expect(h.queue.items()).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("sends once, removes the item and reports it", async () => {
    const h = harness(() => Promise.resolve({ id: "server-1" }));
    await h.queue.add("c1", { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 25000 });
    await h.queue.flush();
    expect(h.sent).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(h.queue.items()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(await h.store.all()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(h.onSent).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ id: "c1" }), { id: "server-1" });
  });

  it("keeps a spend through a network error and retries it with backoff, then sends it once", async () => {
    let online = false;
    const h = harness(() => (online ? Promise.resolve({ id: "s" }) : Promise.reject(new NetworkError("offline"))));
    await h.queue.add("c1", { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 25000 });
    await h.queue.flush();
    expect(h.queue.items()[0]).toMatchObject({ state: "pending", attempts: 1 });
    expect(h.lastTimer()?.ms).toBe(2_000);
    online = true;
    h.advance(2_000);
    h.lastTimer()?.fn();
    await h.queue.flush();
    expect(h.queue.items()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(h.onSent).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("backs off 2 s, 4 s, 8 s … and never more than 5 minutes", () => {
    expect([1, 2, 3, 4, 8, 9, 20].map(backoffMs)).toEqual([2_000, 4_000, 8_000, 16_000, 256_000, 300_000, 300_000]);
  });

  it("treats 429, 408 and 5xx as try-again-later", async () => {
    for (const status of [429, 408, 500, 503]) {
      const h = harness(() => Promise.reject(new ApiError("X", status, "")));
      await h.queue.add("c1", { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 100 });
      await h.queue.flush();
      expect(h.queue.items()[0]?.state).toBe("pending");
      expect(h.onFailed).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    }
  });

  it("after one network failure the rest of the backlog waits instead of hammering", async () => {
    const store = memoryStore<Spend>();
    for (const [i, id] of ["c1", "c2", "c3"].entries()) {
      await store.insert({ id, payload: { clientId: id, amountPaise: 100 }, state: "pending", attempts: 0, nextAttemptAt: 0, error: null, createdAt: i });
    }
    const h = harness(() => Promise.reject(new NetworkError("offline")), store);
    await h.queue.ready;
    await h.queue.flush();
    expect(h.sent.map((p) => p.clientId)).toEqual(["c1"]);
    expect(h.lastTimer()?.ms).toBe(2_000);
  });

  it("an explicit flush (network back, app opened) tries at once; the timer respects the backoff", async () => {
    let online = false;
    const h = harness(() => (online ? Promise.resolve({}) : Promise.reject(new NetworkError("offline"))));
    await h.queue.add("c1", { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 100 });
    await h.queue.flush();
    expect(h.sent).toHaveLength(1);
    online = true;
    await h.queue.flush();
    expect(h.sent).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(h.queue.items()).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("sends the oldest first", async () => {
    const h = harness(() => Promise.resolve({}));
    const order: string[] = [];
    h.onSent.mockImplementation((item: { id: string }) => order.push(item.id));
    await h.queue.add("a", { clientId: "a", amountPaise: 1 });
    await h.queue.add("b", { clientId: "b", amountPaise: 2 });
    await h.queue.flush();
    expect(order).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
  });
});

describe("rejected spends (ERR-17)", () => {
  it("a 4xx marks the spend failed with a reason and never retries it", async () => {
    const h = harness(() => Promise.reject(new ApiError("CLIENT_ID_CONFLICT", 409, "server text")));
    await h.queue.add("c1", { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 25000 });
    await h.queue.flush();
    expect(h.queue.items()[0]).toMatchObject({ state: "failed", error: "This spend was already saved with different details" });
    expect(h.onFailed).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await h.queue.flush();
    expect(h.sent).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(h.timers.filter((t) => t.live)).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("a validation failure gets a plain reason, never the server's text", () => {
    expect(failureMessage(new ApiError("VALIDATION", 400, "occurredAt: That time is in the future"))).toBe(
      "Check the date and amount, then add it again",
    );
  });

  it("a failed spend can be removed", async () => {
    const h = harness(() => Promise.reject(new ApiError("VALIDATION", 400, "")));
    await h.queue.add("c1", { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 25000 });
    await h.queue.flush();
    await h.queue.remove("c1");
    expect(h.queue.items()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(await h.store.all()).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

describe("dedupe across restarts (APP-10)", () => {
  it("a restart mid-sync resends the same clientId, so the server keeps one spend", async () => {
    const server = new Map<string, Spend>();
    const store = memoryStore<Spend>();
    const first = harness(never, store); // the app dies while this POST is in flight
    await first.queue.add("c1", { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 25000 });
    server.set("c1", { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 25000 }); // the server got it; the answer was lost

    const second = harness((p) => {
      server.set(p.clientId, p);
      return Promise.resolve({});
    }, store);
    await second.queue.ready;
    await second.queue.flush();
    expect(first.sent.map((p) => p.clientId)).toEqual(["c1"]);
    expect(second.sent.map((p) => p.clientId)).toEqual(["c1"]);
    expect(server.size).toBe(1);
    expect(await store.all()).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("adding the same id twice keeps one item", async () => {
    const h = harness(never);
    await h.queue.add("c1", { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 1 });
    await h.queue.add("c1", { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 1 });
    expect(h.queue.items()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(await h.store.all()).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

describe("clear", () => {
  it("drops everything, and a send that was in flight cannot bring its item back", async () => {
    let answer: (v: unknown) => void = () => undefined;
    const h = harness(() => new Promise((resolve) => (answer = resolve)));
    await h.queue.add("c1", { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 1 });
    await h.queue.clear();
    answer({});
    await h.queue.flush();
    expect(h.queue.items()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(h.onSent).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("notifies subscribers and keeps the same snapshot between changes", async () => {
    const h = harness(never);
    const listener = jest.fn();
    h.queue.subscribe(listener);
    const empty = h.queue.items();
    expect(h.queue.items()).toBe(empty);
    await h.queue.add("c1", { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 1 });
    expect(listener).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(h.queue.items()).not.toBe(empty);
  });
});
```

`app/src/lib/queue-store.test.ts`:

```ts
import { rowToItem, sqliteStore, type SqlDb } from "./queue-store";

function fakeDb() {
  const calls: { sql: string; params: unknown[] }[] = [];
  const db = {
    execAsync: jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve()),
    runAsync: jest.fn((sql: string, params: unknown[]) => {
      calls.push({ sql, params });
      return Promise.resolve({ lastInsertRowId: 1, changes: sql.startsWith("INSERT") ? 1 : 0 });
    }),
    getAllAsync: jest.fn(() =>
      Promise.resolve([
        { id: "c1", payload: '{"clientId":"c1","amountPaise":25000}', state: "pending", attempts: 0, next_attempt_at: 5, error: null, created_at: 1 },
        { id: "bad", payload: "{not json", state: "pending", attempts: 0, next_attempt_at: 5, error: null, created_at: 2 },
      ]),
    ),
  };
  return { db: db as unknown as SqlDb, calls, raw: db };
}

describe("SQLite outbox store", () => {
  it("creates its table once and inserts with INSERT OR IGNORE so a second add of one id is a no-op", async () => {
    const f = fakeDb();
    const store = sqliteStore<{ clientId: string }>("t.db", () => Promise.resolve(f.db));
    const inserted = await store.insert({ id: "c1", payload: { clientId: "c1" }, state: "pending", attempts: 0, nextAttemptAt: 5, error: null, createdAt: 1 });
    await store.remove("c1");
    expect(inserted).toBe(true);
    expect(f.raw.execAsync).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(f.calls[0]?.sql).toMatch(/^INSERT OR IGNORE INTO outbox/);
    expect(f.calls[0]?.params).toEqual(["c1", '{"clientId":"c1"}', "pending", 0, 5, null, 1]);
    expect(f.calls[1]).toEqual({ sql: "DELETE FROM outbox WHERE id = ?", params: ["c1"] });
  });

  it("reads rows back and skips one it cannot parse", async () => {
    const f = fakeDb();
    const store = sqliteStore<{ clientId: string; amountPaise: number }>("t.db", () => Promise.resolve(f.db));
    const items = await store.all();
    expect(items).toEqual([{ id: "c1", payload: { clientId: "c1", amountPaise: 25000 }, state: "pending", attempts: 0, nextAttemptAt: 5, error: null, createdAt: 1 }]);
  });

  it("rowToItem keeps a failed state and its reason", () => {
    expect(rowToItem({ id: "x", payload: "{}", state: "failed", attempts: 1, next_attempt_at: 0, error: "No", created_at: 0 })).toMatchObject({ state: "failed", error: "No" });
  });
});
```
