`app/src/features/spends/outbox.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider, onlineManager } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { act, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import React from "react";
import { Text } from "react-native";
import { ApiError, NetworkError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { createQueue, memoryStore, type Queue } from "@/lib/queue";
import type { SessionStatus } from "@/features/auth/session";
import { todayKey } from "@/features/budget/hooks";
import type { NewSpend } from "./api";
import { OutboxProvider, createSpendQueue, useOutboxItems } from "./outbox";

jest.mock("./api", () => ({ spendsApi: { create: jest.fn() } }));
import { spendsApi } from "./api";
const create = spendsApi.create as jest.Mock;

const spend = (clientId: string): NewSpend => ({
  clientId,
  kind: "SPEND",
  amountPaise: 25000,
  occurredAt: "2026-10-23T03:42:00.000Z",
  category: "eating_out",
  paymentMethod: "UPI",
  spreadOverCycle: false,
});

function Count() {
  const items = useOutboxItems();
  return <Text testID="count">{String(items.length)}</Text>;
}

function mount(queue: Queue<NewSpend>, status: SessionStatus) {
  const qc = new QueryClient();
  const ui = (s: SessionStatus) => (
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <OutboxProvider status={s} queue={queue}>
        <Count />
      </OutboxProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
  const r = render(ui(status));
  return { qc, setStatus: (s: SessionStatus) => r.rerender(ui(s)) };
}

beforeEach(() => {
  create.mockReset();
  onlineManager.setOnline(true);
});

describe("outbox provider", () => {
  it("signOutClearsTheQueue: spends waiting at sign-out are never sent for the next account", async () => {
    const store = memoryStore<NewSpend>();
    const send = jest.fn((): Promise<unknown> => Promise.reject(new NetworkError("offline")));
    const queue = createQueue<NewSpend, unknown>({ store, send });
    const view = mount(queue, "signedIn");
    await act(() => queue.add("c1", spend("c1")));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId("count")).toHaveTextContent("1"));

    view.setStatus("signedOut");
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId("count")).toHaveTextContent("0"));
    expect(await store.all()).toHaveLength(0);

    send.mockClear();
    send.mockImplementation(() => Promise.resolve({}));
    view.setStatus("signedIn"); // someone else signs in on this phone
    await act(() => queue.flush());
    expect(send).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("resumes sending after sign-in hydration (a restart with spends still stored)", async () => {
    const store = memoryStore<NewSpend>();
    await store.insert({ id: "c1", payload: spend("c1"), state: "pending", attempts: 0, nextAttemptAt: 0, error: null, createdAt: 0 });
    const send = jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve({}));
    const queue = createQueue<NewSpend, unknown>({ store, send });
    mount(queue, "signedIn");
    await waitFor(() => expect(send).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
  });

  it("sends when the network comes back", async () => {
    let online = false;
    const send = jest.fn(() => (online ? Promise.resolve({}) : Promise.reject(new NetworkError("offline"))));
    const queue = createQueue<NewSpend, unknown>({ store: memoryStore<NewSpend>(), send });
    mount(queue, "signedIn");
    await act(() => queue.add("c1", spend("c1")));
    online = true;
    act(() => onlineManager.setOnline(false));
    act(() => onlineManager.setOnline(true));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId("count")).toHaveTextContent("0"));
  });

  it("a sent spend refreshes today's figures and the lists; a rejected one refreshes today's figures", async () => {
    const qc = new QueryClient();
    const invalidate = jest.spyOn(qc, "invalidateQueries");
    create.mockResolvedValueOnce({ id: "s1" }).mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError("VALIDATION", 400, ""));
    const queue = createSpendQueue(qc, memoryStore<NewSpend>());
    await queue.add("c1", spend("c1"));
    await queue.flush();
    expect(invalidate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ queryKey: todayKey });
    expect(invalidate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ queryKey: ["transactions"] });
    invalidate.mockClear();
    await queue.add("c2", spend("c2"));
    await queue.flush();
    expect(invalidate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ queryKey: todayKey });
  });
});
```

```ts
/** Whole rupees with Indian grouping and no symbol, for layouts that draw ₹ apart: 123456700 paise → "12,34,567". */
export function formatRupeeDigits(paise: number): string {
```

Add to `app/src/lib/dates.ts`:

```ts
/** India has one offset and no daylight saving, so instants convert by adding 5 h 30 min. */
const IST_OFFSET_MS = 330 * 60_000;

/** The IST calendar date of an instant: "2026-10-22T18:30:00Z" → "2026-10-23". */
export function istDateOf(iso: string): string {
  return new Date(Date.parse(iso) + IST_OFFSET_MS).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

/** "2026-10-23T03:42:00Z" → "9:12 am", in IST, as the mockups write times. */
export function formatTime(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(Date.parse(iso) + IST_OFFSET_MS);
  const h = d.getUTCHours();
  const m = d.getUTCMinutes();
  return `${h % 12 === 0 ? 12 : h % 12}:${String(m).padStart(2, "0")} ${h < 12 ? "am" : "pm"}`;
}
```

```ts
import type { IconName } from "@/components/ui/icon";

/** Spec 2.4. Codes, order and names match the server's categories table (V006); categories.test pins them. */
export const CATEGORIES = [
  { code: "eating_out", label: "Eating out", icon: "coffee" },
  { code: "food_delivery", label: "Food delivery", icon: "food" },
  { code: "quick_commerce", label: "Quick commerce", icon: "cart" },
  { code: "shopping", label: "Shopping", icon: "bag" },
  { code: "travel", label: "Travel", icon: "car" },
  { code: "upi_to_people", label: "UPI to people", icon: "people" },
  { code: "groceries", label: "Groceries", icon: "basket" },
  { code: "subscriptions", label: "Subscriptions", icon: "tv" },
  { code: "bills_recharges", label: "Bills and recharges", icon: "file" },
  { code: "entertainment", label: "Entertainment", icon: "ticket" },
  { code: "health", label: "Health", icon: "heart" },
  { code: "other", label: "Other", icon: "dots" },
] as const satisfies readonly { code: string; label: string; icon: IconName }[];

export type CategoryCode = (typeof CATEGORIES)[number]["code"];

const OTHER = CATEGORIES[CATEGORIES.length - 1];

function find(code: string) {
  return CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.code === code) ?? OTHER;
}

/** A code this app version does not know (a newer server) reads as Other rather than breaking the row. */
export function categoryLabel(code: string): string {
  return find(code).label;
}

export function categoryIcon(code: string): IconName {
  return find(code).icon;
}
```

```ts
import { api } from "@/lib/client";
import type { CategoryCode } from "./categories";

export type TransactionKind = "SPEND" | "REFUND" | "INCOME";
export type PaymentMethod = "UPI" | "CARD" | "CASH" | "UNKNOWN";
export type ManualPayment = Exclude<PaymentMethod, "UNKNOWN">;
export type SpendTag = "NEED" | "WANT" | "REGRET";

/** GET/POST/PATCH /transactions (server Task 4). Amounts in paise; times are ISO instants. */
export type Transaction = {
  id: string;
  clientId: string | null;
  kind: TransactionKind;
  amountPaise: number;
  occurredAt: string;
  merchant: string | null;
  note: string | null;
  category: string;
  tag: SpendTag | null;
  paymentMethod: PaymentMethod;
  source: "MANUAL" | "STATEMENT" | "AA";
  status: "PENDING" | "CONFIRMED" | "STATEMENT_ONLY";
  spreadOverCycle: boolean;
  linked: boolean;
  assignedSavingsPaise: number | null;
  assignedPoolPaise: number | null;
  xpAwarded: number;
};

/** What quick-add queues and sends. clientId makes every resend safe (spec 2.3). */
export type NewSpend = {
  clientId: string;
  kind: "SPEND";
  amountPaise: number;
  occurredAt: string;
  category: CategoryCode;
  paymentMethod: ManualPayment;
  merchant?: string;
  note?: string;
  spreadOverCycle: boolean;
};

/** A missing field keeps its value; "" clears merchant or note. */
export type SpendPatch = {
  amountPaise?: number;
  category?: CategoryCode;
  merchant?: string;
  note?: string;
  paymentMethod?: ManualPayment;
  tag?: SpendTag;
  spreadOverCycle?: boolean;
};

export type TransactionPage = { items: Transaction[]; nextCursor?: string | null };

export const spendsApi = {
  create: (spend: NewSpend) => api.post<Transaction>("/transactions", spend),
  /** One IST day; 100 is the server's page cap and far above a day of spends. */
  listDay: (date: string) => api.get<TransactionPage>(`/transactions?from=${date}&to=${date}&limit=100`),
  get: (id: string) => api.get<Transaction>(`/transactions/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),
  patch: (id: string, patch: SpendPatch) => api.patch<Transaction>(`/transactions/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, patch),
  remove: (id: string) => api.del<void>(`/transactions/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),
};
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The queue**

`app/src/lib/queue.ts`:

```ts
import { ApiError } from "./api-errors";
import { messageFor } from "./errors";
import { log } from "./log";

export type QueueState = "pending" | "failed";

export type QueueItem<T> = {
  /** The item's identity. For spends it is the clientId, so a resend after a restart is deduplicated by the server. */
  id: string;
  payload: T;
  state: QueueState;
  attempts: number;
  /** Epoch ms before which a pending item is not sent again. */
  nextAttemptAt: number;
  /** Why the server refused a failed item, ready to show. */
  error: string | null;
  createdAt: number;
};

export interface QueueStore<T> {
  all(): Promise<QueueItem<T>[]>;
  /** Inserts unless an item with the same id exists; true when inserted. */
  insert(item: QueueItem<T>): Promise<boolean>;
  update(item: QueueItem<T>): Promise<void>;
  remove(id: string): Promise<void>;
  clear(): Promise<void>;
}

export type Queue<T> = {
  /** Resolves once stored items are loaded. */
  ready: Promise<void>;
  /** The current items; the same array until something changes (safe for useSyncExternalStore). */
  items(): readonly QueueItem<T>[];
  subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void;
  /** Writes to the store first, then tries to send. Rejects only when the store write fails. */
  add(id: string, payload: T): Promise<void>;
  flush(): Promise<void>;
  remove(id: string): Promise<void>;
  /** Drops every item. A send already in flight can no longer report back. */
  clear(): Promise<void>;
};

export type QueueOptions<T, R> = {
  store: QueueStore<T>;
  send: (payload: T) => Promise<R>;
  onSent?: (item: QueueItem<T>, result: R) => void;
  onFailed?: (item: QueueItem<T>) => void;
  now?: () => number;
  setTimer?: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => () => void;
};

const BASE_DELAY_MS = 2_000;
const MAX_DELAY_MS = 5 * 60_000;

/** 2 s, 4 s, 8 s … capped at 5 minutes. */
export function backoffMs(attempts: number): number {
  return Math.min(MAX_DELAY_MS, BASE_DELAY_MS * 2 ** Math.max(0, attempts - 1));
}

/** A 5xx, a timeout or a rate limit is worth another try, and so is anything that is not a server answer; any other 4xx is final (ERR-17). */
export function isRetryable(e: unknown): boolean {
  if (e instanceof ApiError) return e.status >= 500 || e.status === 429 || e.status === 408;
  return true;
}

/** What Home shows on a refused spend. Never the server's own text (spec 4.8). */
export function failureMessage(e: unknown): string {
  if (e instanceof ApiError) {
    return e.code === "VALIDATION" ? "Check the date and amount, then add it again" : messageFor(e.code);
  }
  return "Something went wrong. Try again in a moment.";
}

export function memoryStore<T>(): QueueStore<T> {
  const rows = new Map<string, QueueItem<T>>();
  return {
    all: () => Promise.resolve([...rows.values()].sort((a, b) => a.createdAt - b.createdAt)),
    insert: (item) => {
      if (rows.has(item.id)) return Promise.resolve(false);
      rows.set(item.id, item);
      return Promise.resolve(true);
    },
    update: (item) => {
      if (rows.has(item.id)) rows.set(item.id, item);
      return Promise.resolve();
    },
    remove: (id) => {
      rows.delete(id);
      return Promise.resolve();
    },
    clear: () => {
      rows.clear();
      return Promise.resolve();
    },
  };
}

/** Write first, send later (spec 4.8): one item at a time, oldest first, with backoff. */
export function createQueue<T, R>(opts: QueueOptions<T, R>): Queue<T> {
  const now = opts.now ?? Date.now;
  const setTimer =
    opts.setTimer ??
    ((fn: () => void, ms: number) => {
      const t = setTimeout(fn, ms);
      return () => clearTimeout(t);
    });
  let items: readonly QueueItem<T>[] = [];
  const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
  let flushing: Promise<void> | null = null;
  let again = false;
  let cancelTimer: (() => void) | null = null;
  let pausedUntil = 0;
  let generation = 0;

  const setItems = (next: readonly QueueItem<T>[]) => {
    items = next;
    listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  };

  const ready = opts.store
    .all()
    .then((stored) => setItems([...stored].sort((a, b) => a.createdAt - b.createdAt)))
    .catch((e: unknown) => log.error("queue.load_failed", { reason: e instanceof Error ? e.name : "unknown" }));

  async function persist(next: QueueItem<T>): Promise<void> {
    await opts.store.update(next);
    setItems(items.map((i) => (i.id === next.id ? next : i)));
  }

  function schedule(): void {
    cancelTimer?.();
    cancelTimer = null;
    const pending = items.filter((i) => i.state === "pending");
    if (pending.length === 0) return;
    const at = Math.max(pausedUntil, Math.min(...pending.map((i) => i.nextAttemptAt)));
    cancelTimer = setTimer(() => void run(false), Math.max(0, at - now()));
  }

  /** {@code force}: an event (add, network back, app opened, sign-in) tries every pending item now; the timer waits for each backoff. */
  async function sendDue(gen: number, force: boolean): Promise<void> {
    if (!force && now() < pausedUntil) return;
    const due = items.filter((i) => i.state === "pending" && (force || i.nextAttemptAt <= now()));
    for (const item of due) {
      if (gen !== generation) return;
      let result: R;
      try {
        result = await opts.send(item.payload);
      } catch (e) {
        if (gen !== generation) return;
        const attempts = item.attempts + 1;
        if (isRetryable(e)) {
          const next: QueueItem<T> = { ...item, attempts, nextAttemptAt: now() + backoffMs(attempts) };
          await persist(next);
          pausedUntil = next.nextAttemptAt; // the rest of the backlog waits too
          log.info("queue.retry_later", { attempts });
          return;
        }
        const failed: QueueItem<T> = { ...item, attempts, state: "failed", error: failureMessage(e) };
        await persist(failed);
        log.warn("queue.rejected", { errorCode: e instanceof ApiError ? e.code : "unknown" });
        opts.onFailed?.(failed);
        continue;
      }
      if (gen !== generation) return;
      await opts.store.remove(item.id);
      setItems(items.filter((i) => i.id !== item.id));
      log.info("queue.sent", { attempts: item.attempts + 1 });
      opts.onSent?.(item, result);
    }
  }

  function run(force: boolean): Promise<void> {
    if (flushing) {
      again = true;
      return flushing;
    }
    flushing = (async () => {
      await ready;
      const gen = generation;
      let forced = force;
      try {
        do {
          again = false;
          await sendDue(gen, forced);
          forced = false; // a repeat pass only picks up what was added meanwhile
        } while (again && gen === generation);
      } catch (e) {
        log.error("queue.flush_failed", { reason: e instanceof Error ? e.name : "unknown" });
      }
    })().finally(() => {
      flushing = null;
      schedule();
    });
    return flushing;
  }

  const flush = (): Promise<void> => run(true);

  async function add(id: string, payload: T): Promise<void> {
    await ready;
    const at = now();
    const item: QueueItem<T> = { id, payload, state: "pending", attempts: 0, nextAttemptAt: at, error: null, createdAt: at };
    const inserted = await opts.store.insert(item);
    if (inserted && !items.some((i) => i.id === id)) setItems([...items, item]);
    void flush();
  }

  async function remove(id: string): Promise<void> {
    await opts.store.remove(id);
    setItems(items.filter((i) => i.id !== id));
  }

  async function clear(): Promise<void> {
    generation += 1;
    cancelTimer?.();
    cancelTimer = null;
    pausedUntil = 0;
    await opts.store.clear();
    setItems([]);
  }

  return {
    ready,
    items: () => items,
    subscribe(listener) {
      listeners.add(listener);
      return () => listeners.delete(listener);
    },
    add,
    flush,
    remove,
    clear,
  };
}
```

`app/src/lib/queue-store.ts`:

```ts
import * as SQLite from "expo-sqlite";
import { log } from "./log";
import type { QueueItem, QueueStore } from "./queue";

/** The three calls the store uses, so tests can pass a fake. */
export type SqlDb = Pick<SQLite.SQLiteDatabase, "execAsync" | "runAsync" | "getAllAsync">;

type Row = { id: string; payload: string; state: string; attempts: number; next_attempt_at: number; error: string | null; created_at: number };

const CREATE = `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS outbox (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
  payload TEXT NOT NULL,
  state TEXT NOT NULL,
  attempts INTEGER NOT NULL,
  next_attempt_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  error TEXT,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);`;

export function rowToItem<T>(row: Row): QueueItem<T> | null {
  try {
    return {
      id: row.id,
      payload: JSON.parse(row.payload) as T,
      state: row.state === "failed" ? "failed" : "pending",
      attempts: row.attempts,
      nextAttemptAt: row.next_attempt_at,
      error: row.error,
      createdAt: row.created_at,
    };
  } catch (e) {
    log.warn("queue.row_unreadable", { reason: e instanceof Error ? e.name : "parse" });
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * The outbox on the phone (spec 4.8: SQLite first). Holds amounts and merchants typed on this phone, which stay
 * on the device until sent; sign-out clears it. The primary key is the item id, so a second insert of one id is a no-op.
 */
export function sqliteStore<T>(name: string, open: () => Promise<SqlDb> = () => SQLite.openDatabaseAsync(name)): QueueStore<T> {
  let db: Promise<SqlDb> | null = null;
  const conn = (): Promise<SqlDb> => {
    db ??= open()
      .then(async (d) => {
        await d.execAsync(CREATE);
        return d;
      })
      .catch((e: unknown) => {
        db = null;
        throw e;
      });
    return db;
  };
  return {
    async all() {
      const rows = await (await conn()).getAllAsync<Row>(
        "SELECT id, payload, state, attempts, next_attempt_at, error, created_at FROM outbox ORDER BY created_at, id",
        [],
      );
      return rows.map((r) => rowToItem<T>(r)).filter((i): i is QueueItem<T> => i !== null);
    },
    async insert(item) {
      const r = await (await conn()).runAsync(
        "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO outbox (id, payload, state, attempts, next_attempt_at, error, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        [item.id, JSON.stringify(item.payload), item.state, item.attempts, item.nextAttemptAt, item.error, item.createdAt],
      );
      return r.changes > 0;
    },
    async update(item) {
      await (await conn()).runAsync("UPDATE outbox SET state = ?, attempts = ?, next_attempt_at = ?, error = ? WHERE id = ?", [
        item.state,
        item.attempts,
        item.nextAttemptAt,
        item.error,
        item.id,
      ]);
    },
    async remove(id) {
      await (await conn()).runAsync("DELETE FROM outbox WHERE id = ?", [id]);
    },
    async clear() {
      await (await conn()).runAsync("DELETE FROM outbox", []);
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: The outbox provider**

`app/src/features/spends/outbox.tsx`:

```tsx
import { onlineManager, useQueryClient, type QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import React, { createContext, use, useEffect, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { AppState } from "react-native";
import type { SessionStatus } from "@/features/auth/session";
import { todayKey } from "@/features/budget/hooks";
import { createQueue, type Queue, type QueueItem, type QueueStore } from "@/lib/queue";
import { sqliteStore } from "@/lib/queue-store";
import { spendsApi, type NewSpend, type Transaction } from "./api";
import { spendKeys } from "./keys";

export type SpendQueue = Queue<NewSpend>;

const OutboxContext = createContext<SpendQueue | null>(null);

/** Sends queued spends; a sent or refused spend makes Home fetch the server's figures again. */
export function createSpendQueue(qc: QueryClient, store: QueueStore<NewSpend> = sqliteStore<NewSpend>("gullak-outbox.db")): SpendQueue {
  return createQueue<NewSpend, Transaction>({
    store,
    send: (spend) => spendsApi.create(spend),
    onSent: () => {
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: todayKey });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: spendKeys.all });
    },
    onFailed: () => {
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: todayKey });
    },
  });
}

/**
 * Owns the one spend queue. Signed in: sends now, when the network returns and when the app comes to the front.
 * Signed out: clears it, so nothing typed by one person is ever sent under another account.
 */
export function OutboxProvider({ children, status, queue }: { children: React.ReactNode; status: SessionStatus; queue?: SpendQueue }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [q] = useState<SpendQueue>(() => queue ?? createSpendQueue(qc));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === "signedOut") void q.clear();
    if (status === "signedIn") void q.flush();
  }, [status, q]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status !== "signedIn") return undefined;
    const offOnline = onlineManager.subscribe((online) => {
      if (online) void q.flush();
    });
    const sub = AppState.addEventListener("change", (s) => {
      if (s === "active") void q.flush();
    });
    return () => {
      offOnline();
      sub.remove();
    };
  }, [status, q]);

  return <OutboxContext value={q}>{children}</OutboxContext>;
}

export function useOutbox(): SpendQueue {
  const q = use(OutboxContext);
  if (!q) throw new Error("useOutbox needs an OutboxProvider");
  return q;
}

export function useOutboxItems(): readonly QueueItem<NewSpend>[] {
  const q = useOutbox();
  return useSyncExternalStore(q.subscribe, q.items, q.items);
}
```

```tsx
/** The outbox follows the session: it clears on sign-out and sends once signed in. */
function OutboxBridge({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const { status } = useSession();
  return <OutboxProvider status={status}>{children}</OutboxProvider>;
}
```

```tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import React from "react";
import { touchTarget } from "@/theme";
import { Segmented } from "./segmented";
import { Toggle } from "./toggle";

function minHeightOf(node: { props: { style?: unknown } }): number {
  const flat = Object.assign({}, ...[node.props.style].flat(Infinity).filter(Boolean)) as { minHeight?: number };
  return flat.minHeight ?? 0;
}

describe("Segmented (APP-29)", () => {
  const options = [
    { value: "UPI", label: "UPI" },
    { value: "CARD", label: "Card" },

    expect(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "UPI" })).toHaveAccessibilityState({ checked: true });
    expect(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "Card" })).toHaveAccessibilityState({ checked: false });

  });
});
```

```tsx
      budgetPaise={opts.budgetPaise === undefined ? 59300 : opts.budgetPaise}
      spendsToday={opts.spendsToday ?? 0}
      onSave={onSave}
      onClose={onClose}
    />,
  );
  return { onSave, onClose };
}

function type(...keys: string[]) {

describe("quick-add", () => {

      .getAllByRole("button")
      .filter((b) => (b.props as { accessibilityState?: { selected?: boolean } }).accessibilityState?.selected !== undefined);
    expect(chips).toHaveLength(12);

  it("saves the whole draft", async () => {
    const { onSave } = setup();
    type("1", "5", "0");
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Quick commerce" }));
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "Cash" }));

    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add a note" }));

      amountPaise: 15000,
      category: "quick_commerce",
      paymentMethod: "CASH",
      merchant: "Zepto",
      note: "milk",
      spreadOverCycle: false,
    });

  it("says so when the phone could not store the spend", async () => {
    setup({ onSave: () => Promise.reject(new Error("disk full")) });
    type("5", "0");
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByTestId("add-spend"));

    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add ₹50 · +5 XP" })).toBeEnabled();
  });
});
```

`app/src/features/spends/amount-pad.ts`:

```ts
export type PadKey = "1" | "2" | "3" | "4" | "5" | "6" | "7" | "8" | "9" | "00" | "0" | "back";

/** The keys in the order the mockup draws them, three to a row. */
export const PAD_KEYS: readonly PadKey[] = ["1", "2", "3", "4", "5", "6", "7", "8", "9", "00", "0", "back"];

/** Spec 2.3: the amount is capped at 7 digits of rupees (₹99,99,999). */
export const MAX_DIGITS = 7;

/** The quick-add reducer (APP-03). The amount is a string of whole rupees with no leading zeros; "0" is empty. */
export function pressKey(amount: string, key: PadKey): string {
  if (key === "back") return amount.length > 1 ? amount.slice(0, -1) : "0";
  if (amount === "0") return key === "00" ? "0" : key;
  return (amount + key).slice(0, MAX_DIGITS);
}

export function amountToPaise(amount: string): number {
  return Number.parseInt(amount, 10) * 100;
}
```

`app/src/features/spends/sure.ts`:

```ts
/** Spec 2.3: above ₹10,000, or above 5 times today's budget, the app asks "Sure?" once. */
export const SURE_ABOVE_PAISE = 10_000_00;

/** With a ₹0 budget every amount would be "5 times" it, so that half of the rule needs a budget above ₹0. */
export function needsSure(amountPaise: number, budgetPaise: number | null): boolean {
  if (amountPaise > SURE_ABOVE_PAISE) return true;
  return budgetPaise !== null && budgetPaise > 0 && amountPaise > budgetPaise * 5;
}
```

`app/src/features/spends/hooks.ts`:

```ts
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { randomUUID } from "expo-crypto";
import { useCallback } from "react";
import { FIRST_SPEND_FLAG } from "@/features/home/flags";
import { kv } from "@/lib/kv";
import { spendsApi, type ManualPayment, type NewSpend, type TransactionPage } from "./api";
import type { CategoryCode } from "./categories";
import { spendKeys } from "./keys";
import { useOutbox } from "./outbox";

/** What the quick-add screen hands over. Text is raw; toNewSpend trims it. */
export type SpendDraft = {
  amountPaise: number;
  category: CategoryCode;
  paymentMethod: ManualPayment;
  merchant: string;
  note: string;
  spreadOverCycle: boolean;
};

/** The time is taken when the spend is typed, so an offline spend keeps its day however late it is sent. */
export function toNewSpend(draft: SpendDraft, clientId: string, at: Date): NewSpend {
  const merchant = draft.merchant.trim();
  const note = draft.note.trim();
  return {
    clientId,
    kind: "SPEND",
    amountPaise: draft.amountPaise,
    occurredAt: at.toISOString(),
    category: draft.category,
    paymentMethod: draft.paymentMethod,
    ...(merchant ? { merchant } : {}),
    ...(note ? { note } : {}),
    spreadOverCycle: draft.spreadOverCycle,
  };
}

export function useDaySpends(date: string) {
  return useQuery<TransactionPage>({ queryKey: spendKeys.day(date), queryFn: () => spendsApi.listDay(date), staleTime: 30_000 });
}

/** Saves on the phone first (the outbox), then the outbox sends it. Task 10 adds the Home update, coin and 80% message. */
export function useAddSpend() {
  const outbox = useOutbox();
  return useCallback(
    async (draft: SpendDraft): Promise<NewSpend> => {
      const spend = toNewSpend(draft, randomUUID(), new Date());
      await outbox.add(spend.clientId, spend);
      void kv.set(FIRST_SPEND_FLAG, "1");
      return spend;
    },
    [outbox],
  );
}
```

`app/src/components/ui/segmented.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { Pressable, View } from "react-native";
import { colors, radius, shadows, spacing, touchTarget } from "@/theme";
import { Text } from "./text";

export type SegmentedOption<T extends string> = { value: T; label: string };

export type SegmentedProps<T extends string> = {
  label: string;
  options: readonly SegmentedOption<T>[];
  value: T | null;
  onChange: (value: T) => void;
  /** "pill": options inside a sand track (Paid with). "boxes": separate bordered boxes (Need / Want / Regret). */
  variant?: "pill" | "boxes";
  testID?: string;
};

/** One choice from a few, exposed as a radio group: each option is a radio with checked state. */
export function Segmented<T extends string>({ label, options, value, onChange, variant = "pill", testID }: SegmentedProps<T>) {
  const pill = variant === "pill";
  return (
    <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }} testID={testID}>
      <Text variant="labelMuted">{label}</Text>
      <View
        accessibilityRole="radiogroup"
        accessibilityLabel={label}
        style={{
          flexDirection: "row",
          gap: pill ? spacing.xs : spacing.sm,
          padding: pill ? spacing.xs : 0,
          borderRadius: radius.lg,
          borderCurve: "continuous",
          backgroundColor: pill ? colors.sand : "transparent",
        }}
      >
        {options.map((o) => {
          const checked = o.value === value;
          return (
            <Pressable
              key={o.value}
              accessibilityRole="radio"
              accessibilityLabel={o.label}
              accessibilityState={{ checked }}
              onPress={() => onChange(o.value)}
              style={({ pressed }) => ({
                flex: 1,
                minHeight: touchTarget,
                alignItems: "center",
                justifyContent: "center",
                borderRadius: radius.md,
                borderCurve: "continuous",
                backgroundColor: checked ? colors.surface : "transparent",
                borderWidth: pill ? 0 : checked ? 2 : 1,
                borderColor: checked ? colors.ink : colors.control,
                boxShadow: pill && checked ? shadows.segment : shadows.none,
                opacity: pressed ? 0.85 : 1,
              })}
            >
              <Text variant={checked ? "bodyStrong" : "body"}>{o.label}</Text>
            </Pressable>
          );
        })}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}
```

`app/src/components/ui/toggle.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { Pressable, View } from "react-native";
import { colors, radius, spacing, touchTarget } from "@/theme";
import { Text } from "./text";

export type ToggleProps = { label: string; description?: string; value: boolean; onChange: (value: boolean) => void; testID?: string };

/** An on/off row exposed as a switch. The whole row is the target. */
export function Toggle({ label, description, value, onChange, testID }: ToggleProps) {
  return (
    <Pressable
      accessibilityRole="switch"
      accessibilityLabel={label}
      accessibilityHint={description}
      accessibilityState={{ checked: value }}
      onPress={() => onChange(!value)}
      testID={testID}
      style={({ pressed }) => ({ minHeight: touchTarget, flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: spacing.md, opacity: pressed ? 0.85 : 1 })}
    >
      <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: spacing.xs }}>
        <Text variant="bodyStrong">{label}</Text>
        {description ? <Text variant="caption">{description}</Text> : null}
      </View>
      <View
        style={{
          width: 48,
          height: 28,
          borderRadius: radius.full,
          padding: 3,
          backgroundColor: value ? colors.ink : colors.sand,
          borderWidth: 1,
          borderColor: value ? colors.ink : colors.control,
          alignItems: value ? "flex-end" : "flex-start",
        }}
      >
        <View style={{ width: 20, height: 20, borderRadius: radius.full, backgroundColor: colors.surface }} />
      </View>
    </Pressable>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { ScrollView } from "react-native";
import { Chip } from "@/components/ui/chip";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { spacing } from "@/theme";
import { CATEGORIES, type CategoryCode } from "../categories";

/** All twelve categories in one horizontally scrolling row that bleeds to the screen edges (APP-08). */
export function CategoryRow({ value, onChange }: { value: CategoryCode; onChange: (code: CategoryCode) => void }) {
  return (
    <ScrollView
      horizontal
      showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
      accessibilityLabel="Category"
      testID="category-row"
      style={{ marginHorizontal: -spacing.md, flexGrow: 0 }}
      contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: spacing.md, gap: spacing.sm }}
    >
      {CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
        <Chip
          key={c.code}
          label={c.label}
          selected={c.code === value}
          onPress={() => onChange(c.code)}
          icon={<Icon name={c.icon} size={16} />}
          testID={`category-${c.code}`}
        />
      ))}
    </ScrollView>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { Pressable, View } from "react-native";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { colors, radius, spacing } from "@/theme";
import { PAD_KEYS, type PadKey } from "../amount-pad";

/** Three keys a row, 52px tall. Press feedback is a tint and a small scale, both instant. */
export function NumberPad({ onKey }: { onKey: (key: PadKey) => void }) {
  return (
    <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: spacing.sm }}>
      {PAD_KEYS.map((k) => (
        <Pressable
          key={k}
          testID={`key-${k}`}
          accessibilityRole="button"
          accessibilityLabel={k === "back" ? "Delete last digit" : k}
          onPress={() => onKey(k)}
          style={({ pressed }) => ({
            flexBasis: "30%",
            flexGrow: 1,
            height: 52,
            borderRadius: radius.md,
            borderCurve: "continuous",
            backgroundColor: pressed ? colors.sand : colors.surface,
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            transform: [{ scale: pressed ? 0.97 : 1 }],
          })}
        >
          {k === "back" ? <Icon name="backspace" size={24} /> : <Text variant="title">{k}</Text>}
        </Pressable>
      ))}
    </View>
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/spends/screens/quick-add-screen.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Stack } from "expo-router";
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { BackHandler, View } from "react-native";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Field } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Screen } from "@/components/ui/screen";
import { Segmented } from "@/components/ui/segmented";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Toggle } from "@/components/ui/toggle";
import { log } from "@/lib/log";
import { formatRupeeDigits } from "@/lib/money";
import { colors, radius, spacing } from "@/theme";
import { amountToPaise, pressKey, type PadKey } from "../amount-pad";
import type { ManualPayment } from "../api";
import type { CategoryCode } from "../categories";
import { CategoryRow } from "../components/category-row";
import { NumberPad } from "../components/number-pad";
import type { SpendDraft } from "../hooks";
import { PAYMENT_OPTIONS } from "../labels";
import { needsSure } from "../sure";

export type QuickAddProps = {
  /** Today's budget from Home's cached figures; null when unknown (the 5× check is then skipped). */
  budgetPaise: number | null;
  /** Spends already logged today; the "+5 XP" hint stops after the tenth (spec 2.6). */
  spendsToday: number;
  onSave: (draft: SpendDraft) => Promise<void>;
  onClose: () => void;
};

/** Spec 2.3: opens straight to the number pad; two taps for the usual coffee (amount, Add). */
export function QuickAddScreen({ budgetPaise, spendsToday, onSave, onClose }: QuickAddProps) {
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("0");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<CategoryCode>("eating_out");
  const [payment, setPayment] = useState<ManualPayment>("UPI");
  const [merchant, setMerchant] = useState("");
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const [noteOpen, setNoteOpen] = useState(false);
  const [spread, setSpread] = useState(false);
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);
  const [sureAsked, setSureAsked] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const paise = amountToPaise(amount);
  const empty = paise === 0;
  const big = needsSure(paise, budgetPaise);
  const shown = formatRupeeDigits(paise);

  function press(key: PadKey) {
    const next = pressKey(amount, key);
    setAmount(next);
    setSureAsked(false);
    if (next === "0") setConfirming(false); // APP-07
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    const sub = BackHandler.addEventListener("hardwareBackPress", () => {
      if (empty) return false;
      setConfirming(true);
      return true;
    });
    return () => sub.remove();
  }, [empty]);

  function close() {
    if (empty) onClose();
    else setConfirming(true);
  }

  async function add() {
    if (empty || saving) return;
    if (big && !sureAsked) {
      setSureAsked(true);
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await onSave({ amountPaise: paise, category, paymentMethod: payment, merchant, note, spreadOverCycle: big && spread });
    } catch (e) {
      log.warn("spend.local_save_failed", { reason: e instanceof Error ? e.name : "unknown" });
      setError("Couldn't save this spend on your phone. Try again.");
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  const xpHint = spendsToday < 10 ? " · +5 XP" : "";
  const addTitle = empty ? "Enter an amount" : sureAsked ? `Sure? Add ₹${shown}` : `Add ₹${shown}${xpHint}`;

  return (
    <>
      {/* iOS swipe-down must not drop a typed amount; the Close button asks instead. */}
      <Stack.Screen options={{ gestureEnabled: empty }} />
      <Screen
        testID="quick-add"
        footer={
          <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
            <NumberPad onKey={press} />
            {sureAsked ? (
              <Text variant="label" color={colors.warnInk} accessibilityRole="alert">
                That's a big one. Tap again to add it.
              </Text>
            ) : null}
            {error ? (
              <Text variant="label" color={colors.bad} accessibilityRole="alert">
                {error}
              </Text>
            ) : null}
            <Button title={addTitle} onPress={() => void add()} disabled={empty} loading={saving} testID="add-spend" />
          </View>
        }
      >
        <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", minHeight: 48 }}>
          <Text variant="title" accessibilityRole="header">
            New expense
          </Text>
          <Button
            title="Close"
            variant="secondary"
            iconOnly
            icon={<Icon name="x" size={20} />}
            onPress={close}
            style={{ width: 48, height: 48, borderRadius: radius.full }}
          />
        </View>
        {confirming ? (
          <View
            accessibilityRole="alert"
            style={{
              flexDirection: "row",
              alignItems: "center",
              gap: spacing.sm,
              paddingVertical: spacing.sm,
              paddingRight: spacing.sm,
              paddingLeft: spacing.md,
              borderRadius: radius.lg,
              borderCurve: "continuous",
              backgroundColor: colors.dark,
            }}
          >
            <Text variant="label" color={colors.darkText} style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              {`Discard this ₹${shown} spend?`}
            </Text>
            <Button title="Keep editing" variant="secondary" onPress={() => setConfirming(false)} />
            <Button title="Discard" variant="ghost" textColor={colors.darkText} onPress={onClose} />
          </View>
        ) : null}
        <View
          accessible
          accessibilityRole="text"
          accessibilityLabel={`Amount ₹${shown}`}
          style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "baseline", justifyContent: "center", gap: spacing.xs, minHeight: 78 }}
        >
          <Text variant="heading" color={colors.muted}>
            ₹
          </Text>
          <Text variant="display" style={{ fontVariant: ["tabular-nums"] }}>
            {shown}
          </Text>
        </View>
        <CategoryRow value={category} onChange={setCategory} />
        <Segmented label="Paid with" options={PAYMENT_OPTIONS} value={payment} onChange={setPayment} />
        <Field
          label="Where (optional)"
          placeholder="e.g. Third Wave Coffee"
          value={merchant}
          onChangeText={setMerchant}
          maxLength={80}
          autoCapitalize="words"
          returnKeyType="done"
        />
        {noteOpen ? (
          <Field label="Note (optional)" value={note} onChangeText={setNote} maxLength={140} returnKeyType="done" />
        ) : (
          <Button title="Add a note" variant="ghost" onPress={() => setNoteOpen(true)} />
        )}
        {big ? (
          <Toggle
            label="Spread over the cycle"
            description="For a big one-off like a flight: it comes out of the whole cycle, not just today"
            value={spread}
            onChange={setSpread}
          />
        ) : null}
      </Screen>
    </>
  );
}
```

`app/app/add.tsx`:

```tsx
import * as Haptics from "expo-haptics";
import { useRouter } from "expo-router";
import React from "react";
import { useToday } from "@/features/budget/hooks";
import { useAddSpend, useDaySpends } from "@/features/spends/hooks";
import { useOutboxItems } from "@/features/spends/outbox";
import { QuickAddScreen } from "@/features/spends/screens/quick-add-screen";
import { istDateOf, todayIst } from "@/lib/dates";

export { RouteErrorFallback as ErrorBoundary } from "@/components/error-boundary";

export default function AddRoute() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const today = useToday();
  const date = today.data?.date ?? todayIst();
  const day = useDaySpends(date);
  const queued = useOutboxItems();
  const addSpend = useAddSpend();
  const saved = day.data?.items.filter((t) => t.kind === "SPEND").length ?? 0;
  const waiting = queued.filter((q) => q.state === "pending" && istDateOf(q.payload.occurredAt) === date).length;
  return (
    <QuickAddScreen
      budgetPaise={today.data?.budgetPaise ?? null}
      spendsToday={saved + waiting}
      onSave={async (draft) => {
        await addSpend(draft);
        void Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Success);
        router.back();
      }}
      onClose={() => router.back()}
    />
  );
}
```

- Produces: `applySpend(today, deltaPaise) : Today`, `percentOf(spent, budget)`, `crossesEighty(today, addPaise)`, `leftAfterRemoving(today, amountPaise)`; `emitGullak(kind)`, `useGullakEvent()`, `latestGullakEvent()`; `NotificationsApi.presentNotificationAsync`, `NotificationsApi.setForegroundHandler`, `configureForegroundDisplay()`, `eightyPercentCopy`, `notifyEightyPercent({personality, date, now}, api?, store?) : Promise<boolean>`; `useAddSpend(now?)` (final form).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`app/src/features/budget/optimistic.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { Today } from "./api";
import { applySpend, crossesEighty, leftAfterRemoving, percentOf } from "./optimistic";

const day: Today = {
  date: "2026-10-23",
  budgetPaise: 59300,
  spentPaise: 18000,
  leftPaise: 41300,
  overPaise: 0,
  poolOverPaise: 0,
  mood: "HAPPY",
  line: "₹413 left for today. Nice and steady.",
  daysLeft: 9,
  cycleStart: "2026-10-01",
  cycleEnd: "2026-10-31",
};

describe("optimistic Home figures (mirror of the server rules)", () => {
  it("adds a spend: spent, left and mood move; the line turns plain until the server's arrives", () => {
    expect(applySpend(day, 15000)).toEqual({ ...day, spentPaise: 33000, leftPaise: 26300, overPaise: 0, mood: "CALM", line: "₹263 left for today" });
  });

  it("going over faints the Gullak and says by how much", () => {
    expect(applySpend(day, 50000)).toMatchObject({ spentPaise: 68000, leftPaise: 0, overPaise: 8700, mood: "FAINTED", line: "Over by ₹87" });
  });

  it("a ₹0 budget is nervous until something is spent, then fainted", () => {
    const zero = { ...day, budgetPaise: 0, spentPaise: 0, leftPaise: 0, mood: "NERVOUS" as const };
    expect(applySpend(zero, 0).mood).toBe("NERVOUS");
    expect(applySpend(zero, 100)).toMatchObject({ mood: "FAINTED", overPaise: 100 });
  });

  it("a pool below zero stays fainted and the over figure includes it", () => {
    expect(applySpend({ ...day, poolOverPaise: 40000 }, 1000)).toMatchObject({ mood: "FAINTED", line: "Over by ₹400" });
  });

  it("taking a spend away (delete or a lower edit) works the other way, never below zero", () => {
    expect(applySpend(day, -15000)).toMatchObject({ spentPaise: 3000, leftPaise: 56300, mood: "HAPPY" });
    expect(applySpend(day, -99999).spentPaise).toBe(0);
  });

  it("percent used matches the server's whole-number rule", () => {
    expect(percentOf(29650, 59300)).toBe(50);
    expect(percentOf(29649, 59300)).toBe(49);
    expect(percentOf(100, 0)).toBe(999);
    expect(percentOf(0, 0)).toBe(0);
  });

  it("crossesEighty only when this spend takes today from under 80% to 80% or more", () => {
    expect(crossesEighty({ ...day, spentPaise: 40000 }, 10000)).toBe(true); // 67% → 84%
    expect(crossesEighty({ ...day, spentPaise: 50000 }, 1000)).toBe(false); // already above
    expect(crossesEighty(day, 1000)).toBe(false); // 30% → 32%
    expect(crossesEighty({ ...day, budgetPaise: 0 }, 1000)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("what is left after removing a spend (the delete dialog's figure)", () => {
    expect(leftAfterRemoving(day, 15000)).toBe(56300); // "Today goes back to ₹563 left"
    expect(leftAfterRemoving({ ...day, spentPaise: 72000, leftPaise: 0 }, 5000)).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

`app/src/features/spends/hooks.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { act, renderHook } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import React from "react";
import type { Today } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { todayKey } from "@/features/budget/hooks";
import { latestGullakEvent } from "@/features/gullak/events";
import { NetworkError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { istDateOf } from "@/lib/dates";
import { createQueue, memoryStore, type QueueStore } from "@/lib/queue";
import type { NewSpend } from "./api";
import { useAddSpend, type SpendDraft } from "./hooks";
import { OutboxProvider } from "./outbox";

jest.mock("expo-crypto", () => {
  let n = 0;
  return { randomUUID: () => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(++n).padStart(12, "0")}` };
});
jest.mock("@/lib/kv", () => ({ kv: { get: jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve(null)), set: jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve()), remove: jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve()) } }));
jest.mock("@/lib/notifications", () => ({ notifyEightyPercent: jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve(true)) }));
jest.mock("@/features/profile/hooks", () => ({ useProfile: () => ({ data: { personality: "SAVAGE" } }) }));
import { kv } from "@/lib/kv";
import { notifyEightyPercent } from "@/lib/notifications";

const day: Today = {
  date: "2026-10-23",
  budgetPaise: 59300,
  spentPaise: 18000,
  leftPaise: 41300,
  overPaise: 0,
  poolOverPaise: 0,
  mood: "HAPPY",
  line: "₹413 left for today. Nice and steady.",
  daysLeft: 9,
  cycleStart: "2026-10-01",
  cycleEnd: "2026-10-31",
};

const draft = (over: Partial<SpendDraft> = {}): SpendDraft => ({
  amountPaise: 15000,
  category: "eating_out",
  paymentMethod: "UPI",
  merchant: " Third Wave Coffee ",
  note: "",
  spreadOverCycle: false,
  ...over,
});

function setup(opts: { now: string; send?: (s: NewSpend) => Promise<unknown>; store?: QueueStore<NewSpend>; today?: Today | null }) {
  const qc = new QueryClient();
  if (opts.today !== null) qc.setQueryData(todayKey, opts.today ?? day);
  const store = opts.store ?? memoryStore<NewSpend>();
  const sent: NewSpend[] = [];
  const queue = createQueue<NewSpend, unknown>({
    store,
    send: (s) => {
      sent.push(s);
      return opts.send ? opts.send(s) : new Promise<never>(() => undefined);
    },
  });
  const wrapper = ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => (
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <OutboxProvider status="signedIn" queue={queue}>
        {children}
      </OutboxProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
  const { result } = renderHook(() => useAddSpend(() => new Date(opts.now)), { wrapper });
  return { qc, store, queue, sent, add: result.current };
}

beforeEach(() => jest.clearAllMocks());

describe("useAddSpend", () => {
  it("stores the spend on the phone before resolving, with a fresh clientId and the time it was typed (APP-09)", async () => {
    const h = setup({ now: "2026-10-23T03:42:00.000Z" });
    let spend: NewSpend | undefined;
    await act(async () => {
      spend = await h.add(draft());
    });
    expect(spend).toMatchObject({ kind: "SPEND", amountPaise: 15000, merchant: "Third Wave Coffee", occurredAt: "2026-10-23T03:42:00.000Z" });
    expect(spend).not.toHaveProperty("note");
    expect((await h.store.all()).map((i) => i.id)).toEqual([spend?.clientId]);
  });

  it("Home's figures move at once (APP-09, optimistic)", async () => {
    const h = setup({ now: "2026-10-23T03:42:00.000Z" });
    await act(async () => void (await h.add(draft())));
    expect(h.qc.getQueryData<Today>(todayKey)).toMatchObject({ spentPaise: 33000, leftPaise: 26300, mood: "CALM" });
  });

  it("a spread spend, or one dated on another day, leaves Home's figures to the server", async () => {
    const h = setup({ now: "2026-10-23T03:42:00.000Z" });
    await act(async () => void (await h.add(draft({ amountPaise: 5_000_000, spreadOverCycle: true }))));
    expect(h.qc.getQueryData<Today>(todayKey)).toEqual(day);
    const late = setup({ now: "2026-10-23T19:00:00.000Z" }); // 00:30 IST on the 24th; the cache still says the 23rd
    await act(async () => void (await late.add(draft())));
    expect(late.qc.getQueryData<Today>(todayKey)).toEqual(day);
  });

  it("drops the coin, writes the first-spend flag", async () => {
    const before = latestGullakEvent()?.key ?? 0;
    const h = setup({ now: "2026-10-23T03:42:00.000Z" });
    await act(async () => void (await h.add(draft())));
    expect(latestGullakEvent()).toEqual({ kind: "coin", key: before + 1 });
    expect(kv.set).toHaveBeenCalledWith("first-spend-logged", "1");
  });

  it("crossing 80% of today asks for the local message, in the user's personality (APP-31)", async () => {
    const h = setup({ now: "2026-10-23T03:42:00.000Z", today: { ...day, spentPaise: 40000, leftPaise: 19300 } });
    await act(async () => void (await h.add(draft({ amountPaise: 10000 }))));
    expect(notifyEightyPercent).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ personality: "SAVAGE", date: "2026-10-23", now: new Date("2026-10-23T03:42:00.000Z") });
  });

  it("not crossing 80% sends nothing", async () => {
    const h = setup({ now: "2026-10-23T03:42:00.000Z" });
    await act(async () => void (await h.add(draft({ amountPaise: 1000 }))));
    expect(notifyEightyPercent).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("queuedSpendKeepsTheTimeItWasTyped: typed offline at 23:59 IST, sent after midnight, still the 23rd", async () => {
    let online = false;
    const h = setup({
      now: "2026-10-23T18:29:00.000Z",
      send: () => (online ? Promise.resolve({}) : Promise.reject(new NetworkError("offline"))),
    });
    await act(async () => void (await h.add(draft())));
    await act(() => h.queue.flush());
    online = true; // 00:20 IST on the 24th
    await act(() => h.queue.flush());
    expect(h.sent.at(-1)?.occurredAt).toBe("2026-10-23T18:29:00.000Z");
    expect(istDateOf(h.sent.at(-1)?.occurredAt ?? "")).toBe("2026-10-23");
    expect(h.queue.items()).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The optimistic figures**

`app/src/features/budget/optimistic.ts`:

```ts
import { moodFor, type Mood, type ServerMood } from "@/features/gullak/moods";
import { formatRupees } from "@/lib/money";
import type { Today } from "./api";

/** Whole-number percent of the budget used, as the server works it out (BudgetCalculator). */
export function percentOf(spentPaise: number, budgetPaise: number): number {
  if (budgetPaise > 0) return Math.min(999, Math.floor((spentPaise * 100) / budgetPaise));
  return spentPaise > 0 ? 999 : 0;
}

function toServer(mood: Mood): ServerMood {
  switch (mood) {
    case "happy":
    case "celebrating":
      return "HAPPY";
    case "calm":
      return "CALM";
    case "nervous":
      return "NERVOUS";
    case "fainted":
      return "FAINTED";
  }
}

/**
 * Home's figures after adding (or, with a negative delta, removing) a spend on today's date, before the server
 * answers (spec 4.8). Same rules as the server: fainted when over or the pool is below zero, nervous at a ₹0
 * budget. The personality line comes back with the next fetch; until then a plain one keeps the figures honest.
 */
export function applySpend(today: Today, deltaPaise: number): Today {
  const spent = Math.max(0, today.spentPaise + deltaPaise);
  const left = Math.max(0, today.budgetPaise - spent);
  const over = Math.max(0, spent - today.budgetPaise);
  const mood: ServerMood =
    today.poolOverPaise > 0 || over > 0 ? "FAINTED" : today.budgetPaise === 0 ? "NERVOUS" : toServer(moodFor(percentOf(spent, today.budgetPaise)));
  const totalOver = over + today.poolOverPaise;
  const line = totalOver > 0 ? `Over by ${formatRupees(totalOver)}` : `${formatRupees(left)} left for today`;
  return { ...today, spentPaise: spent, leftPaise: left, overPaise: over, mood, line };
}

/** Spec 2.7: the 80% message fires when a quick-add takes today from under 80% to 80% or more. */
export function crossesEighty(today: Today, addPaise: number): boolean {
  if (today.budgetPaise <= 0) return false;
  return percentOf(today.spentPaise, today.budgetPaise) < 80 && percentOf(today.spentPaise + addPaise, today.budgetPaise) >= 80;
}

/** "Today goes back to ₹563 left": what is left once a spend from today is taken away. */
export function leftAfterRemoving(today: Today, amountPaise: number): number {
  return Math.max(0, today.budgetPaise - Math.max(0, today.spentPaise - amountPaise));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The Gullak's event store**

`app/src/features/gullak/events.ts`:

```ts
import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import type { GullakEvent } from "./gullak";

let current: GullakEvent | undefined;
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

function subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => listeners.delete(listener);
}

/** Bumps the event key so whichever Gullak is on screen plays it (a coin on every saved spend; Plan 6 adds celebrate). */
export function emitGullak(kind: GullakEvent["kind"]): void {
  current = { kind, key: (current?.key ?? 0) + 1 };
  listeners.forEach((l) => l());
}

export function latestGullakEvent(): GullakEvent | undefined {
  return current;
}

export function useGullakEvent(): GullakEvent | undefined {
  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, latestGullakEvent, latestGullakEvent);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Notifications**

`app/src/lib/notifications.ts` (whole file; the daily part is Plan 2's, unchanged):

```ts
import type { Personality } from "@/features/profile/api";
import { hourIst } from "./dates";
import { kv } from "./kv";
import { log } from "./log";

export type NotificationsApi = {
  getPermissionsAsync(): Promise<{ granted: boolean }>;
  requestPermissionsAsync(): Promise<{ granted: boolean }>;
  getAllScheduledNotificationsAsync(): Promise<{ identifier: string }[]>;
  cancelScheduledNotificationAsync(identifier: string): Promise<void>;
  scheduleNotificationAsync(request: { identifier: string; content: { title: string; body: string }; trigger: { type: "daily"; hour: number; minute: number } }): Promise<string>;
  /** Shows a message now. */
  presentNotificationAsync(request: { identifier: string; content: { title: string; body: string } }): Promise<string>;
  /** Lets messages show while the app is open (the 80% one fires while the user is in it). */
  setForegroundHandler(): void;
};

/** Static per-personality copy; no amounts, because the message is scheduled hours ahead (spec 2.7). */
export const notificationCopy: Record<Personality, { morning: { title: string; body: string }; night: { title: string; body: string } }> = {
  CHILL: {
    morning: { title: "Morning", body: "Your number for today is ready" },
    night: { title: "How did today go?", body: "Your report card is in" },
  },
  SAVAGE: {
    morning: { title: "Wake up", body: "Your budget is waiting. So is Swiggy." },
    night: { title: "Report card time", body: "Brace yourself" },
  },
  MOM: {
    morning: { title: "Beta", body: "Check your budget before you leave the house" },
    night: { title: "Did you eat?", body: "Also, your report card is ready" },
  },
  HYPE: {
    morning: { title: "New day, new number", body: "Let's go" },
    night: { title: "Day done", body: "Come see your score" },
  },
};

/** Spec 2.7 "80% of today used". No amount: the lock screen is not private. */
export const eightyPercentCopy: Record<Personality, { title: string; body: string }> = {
  CHILL: { title: "Heads up", body: "80% of today's budget is gone. Easy for the rest of the day." },
  SAVAGE: { title: "Slow down", body: "80% of today's budget, gone. Put the phone down." },
  MOM: { title: "Beta, careful", body: "80% of today's money is spent. Eat at home tonight." },
  HYPE: { title: "Almost there", body: "80% used. Finish the day strong." },
};

function realApi(): NotificationsApi {
  // Imported lazily so the web build never loads expo-notifications (it is phone-only).
  const N = require("expo-notifications") as typeof import("expo-notifications");
  return {
    getPermissionsAsync: () => N.getPermissionsAsync(),
    requestPermissionsAsync: () => N.requestPermissionsAsync(),
    getAllScheduledNotificationsAsync: () => N.getAllScheduledNotificationsAsync(),
    cancelScheduledNotificationAsync: (id) => N.cancelScheduledNotificationAsync(id),
    scheduleNotificationAsync: (req) =>
      N.scheduleNotificationAsync({
        identifier: req.identifier,
        content: req.content,
        trigger: { type: N.SchedulableTriggerInputTypes.DAILY, hour: req.trigger.hour, minute: req.trigger.minute },
      }),
    presentNotificationAsync: (req) => N.scheduleNotificationAsync({ identifier: req.identifier, content: req.content, trigger: null }),
    setForegroundHandler: () =>
      N.setNotificationHandler({
        handleNotification: () => Promise.resolve({ shouldShowBanner: true, shouldShowList: true, shouldPlaySound: false, shouldSetBadge: false }),
      }),
  };
}

export const notificationsSupported = process.env.EXPO_OS !== "web";

export async function askPermission(api: NotificationsApi = realApi()): Promise<boolean> {
  const current = await api.getPermissionsAsync();
  if (current.granted) return true;
  const asked = await api.requestPermissionsAsync();
  log.info("notifications.permission", { granted: asked.granted });
  return asked.granted;
}

function parse(t: string): { hour: number; minute: number } {
  const [h, m] = t.split(":").map(Number) as [number, number];
  return { hour: h, minute: m };
}

/** Replaces Gullak's two daily messages. Does nothing without permission. */
export async function scheduleDaily(opts: { morningTime: string; nightTime: string; personality: Personality }, api: NotificationsApi = realApi()): Promise<void> {
  if (!(await api.getPermissionsAsync()).granted) return;
  const existing = await api.getAllScheduledNotificationsAsync();
  for (const n of existing) if (n.identifier.startsWith("gullak.")) await api.cancelScheduledNotificationAsync(n.identifier);
  const copy = notificationCopy[opts.personality];
  await api.scheduleNotificationAsync({ identifier: "gullak.morning", content: copy.morning, trigger: { type: "daily", ...parse(opts.morningTime) } });
  await api.scheduleNotificationAsync({ identifier: "gullak.night", content: copy.night, trigger: { type: "daily", ...parse(opts.nightTime) } });
  log.info("notifications.scheduled", { count: 2 });
}

/** Call once at startup on phones. */
export function configureForegroundDisplay(api?: NotificationsApi): void {
  if (!notificationsSupported) return;
  (api ?? realApi()).setForegroundHandler();
}

const EIGHTY_KEY = "alert80.date";

/**
 * Spec 2.7: when a quick-add crosses 80% of today. Once a day, never in quiet hours (23:00–07:00 IST), only if
 * notifications were already allowed; it never asks for permission. Returns whether it showed.
 */
export async function notifyEightyPercent(
  opts: { personality: Personality; date: string; now: Date },
  api: NotificationsApi = realApi(),
  store: { get(key: string): Promise<string | null>; set(key: string, value: string): Promise<void> } = kv,
): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!notificationsSupported) return false;
  const hour = hourIst(opts.now);
  if (hour >= 23 || hour < 7) return false;
  if ((await store.get(EIGHTY_KEY)) === opts.date) return false;
  if (!(await api.getPermissionsAsync()).granted) return false;
  await api.presentNotificationAsync({ identifier: `gullak.eighty.${opts.date}`, content: eightyPercentCopy[opts.personality] });
  await store.set(EIGHTY_KEY, opts.date);
  log.info("notifications.eighty_percent");
  return true;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The final `useAddSpend`**

`app/src/features/spends/hooks.ts` (whole file):

```ts
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { randomUUID } from "expo-crypto";
import { useCallback } from "react";
import type { Today } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { todayKey } from "@/features/budget/hooks";
import { applySpend, crossesEighty } from "@/features/budget/optimistic";
import { emitGullak } from "@/features/gullak/events";
import { FIRST_SPEND_FLAG } from "@/features/home/flags";
import { useProfile } from "@/features/profile/hooks";
import { istDateOf } from "@/lib/dates";
import { kv } from "@/lib/kv";
import { notifyEightyPercent } from "@/lib/notifications";
import { spendsApi, type ManualPayment, type NewSpend, type TransactionPage } from "./api";
import type { CategoryCode } from "./categories";
import { spendKeys } from "./keys";
import { useOutbox } from "./outbox";

/** What the quick-add screen hands over. Text is raw; toNewSpend trims it. */
export type SpendDraft = {
  amountPaise: number;
  category: CategoryCode;
  paymentMethod: ManualPayment;
  merchant: string;
  note: string;
  spreadOverCycle: boolean;
};

/** The time is taken when the spend is typed, so an offline spend keeps its day however late it is sent. */
export function toNewSpend(draft: SpendDraft, clientId: string, at: Date): NewSpend {
  const merchant = draft.merchant.trim();
  const note = draft.note.trim();
  return {
    clientId,
    kind: "SPEND",
    amountPaise: draft.amountPaise,
    occurredAt: at.toISOString(),
    category: draft.category,
    paymentMethod: draft.paymentMethod,
    ...(merchant ? { merchant } : {}),
    ...(note ? { note } : {}),
    spreadOverCycle: draft.spreadOverCycle,
  };
}

export function useDaySpends(date: string) {
  return useQuery<TransactionPage>({ queryKey: spendKeys.day(date), queryFn: () => spendsApi.listDay(date), staleTime: 30_000 });
}

const systemNow = () => new Date();

/**
 * Saving a spend (spec 2.3, 4.8): on the phone first, then Home's figures move at once, the Gullak's coin drops,
 * and crossing 80% of today shows the local message. The outbox sends it and Home refetches the server's truth.
 */
export function useAddSpend(now: () => Date = systemNow) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const outbox = useOutbox();
  const personality = useProfile().data?.personality ?? "CHILL";
  return useCallback(
    async (draft: SpendDraft): Promise<NewSpend> => {
      const at = now();
      const spend = toNewSpend(draft, randomUUID(), at);
      await outbox.add(spend.clientId, spend);
      const today = qc.getQueryData<Today>(todayKey);
      if (today && !spend.spreadOverCycle && today.date === istDateOf(spend.occurredAt)) {
        await qc.cancelQueries({ queryKey: todayKey });
        qc.setQueryData<Today>(todayKey, applySpend(today, spend.amountPaise));
        if (crossesEighty(today, spend.amountPaise)) {
          void notifyEightyPercent({ personality, date: today.date, now: at });
        }
      }
      void kv.set(FIRST_SPEND_FLAG, "1");
      emitGullak("coin");
      return spend;
    },
    [now, qc, outbox, personality],
  );
}
```

```ts
  overPaise: 0,
  poolOverPaise: 0,
  mood: "HAPPY",
  line: "",
  daysLeft: 9,
  cycleStart: "2026-10-01",
  cycleEnd: "2026-10-31",
};

const coffee: Transaction = {
  id: "t1",
  clientId: "c1",
  kind: "SPEND",
  amountPaise: 15000,
  occurredAt: "2026-10-23T03:42:00Z",
  merchant: "Third Wave Coffee",
  note: null,
  category: "eating_out",
  tag: "WANT",
  paymentMethod: "UPI",
  source: "MANUAL",
  status: "PENDING",
  spreadOverCycle: false,
  linked: false,
  assignedSavingsPaise: null,
  assignedPoolPaise: null,
  xpAwarded: 5,
};

describe("what deleting changes (spec 2.3)", () => {
  it("a spend from today: the mockup's sentence", () => {
    expect(deleteConsequence(coffee, day)).toBe("Today goes back to ₹563 left. The +5 XP for logging it goes too.");
  });

  it("no XP to take back: no XP sentence", () => {
    expect(deleteConsequence({ ...coffee, xpAwarded: 0 }, day)).toBe("Today goes back to ₹563 left.");
  });

  it("an earlier day this cycle, or a spread spend, raises the rest of the cycle", () => {
    expect(deleteConsequence({ ...coffee, occurredAt: "2026-10-10T06:00:00Z", xpAwarded: 0 }, day)).toBe("Your budget for the rest of the cycle goes up a little.");
    expect(deleteConsequence({ ...coffee, spreadOverCycle: true, xpAwarded: 0 }, day)).toBe("Your budget for the rest of the cycle goes up a little.");
  });

  it("a refund going away lowers it; an earlier cycle leaves today alone", () => {
    expect(deleteConsequence({ ...coffee, kind: "REFUND", xpAwarded: 0 }, day)).toBe("Your budget for the rest of the cycle goes down a little.");
    expect(deleteConsequence({ ...coffee, occurredAt: "2026-09-20T06:00:00Z", xpAwarded: 0 }, day)).toBe("Today's budget doesn't change.");
  });

  it("without today's figures it still says something true", () => {
    expect(deleteConsequence({ ...coffee, xpAwarded: 0 }, undefined)).toBe("Your budget updates once it's gone.");
  });
});
```

```tsx
jest.mock("@/features/budget/api", () => ({ budgetApi: { getToday: jest.fn(), setFirstCycle: jest.fn() } }));

import { spendsApi } from "./api";

  kind: "SPEND",

  tag: "WANT",

  assignedSavingsPaise: null,

};

function setup(t: Transaction = coffee, cached = true) {

}

const dayItems = (qc: QueryClient) => qc.getQueryData<TransactionPage>(spendKeys.day("2026-10-23"))?.items ?? [];

beforeEach(() => {
  jest.clearAllMocks();
  notices.clear();
});

describe("spend detail", () => {
  it("shows the spend as the mockup draws it", async () => {
    setup();
    expect(await screen.findByText("Fri, 23 Oct · 9:12 am · logged by you, not yet matched to your bank")).toBeTruthy();

    expect(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "UPI" })).toHaveAccessibilityState({ checked: true });
    expect(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "Want" })).toHaveAccessibilityState({ checked: true });

  it("Save is disabled at ₹0", async () => {

    fireEvent.changeText(await screen.findByLabelText("Amount"), "");
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Enter an amount above ₹0" })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it("an edit shows at once on Home's caches and is sent (API-SP-25)", async () => {
    api.patch.mockResolvedValue({ ...coffee, amountPaise: 18000, tag: "REGRET" });
    const { qc, onDone } = setup();
    // what the server answers once the edit lands, so a refetch agrees with the optimistic state
    api.listDay.mockResolvedValue({ items: [{ ...coffee, amountPaise: 18000, tag: "REGRET" }] });
    (budgetApi.getToday as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue({ ...day, spentPaise: 21000, leftPaise: 38300 });

    const { onDone } = setup();
    fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Save changes" }));
    expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(api.patch).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("a refused edit puts everything back and says so (ERR-14)", async () => {
    api.patch.mockRejectedValue(new ApiError("INTERNAL", 500, "Something went wrong"));
    const { qc } = setup();

    expect(screen.getByText("Delete this ₹150 spend?")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Today goes back to ₹563 left. The +5 XP for logging it goes too.")).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Keep it" }));
    expect(screen.queryByText("Delete this ₹150 spend?")).toBeNull();
    expect(api.remove).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

  it("delete removes the row and restores today's money at once (API-SP-31)", async () => {
    api.remove.mockResolvedValue(undefined);

    api.listDay.mockResolvedValue({ items: [] });
    (budgetApi.getToday as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue({ ...day, spentPaise: 3000, leftPaise: 56300 });
    fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Delete this spend" }));
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete" }));
    expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await waitFor(() => expect(api.remove).toHaveBeenCalledWith("t1"));
    expect(dayItems(qc)).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(qc.getQueryData<Today>(todayKey)).toMatchObject({ spentPaise: 3000, leftPaise: 56300 });
  });

  it("a refused delete brings the row back with a message (ERR-14)", async () => {
    api.remove.mockRejectedValue(new ApiError("INTERNAL", 500, "Something went wrong"));
    const { qc } = setup();
    fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Delete this spend" }));
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(notices.current()).toBe("Couldn't delete Third Wave Coffee. It's back on your list."));
    expect(dayItems(qc).map((t) => t.id)).toEqual(["t1"]);
    expect(qc.getQueryData<Today>(todayKey)?.spentPaise).toBe(18000);
  });

  it("a bank-confirmed amount cannot be edited (API-SP-26)", async () => {
    setup({ ...coffee, status: "CONFIRMED", linked: true });
    expect(await screen.findByText("Fri, 23 Oct · 9:12 am · confirmed by your bank")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Amount")).toHaveProp("editable", false);
    expect(screen.getByText("Your bank confirmed this amount, so it can't be changed")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("a spend that no longer exists says so and offers the way back", async () => {
    api.get.mockRejectedValue(new ApiError("NOT_FOUND", 404, "Spend not found"));
    const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
    const onDone = jest.fn();
    render(
      <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
        <SpendDetailScreen id="gone" onDone={onDone} />
      </QueryClientProvider>,
    );
    expect(await screen.findByText("This spend is no longer here")).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Back to Home" }));
    expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("any other failure shows Retry", async () => {
    api.get.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError("INTERNAL", 500, "x")).mockResolvedValueOnce(coffee);
    const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
    render(
      <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
        <SpendDetailScreen id="t1" onDone={() => undefined} />
      </QueryClientProvider>,
    );
    fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Retry" }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Save changes" })).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```
