`app/src/features/spends/notices.ts`:

```ts
import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";

/** One passive message at a time (APP-28: no action), cleared after 6 seconds. Failed edits and deletes post here; Home shows it. */
type Notice = { id: number; message: string };

const SHOW_MS = 6_000;
let current: Notice | null = null;
let nextId = 1;
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

function emit(): void {
  listeners.forEach((l) => l());
}

export const notices = {
  show(message: string): void {
    const notice = { id: nextId++, message };
    current = notice;
    emit();
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (current?.id === notice.id) {
        current = null;
        emit();
      }
    }, SHOW_MS);
  },
  current(): string | null {
    return current?.message ?? null;
  },
  clear(): void {
    current = null;
    emit();
  },
  subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void {
    listeners.add(listener);
    return () => listeners.delete(listener);
  },
};

export function useNotice(): string | null {
  return useSyncExternalStore(notices.subscribe, notices.current, notices.current);
}
```

`app/src/features/spends/delete-copy.ts`:

```ts
import type { Today } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { leftAfterRemoving } from "@/features/budget/optimistic";
import { istDateOf } from "@/lib/dates";
import { formatRupees } from "@/lib/money";
import type { Transaction } from "./api";

/** Spec 2.3: delete "says what changes". The first sentence is about the budget, the second about XP. */
export function deleteConsequence(t: Transaction, today: Today | undefined): string {
  const day = istDateOf(t.occurredAt);
  let budget: string;
  if (!today || day > today.date) {
    budget = "Your budget updates once it's gone.";
  } else if (day < today.cycleStart) {
    budget = "Today's budget doesn't change.";
  } else if (t.kind !== "SPEND") {
    budget = "Your budget for the rest of the cycle goes down a little.";
  } else if (!t.spreadOverCycle && day === today.date) {
    budget = `Today goes back to ${formatRupees(leftAfterRemoving(today, t.amountPaise))} left.`;
  } else {
    budget = "Your budget for the rest of the cycle goes up a little.";
  }
  return t.xpAwarded > 0 ? `${budget} The +${t.xpAwarded} XP for logging it goes too.` : budget;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Hooks for one spend, edit and delete**

In `app/src/features/spends/hooks.ts`, change the first import to `import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient, type QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";`, extend the `./api` import with `type SpendPatch` and `type Transaction`, add `import { spendTitle } from "./labels";` and `import { notices } from "./notices";`, and append:

```ts
/** A spend from any cached day list, so the detail opens instantly from Home. */
function findCached(qc: QueryClient, id: string): Transaction | undefined {
  for (const [, page] of qc.getQueriesData<TransactionPage>({ queryKey: ["transactions", "day"] })) {
    const hit = page?.items.find((t) => t.id === id);
    if (hit) return hit;
  }
  return undefined;
}

export function useTransaction(id: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useQuery<Transaction>({
    queryKey: spendKeys.one(id),
    queryFn: () => spendsApi.get(id),
    initialData: () => findCached(qc, id),
    initialDataUpdatedAt: 0, // shown at once, then fetched fresh
  });
}

/** The row as it will look after the patch; "" clears merchant or note. */
export function applyPatch(before: Transaction, patch: SpendPatch): Transaction {
  const text = (v: string | undefined, old: string | null) => (v === undefined ? old : v.trim() === "" ? null : v.trim());
  return {
    ...before,
    amountPaise: patch.amountPaise ?? before.amountPaise,
    category: patch.category ?? before.category,
    merchant: text(patch.merchant, before.merchant),
    note: text(patch.note, before.note),
    paymentMethod: patch.paymentMethod ?? before.paymentMethod,
    tag: patch.tag ?? before.tag,
    spreadOverCycle: patch.spreadOverCycle ?? before.spreadOverCycle,
  };
}

type Snapshot = { date: string; day: TransactionPage | undefined; one: Transaction | undefined; today: Today | undefined };

async function takeSnapshot(qc: QueryClient, t: Transaction): Promise<Snapshot> {
  await qc.cancelQueries({ queryKey: spendKeys.all });
  await qc.cancelQueries({ queryKey: todayKey });
  const date = istDateOf(t.occurredAt);
  return {
    date,
    day: qc.getQueryData<TransactionPage>(spendKeys.day(date)),
    one: qc.getQueryData<Transaction>(spendKeys.one(t.id)),
    today: qc.getQueryData<Today>(todayKey),
  };
}

function restore(qc: QueryClient, id: string, s: Snapshot): void {
  if (s.day) qc.setQueryData(spendKeys.day(s.date), s.day);
  if (s.one) qc.setQueryData(spendKeys.one(id), s.one);
  if (s.today) qc.setQueryData(todayKey, s.today);
}

function refreshAll(qc: QueryClient): void {
  void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: spendKeys.all });
  void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: todayKey });
}

/** Spec 4.8: an edit shows at once; if the server says no, the row returns and a message says so (ERR-14). */
export function useUpdateSpend() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationKey: ["spend", "update"],
    mutationFn: ({ before, patch }: { before: Transaction; patch: SpendPatch }) => spendsApi.patch(before.id, patch),
    onMutate: async ({ before, patch }) => {
      const snap = await takeSnapshot(qc, before);
      const after = applyPatch(before, patch);
      qc.setQueryData<TransactionPage>(spendKeys.day(snap.date), (p) => (p ? { ...p, items: p.items.map((t) => (t.id === before.id ? after : t)) } : p));
      qc.setQueryData(spendKeys.one(before.id), after);
      const sameDay = snap.today?.date === snap.date;
      const plain = before.kind === "SPEND" && !before.spreadOverCycle && !after.spreadOverCycle;
      if (snap.today && sameDay && plain && after.amountPaise !== before.amountPaise) {
        qc.setQueryData(todayKey, applySpend(snap.today, after.amountPaise - before.amountPaise));
      }
      return snap;
    },
    onError: (_e, { before }, snap) => {
      if (snap) restore(qc, before.id, snap);
      notices.show(`Couldn't save your changes to ${spendTitle(before.merchant, before.category)}. Nothing changed.`);
    },
    onSettled: () => refreshAll(qc),
  });
}

/** Spec 4.8: a delete shows at once; if the server says no, the row returns with a message (ERR-14). */
export function useDeleteSpend() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationKey: ["spend", "delete"],
    mutationFn: (t: Transaction) => spendsApi.remove(t.id),
    onMutate: async (t) => {
      const snap = await takeSnapshot(qc, t);
      qc.setQueryData<TransactionPage>(spendKeys.day(snap.date), (p) => (p ? { ...p, items: p.items.filter((x) => x.id !== t.id) } : p));
      if (snap.today && snap.today.date === snap.date && t.kind === "SPEND" && !t.spreadOverCycle) {
        qc.setQueryData(todayKey, applySpend(snap.today, -t.amountPaise));
      }
      return snap;
    },
    onError: (_e, t, snap) => {
      if (snap) restore(qc, t.id, snap);
      notices.show(`Couldn't delete ${spendTitle(t.merchant, t.category)}. It's back on your list.`);
    },
    onSettled: () => refreshAll(qc),
  });
}
```

Both mutations keep running after the detail screen closes: TanStack runs `useMutation` callbacks on the mutation itself, so the rollback and the notice happen on Home.

- [ ] **Step 5: The screen and the route**

`app/src/features/spends/screens/spend-detail-screen.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { View } from "react-native";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/empty-state";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/ui/error-state";
import { Field } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Screen } from "@/components/ui/screen";
import { Segmented } from "@/components/ui/segmented";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Toggle } from "@/components/ui/toggle";
import { useToday } from "@/features/budget/hooks";
import { isApiError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { formatDay, formatTime, istDateOf } from "@/lib/dates";
import { formatRupeeDigits, parseRupeesToPaise } from "@/lib/money";
import { colors, radius, spacing } from "@/theme";
import type { ManualPayment, SpendPatch, SpendTag, Transaction } from "../api";
import { isCategoryCode, type CategoryCode } from "../categories";
import { CategoryRow } from "../components/category-row";
import { deleteConsequence } from "../delete-copy";
import { useDeleteSpend, useTransaction, useUpdateSpend } from "../hooks";
import { PAYMENT_OPTIONS, TAG_OPTIONS } from "../labels";

function provenance(t: Transaction): string {
  if (t.source === "STATEMENT") return "from your bank statement";
  if (t.status === "CONFIRMED") return "confirmed by your bank";
  return "logged by you, not yet matched to your bank";
}

function Header({ onBack }: { onBack: () => void }) {
  return (
    <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: spacing.cm, minHeight: 48 }}>
      <Button title="Back to Home" variant="secondary" iconOnly icon={<Icon name="left" size={20} />} onPress={onBack} style={{ width: 48, height: 48, borderRadius: radius.full }} />
      <Text variant="title" accessibilityRole="header">
        Spend
      </Text>
    </View>
  );
}

export function SpendDetailScreen({ id, onDone }: { id: string; onDone: () => void }) {
  const tx = useTransaction(id);
  if (tx.data) return <SpendForm key={tx.data.id} t={tx.data} onDone={onDone} />;
  return (
    <Screen testID="spend-detail">
      <Header onBack={onDone} />
      {tx.isLoading ? (
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.md }}>
          <Skeleton height={56} />
          <Skeleton height={44} />
          <Skeleton height={48} />
        </View>
      ) : isApiError(tx.error, "NOT_FOUND") ? (
        <EmptyState title="This spend is no longer here" action={<Button title="Back to Home" variant="secondary" onPress={onDone} />} />
      ) : (
        <ErrorState onRetry={() => void tx.refetch()} />
      )}
    </Screen>
  );
}

function SpendForm({ t, onDone }: { t: Transaction; onDone: () => void }) {
  const today = useToday();
  const update = useUpdateSpend();
  const remove = useDeleteSpend();
  const [amountText, setAmountText] = useState(String(Math.trunc(t.amountPaise / 100)));
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<CategoryCode>(isCategoryCode(t.category) ? t.category : "other");
  const [merchant, setMerchant] = useState(t.merchant ?? "");
  const [payment, setPayment] = useState<ManualPayment | null>(t.paymentMethod === "UNKNOWN" ? null : t.paymentMethod);
  const [tag, setTag] = useState<SpendTag | null>(t.tag);
  const [spread, setSpread] = useState(t.spreadOverCycle);
  const [confirmDelete, setConfirmDelete] = useState(false);

  const locked = t.status === "CONFIRMED";
  const amountPaise = parseRupeesToPaise(amountText);
  const isSpend = t.kind === "SPEND";

  function save() {
    const patch: SpendPatch = {};
    if (!locked && amountPaise !== t.amountPaise) patch.amountPaise = amountPaise;
    if (category !== t.category) patch.category = category;
    if (merchant.trim() !== (t.merchant ?? "")) patch.merchant = merchant;
    if (payment !== null && payment !== t.paymentMethod) patch.paymentMethod = payment;
    if (isSpend && tag !== null && tag !== t.tag) patch.tag = tag;
    if (isSpend && spread !== t.spreadOverCycle) patch.spreadOverCycle = spread;
    if (Object.keys(patch).length > 0) update.mutate({ before: t, patch });
    onDone();
  }

  function confirmRemove() {
    remove.mutate(t);
    onDone();
  }

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
      <Screen
        testID="spend-detail"
        footer={
          <View style={{ gap: spacing.xs }}>
            <Button title={amountPaise > 0 ? "Save changes" : "Enter an amount above ₹0"} onPress={save} disabled={amountPaise === 0} />
            <Button title="Delete this spend" variant="ghost" textColor={colors.bad} onPress={() => setConfirmDelete(true)} />
          </View>
        }
      >
        <Header onBack={onDone} />
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
          <Field
            label="Amount"
            prefix="₹"
            value={amountText}
            onChangeText={(s) => setAmountText(s.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 7))}
            keyboardType="number-pad"
            editable={!locked}
            hint={locked ? "Your bank confirmed this amount, so it can't be changed" : undefined}
            testID="spend-amount"
          />
          <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: spacing.xs }}>
            <Icon name="calendar" size={14} color={colors.muted} />
            <Text variant="caption">{`${formatDay(istDateOf(t.occurredAt))} · ${formatTime(t.occurredAt)} · ${provenance(t)}`}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
          <Text variant="labelMuted">Category</Text>
          <CategoryRow value={category} onChange={setCategory} />
        </View>
        <Field label="Where" value={merchant} onChangeText={setMerchant} maxLength={80} autoCapitalize="words" />
        <Segmented label="Paid with" options={PAYMENT_OPTIONS} value={payment} onChange={setPayment} />
        {isSpend ? (
          <>
            <Segmented label="How do you feel about it?" options={TAG_OPTIONS} value={tag} onChange={setTag} variant="boxes" />
            <Toggle
              label="Spread over the cycle"
              description="For a big one-off like a flight: it comes out of the whole cycle, not just today"
              value={spread}
              onChange={setSpread}
            />
          </>
        ) : null}
      </Screen>
      {confirmDelete ? (
        <View style={{ position: "absolute", top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0, backgroundColor: colors.scrim, justifyContent: "flex-end", padding: spacing.md }}>
          <View
            accessibilityRole="alert"
            accessibilityViewIsModal
            style={{ gap: spacing.cm, padding: spacing.lg, borderRadius: radius.lg, borderCurve: "continuous", backgroundColor: colors.surface }}
          >
            <Text variant="title">{`Delete this ₹${formatRupeeDigits(t.amountPaise)} spend?`}</Text>
            <Text variant="label">{deleteConsequence(t, today.data)}</Text>
            <Button title="Keep it" variant="secondary" onPress={() => setConfirmDelete(false)} />
            <Button title="Delete" variant="danger" onPress={confirmRemove} />
          </View>
        </View>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}
```

  });
});

```tsx
function renderHome(
  qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false, networkMode: "offlineFirst" } } }),
  store: QueueStore<NewSpend> = memoryStore<NewSpend>(),
) {
  const queue = createQueue<NewSpend, unknown>({ store, send: () => new Promise<never>(() => undefined) });
  render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <OutboxProvider status="signedIn" queue={queue}>
        <HomeScreen now={new Date("2026-10-23T08:00:00Z")} />
      </OutboxProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
  return qc;
}
```

`app/src/features/home/spend-rows.ts`:

```ts
import type { IconName } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import type { NewSpend, Transaction } from "@/features/spends/api";
import { categoryIcon, categoryLabel } from "@/features/spends/categories";
import { paymentLabel, spendTitle } from "@/features/spends/labels";
import { formatTime, istDateOf } from "@/lib/dates";
import { formatRupees } from "@/lib/money";
import type { QueueItem } from "@/lib/queue";

export type SpendRowModel = {
  key: string;
  status: "saved" | "pending" | "failed";
  /** Server id; only saved rows have one and open the spend detail. */
  id: string | null;
  /** Outbox id; failed rows are removed by it. */
  clientId: string | null;
  title: string;
  meta: string;
  amountText: string;
  icon: IconName;
  label: string;
  isSpend: boolean;
  at: string;
};

function savedRow(t: Transaction): SpendRowModel {
  const title = spendTitle(t.merchant, t.category);
  const prefix = t.kind === "REFUND" ? "Refund · " : t.kind === "INCOME" ? "Extra income · " : "";
  const spread = t.spreadOverCycle ? " · Spread over the cycle" : "";
  return {
    key: t.id,
    status: "saved",
    id: t.id,
    clientId: t.clientId,
    title,
    meta: `${prefix}${categoryLabel(t.category)} · ${paymentLabel(t.paymentMethod)} · ${formatTime(t.occurredAt)}${spread}`,
    amountText: t.kind === "SPEND" ? formatRupees(-t.amountPaise) : `+${formatRupees(t.amountPaise)}`,
    icon: categoryIcon(t.category),
    label: `Edit ${title} ${formatRupees(t.amountPaise)}`,
    isSpend: t.kind === "SPEND",
    at: t.occurredAt,
  };
}

function queuedRow(q: QueueItem<NewSpend>): SpendRowModel {
  const p = q.payload;
  const title = spendTitle(p.merchant, p.category);
  const amount = formatRupees(p.amountPaise);
  const failed = q.state === "failed";
  return {
    key: q.id,
    status: failed ? "failed" : "pending",
    id: null,
    clientId: q.id,
    title,
    meta: failed ? `Not saved: ${q.error ?? "Something went wrong"}` : `${categoryLabel(p.category)} · ${paymentLabel(p.paymentMethod)} · Waiting to send`,
    amountText: formatRupees(-p.amountPaise),
    icon: categoryIcon(p.category),
    label: failed ? `${title} ${amount}, not saved` : `${title} ${amount}, waiting to send`,
    isSpend: true,
    at: p.occurredAt,
  };
}

/** Today's rows, newest first: what the server has, plus queued spends it does not have yet. */
export function buildRows(saved: readonly Transaction[], queued: readonly QueueItem<NewSpend>[], date: string): SpendRowModel[] {
  const known = new Set(saved.map((t) => t.clientId).filter((c): c is string => c !== null));
  const rows = [
    ...saved.map(savedRow),
    ...queued.filter((q) => !known.has(q.id) && istDateOf(q.payload.occurredAt) === date).map(queuedRow),
  ];
  return rows.sort((a, b) => Date.parse(b.at) - Date.parse(a.at));
}

export function spendCountLabel(rows: readonly SpendRowModel[]): string {
  const n = rows.filter((r) => r.isSpend && r.status !== "failed").length;
  return `${n} spend${n === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
}
```

`app/src/features/home/spend-row.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { Pressable, View } from "react-native";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { colors, fonts, radius, spacing, touchTarget } from "@/theme";
import type { SpendRowModel } from "./spend-rows";

function Body({ row }: { row: SpendRowModel }) {
  return (
    <>
      <View style={{ width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: radius.md, backgroundColor: colors.accentTint, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" }}>
        <Icon name={row.icon} size={20} color={colors.accentDeep} />
      </View>
      <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, gap: spacing.xs }}>
        <Text variant="body" numberOfLines={1} style={{ fontFamily: fonts.ui500 }}>
          {row.title}
        </Text>
        <Text variant="caption" numberOfLines={1} color={row.status === "failed" ? colors.bad : colors.muted}>
          {row.meta}
        </Text>
      </View>
      <Text variant="bodyStrong" style={{ fontVariant: ["tabular-nums"] }}>
        {row.amountText}
      </Text>
    </>
  );
}

/** One row of Home's Today card (Main.dc.html). Saved rows open the spend; failed rows can be removed; waiting rows only inform. */
export function SpendRow({ row, last, onOpen, onRemove }: { row: SpendRowModel; last: boolean; onOpen: (id: string) => void; onRemove: (clientId: string) => void }) {
  const base = {
    flexDirection: "row" as const,
    alignItems: "center" as const,
    gap: spacing.cm,
    paddingVertical: spacing.cm,
    paddingHorizontal: spacing.md,
    minHeight: touchTarget + 20,
    borderBottomWidth: last ? 0 : 1,
    borderBottomColor: colors.line,
  };
  const id = row.id;
  const clientId = row.clientId;
  if (row.status === "saved" && id) {
    return (
      <Pressable accessibilityRole="link" accessibilityLabel={row.label} onPress={() => onOpen(id)} style={({ pressed }) => [base, { opacity: pressed ? 0.7 : 1 }]}>
        <Body row={row} />
        <Icon name="right" size={16} color={colors.muted} />
      </Pressable>
    );
  }
  if (row.status === "failed" && clientId) {
    return (
      <View style={base}>
        <Body row={row} />
        <Button title={`Remove ${row.title} ${row.amountText.replace("−", "")}`} variant="ghost" iconOnly icon={<Icon name="x" size={18} />} onPress={() => onRemove(clientId)} />
      </View>
    );
  }
  return (
    <View accessible accessibilityLabel={row.label} style={base}>
      <Body row={row} />
    </View>
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/home/today-section.tsx` (whole file):

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { View } from "react-native";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/empty-state";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/ui/error-state";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { colors, radius, spacing } from "@/theme";
import { SpendRow } from "./spend-row";
import { spendCountLabel, type SpendRowModel } from "./spend-rows";

export type TodaySectionProps = {
  rows: SpendRowModel[];
  firstRun: boolean;
  loading: boolean;
  failed: boolean;
  onRetry: () => void;
  onAdd: () => void;
  onOpen: (id: string) => void;
  onRemove: (clientId: string) => void;
};

/** The day's spends are few, so a plain list inside Home's scroll view is enough; no virtualised list. */
export function TodaySection({ rows, firstRun, loading, failed, onRetry, onAdd, onOpen, onRemove }: TodaySectionProps) {
  return (
    <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "baseline" }}>
        <Text variant="bodyStrong" accessibilityRole="header">
          Today
        </Text>
        {rows.length > 0 ? <Text variant="labelMuted">{spendCountLabel(rows)}</Text> : null}
      </View>
      {rows.length > 0 ? (
        <View style={{ backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.lg, borderCurve: "continuous", borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.line }}>
          {rows.map((r, i) => (
            <SpendRow key={r.key} row={r} last={i === rows.length - 1} onOpen={onOpen} onRemove={onRemove} />
          ))}
        </View>
      ) : loading ? (
        <View testID="today-skeleton" style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
          <Skeleton height={64} />
          <Skeleton height={64} />
        </View>
      ) : failed ? (
        <ErrorState title="Couldn't load today's spends" onRetry={onRetry} />
      ) : (
        <EmptyState
          title={firstRun ? "Your spends show up here. Log one as soon as you pay." : "Nothing logged today"}
          action={
            <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
              <Button title={firstRun ? "Add first spend" : "Add a spend"} onPress={onAdd} />
              {firstRun ? (
                <Text variant="caption" align="center">
                  Or upload last month's statement
                </Text>
              ) : null}
            </View>
          }
        />
      )}
    </View>
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/home/home-screen.tsx` (whole file):

```tsx
import { Redirect, useRouter } from "expo-router";
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AppState, View } from "react-native";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/ui/error-state";
import { Screen } from "@/components/ui/screen";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Toast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { useToday } from "@/features/budget/hooks";
import { useDaySpends } from "@/features/spends/hooks";
import { useNotice } from "@/features/spends/notices";
import { useOutbox, useOutboxItems } from "@/features/spends/outbox";
import { describeError, isApiError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { formatDay, greeting, relativeTime, todayIst } from "@/lib/dates";
import { messageFor } from "@/lib/errors";
import { kv } from "@/lib/kv";
import { spacing } from "@/theme";
import { FIRST_SPEND_FLAG } from "./flags";
import { Hero } from "./hero";
import { buildRows } from "./spend-rows";
import { TodaySection } from "./today-section";

export { FIRST_SPEND_FLAG } from "./flags";

export function HomeScreen({ now = new Date() }: { now?: Date }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const today = useToday();
  const date = today.data?.date ?? todayIst(now);
  const day = useDaySpends(date);
  const queued = useOutboxItems();
  const outbox = useOutbox();
  const notice = useNotice();
  const [everLogged, setEverLogged] = useState<boolean | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    void kv.get(FIRST_SPEND_FLAG).then((v) => alive && setEverLogged(v === "1"));
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const refetchToday = today.refetch;
  const refetchDay = day.refetch;
  useEffect(() => {
    const sub = AppState.addEventListener("change", (state) => {
      if (state === "active") {
        void refetchToday();
        void refetchDay();
      }
    });
    return () => sub.remove();
  }, [refetchToday, refetchDay]);

  if (isApiError(today.error, "SETUP_INCOMPLETE")) {
    return (
      <View testID="redirect-onboarding">
        <Redirect href="/onboarding/income" />
      </View>
    );
  }

  const data = today.data;
  const rows = buildRows(day.data?.items ?? [], queued, date);
  const firstRun = !!data && data.spentPaise === 0 && everLogged === false && rows.length === 0;

  return (
    <Screen testID="home">
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center" }}>
        <Text variant="labelMuted">{formatDay(date)}</Text>
        <Text variant="label">{firstRun ? "Day one" : greeting(now)}</Text>
      </View>
      {notice ? <Toast message={notice} tone="bad" /> : null}
      {!data && today.isLoading ? (
        <View testID="home-skeleton" style={{ gap: spacing.md }}>
          <Skeleton height={160} round />
          <Skeleton height={72} width="60%" />
          <Skeleton height={8} />
          <Skeleton height={120} />
        </View>
      ) : !data ? (
        <ErrorState body={describeError(today.error, messageFor)} onRetry={() => void today.refetch()} />
      ) : (
        <>
          {today.fetchStatus === "paused" ? (
            <Toast message={`Updated ${relativeTime(today.dataUpdatedAt, now.getTime())}`} />
          ) : today.error ? (
            <Toast message="Could not refresh. Showing saved data." tone="bad" />
          ) : null}
          <Hero today={data} firstRun={firstRun} />
          <TodaySection
            rows={rows}
            firstRun={firstRun}
            loading={day.isLoading && !day.data}
            failed={!day.data && !!day.error}
            onRetry={() => void day.refetch()}
            onAdd={() => router.push("/add")}
            onOpen={(id) => router.push({ pathname: "/spend/[id]", params: { id } })}
            onRemove={(clientId) => void outbox.remove(clientId)}
          />
        </>
      )}
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

Plan 2's `foregroundRefetchesToday` still passes: the foreground handler refetches today (and now the list).

`app/e2e/offline.yaml` (Android: Maestro switches airplane mode there only):

```yaml
appId: in.gullak.app
---
# E2E-08: airplane mode → quick-add → back online → the spend reaches the server once.
- runFlow: setup.yaml
- setAirplaneMode: enabled
- tapOn: "Add first spend"
- tapOn:
    id: "key-6"
- tapOn:
    id: "key-0"
- tapOn: "Cash"
- tapOn:
    id: "add-spend"
- assertVisible: "Eating out · Cash · Waiting to send"
- assertVisible: "₹60 spent"
- setAirplaneMode: disabled
- extendedWaitUntil:
    visible: "Edit Eating out ₹60"
    timeout: 60000
- assertVisible: "1 spend"
# Restart: the outbox must not send it again, and the server holds one row.
- stopApp
- launchApp
- assertVisible: "Edit Eating out ₹60"
- assertVisible: "1 spend"
```
